use ed25519_dalek::SigningKey;
use rand::rngs::OsRng;

pub struct Identity {
    pub key: SigningKey,
}

impl Identity {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        let mut csprng = OsRng;
        let key = SigningKey::generate(&mut csprng);
        Self { key }
    }
}

impl Default for Identity {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

pub struct MemoryGraph {
    pub state: std::collections::HashMap<String, Vec<u8>>,
}

impl Default for MemoryGraph {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl MemoryGraph {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { state: std::collections::HashMap::new() }
    }

    pub fn atomic_update(&mut self, key: String, value: Vec<u8>) {
        self.state.insert(key, value);
    }

    pub fn get_state(&self, key: &str) -> Option<&Vec<u8>> {
        self.state.get(key)
    }
}

pub const MODEL_FLASH_LITE: &str = "gemini-3.1-flash-lite";
pub const MODEL_31B: &str = "gemma-4-31b-it";

pub struct AgentFactory;

impl AgentFactory {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self
    }

    pub fn get_model_for_turn(turn: usize, error_count: usize) -> &'static str {
        if error_count > 0 {
            return MODEL_31B;
        }
        match turn {
            0..=4 => MODEL_FLASH_LITE,
            _ => MODEL_31B,
        }
    }

    pub fn failover_model(_current_model: &str) -> &'static str {
        MODEL_31B
    }
}

impl Default for AgentFactory {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

FILE: crates/nexus_model_hub/src/universal_provider.rs

use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::env;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ProviderKind {
    Google,
    OpenAiCompatible, // Covers NVIDIA NIM, Ollama, vLLM, Groq, Together
}

impl ProviderKind {
    pub fn detect(model_or_provider: &str) -> Self {
        let env_provider = env::var("NEXUS_PROVIDER").unwrap_or_default().to_lowercase();
        if env_provider == "nvidia" || env_provider == "openai" || env_provider == "ollama" {
            return ProviderKind::OpenAiCompatible;
        }

        if model_or_provider.contains('/')
            || model_or_provider.contains("llama")
            || model_or_provider.contains("deepseek")
            || model_or_provider.contains("nvidia")
            || model_or_provider.contains("mistral")
        {
            ProviderKind::OpenAiCompatible
        } else {
            ProviderKind::Google
        }
    }
}

pub struct UniversalProvider;

impl UniversalProvider {
    pub async fn generate(
        client: &Client,
        api_key: &str,
        model: &str,
        system_prompt: Option<&str>,
        user_prompt: &str,
        temperature: f32,
        max_tokens: u32,
    ) -> Result<String> {
        let kind = ProviderKind::detect(model);

        match kind {
            ProviderKind::Google => {
                let base_url = env::var("NEXUS_API_GATEWAY")
                    .unwrap_or_else(|_| "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com".to_string());
                let url = format!(
                    "{}/v1beta/models/{}:generateContent?key={}",
                    base_url.trim_end_matches('/'),
                    model.replace("models/", ""),
                    api_key.trim()
                );

                let mut contents = Vec::new();
                if let Some(sys) = system_prompt {
                    contents.push(json!({
                        "role": "user",
                        "parts": [{"text": format!("SYSTEM INSTRUCTION: {}", sys)}]
                    }));
                }
                contents.push(json!({
                    "role": "user",
                    "parts": [{"text": user_prompt}]
                }));

                let payload = json!({
                    "contents": contents,
                    "generationConfig": {
                        "temperature": temperature,
                        "maxOutputTokens": max_tokens
                    }
                });

                let res = client.post(&url).json(&payload).send().await?;
                let body: Value = res.json().await.unwrap_or_default();

                if let Some(err) = body.pointer("/error/message").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) {
                    return Err(anyhow!("Google API Error: {}", err));
                }

                let text = body
                    .pointer("/candidates/0/content/parts/0/text")
                    .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
                    .unwrap_or_default();

                Ok(text.replace("```json", "").replace("```", "").trim().to_string())
            }

            ProviderKind::OpenAiCompatible => {
                let base_url = env::var("NVIDIA_API_GATEWAY")
                    .or_else(|_| env::var("NEXUS_OPENAI_GATEWAY"))
                    .unwrap_or_else(|_| "https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1".to_string());

                let url = format!("{}/chat/completions", base_url.trim_end_matches('/'));

                let mut messages = Vec::new();
                if let Some(sys) = system_prompt {
                    messages.push(json!({"role": "system", "content": sys}));
                }
                messages.push(json!({"role": "user", "content": user_prompt}));

                let payload = json!({
                    "model": model,
                    "messages": messages,
                    "temperature": temperature,
                    "max_tokens": max_tokens
                });

                let auth_key = env::var("NVIDIA_API_KEY").unwrap_or_else(|_| api_key.to_string());

                let res = client
                    .post(&url)
                    .header("Authorization", format!("Bearer {}", auth_key.trim()))
                    .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
                    .json(&payload)
                    .send()
                    .await?;

                let body: Value = res.json().await.unwrap_or_default();

                if let Some(err) = body.pointer("/error/message").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) {
                    return Err(anyhow!("OpenAI/NIM API Error: {}", err));
                }

                let text = body
                    .pointer("/choices/0/message/content")
                    .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
                    .unwrap_or_default();

                Ok(text.replace("```json", "").replace("```", "").trim().to_string())
            }
        }
    }
}

FILE: crates/nexus_model_hub/src/neural_agent.rs

use anyhow::{Context, Result};
use futures_util::StreamExt;
use reqwest::Client;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::collections::VecDeque;
use std::sync::Mutex;
use std::time::Instant;
use tokio::sync::broadcast;

const MAX_HISTORY_TURNS: usize = 12;
const SOFT_TOKEN_LIMIT: usize = 110_000;
const HARD_TOKEN_LIMIT: usize = 140_000;
const RESERVED_TOKENS: usize = 8192;

#[derive(Default, Serialize, Deserialize, Clone)]
pub struct ContextPacket {
    pub system_prompt: Option<String>,
    pub model_override: Option<String>,
    pub file_content: Option<String>,
    pub terminal_log: Option<String>,
    pub memory_recall: Option<String>,
    pub system_stats: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum BuilderError {
    #[error("Missing mandatory field: {0}")]
    MissingField(String),
}

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct MicroAgentBuilder {
    system_prompt: Option<String>,
    model: Option<String>,
}

impl MicroAgentBuilder {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { system_prompt: None, model: None }
    }

    pub fn system_prompt(mut self, prompt: &str) -> Self {
        self.system_prompt = Some(prompt.to_string());
        self
    }

    pub fn model(mut self, model: &str) -> Self {
        self.model = Some(model.to_string());
        self
    }

    pub fn build(self, tx: broadcast::Sender<String>) -> Result<MicroAgent, BuilderError> {
        if self.system_prompt.is_none() {
            return Err(BuilderError::MissingField("system_prompt".to_string()));
        }
        if self.model.is_none() {
            return Err(BuilderError::MissingField("model".to_string()));
        }

        Ok(MicroAgent { memory: Mutex::new(VecDeque::with_capacity(MAX_HISTORY_TURNS * 2)), tx })
    }
}

pub struct MicroAgent {
    pub memory: Mutex<VecDeque<Value>>,
    pub tx: broadcast::Sender<String>,
}

impl MicroAgent {
    pub fn builder() -> MicroAgentBuilder {
        MicroAgentBuilder::new()
    }

    pub fn init(tx: broadcast::Sender<String>) -> Result<Self> {
        Ok(MicroAgent { memory: Mutex::new(VecDeque::with_capacity(MAX_HISTORY_TURNS * 2)), tx })
    }

    pub async fn prepare_and_run(
        &self,
        client: &Client,
        api_key: &str,
        user_text: &str,
        context: ContextPacket,
    ) -> Result<(String, u128)> {
        let start = Instant::now();
        let model = context.model_override.as_deref().unwrap_or("gemma-4-31b-it");
        let url = format!(
            "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/{}:streamGenerateContent?alt=sse&key={}",
            model,
            api_key.trim()
        );

        let system_instruction =
            context.system_prompt.unwrap_or_else(|| "You are NEXUS.".to_string());

        let mut enriched = format!(
            "SYSTEM: {}

USER REQUEST:
{}",
            system_instruction, user_text
        );
        if let Some(code) = &context.file_content {
            enriched += &format!(
                "

ACTIVE FILE CONTENT:
```
{}
```",
                code
            );
        }
        if let Some(logs) = &context.terminal_log {
            enriched += &format!(
                "

RECENT TERMINAL LOGS:
```
{}
```",
                logs
            );
        }

        let mut history: Vec<Value> = self.memory.lock().unwrap().iter().cloned().collect();
        trim_history_token_aware(&mut history, &enriched);

        history.push(json!({
            "role": "user",
            "parts": [{"text": enriched}]
        }));

        let payload = json!({
            "contents": history,
            "generationConfig": {
                "maxOutputTokens": 8192,
                "temperature": 0.15
            }
        });

        let response =
            client.post(&url).json(&payload).send().await.context("API request failed")?;
        let mut full_response = String::new();
        let mut byte_buffer = Vec::new();
        let mut stream = response.bytes_stream();

        while let Some(chunk_result) = stream.next().await {
            if let Ok(chunk) = chunk_result {
                byte_buffer.extend_from_slice(&chunk);
                while let Some(pos) = byte_buffer.iter().position(|&b| b == b'\n') {
                    let line_bytes: Vec<u8> = byte_buffer.drain(..=pos).collect();
                    let line = String::from_utf8_lossy(&line_bytes);
                    let clean = line.trim_start_matches("data: ").trim();
                    if clean.is_empty() || clean == "[DONE]" {
                        continue;
                    }

                    if let Ok(val) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(clean) {
                        if let Some(parts) =
                            val.pointer("/candidates/0/content/parts").and_then(|v| v.as_array())
                        {
                            for part in parts {
                                if part.get("thought").and_then(|t| t.as_bool()).unwrap_or(false) {
                                    if let Some(token) = part.get("text").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) {
                                        let _ = self.tx.send(
                                            json!({"type": "NEURAL_THOUGHT", "payload": token})
                                                .to_string(),
                                        );
                                    }
                                    continue;
                                }

                                if let Some(token) = part.get("text").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) {
                                    full_response.push_str(token);
                                    let _ = self.tx.send(
                                        json!({"type": "NEURAL_STREAM", "payload": token})
                                            .to_string(),
                                    );
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        {
            let mut mem = self.memory.lock().unwrap();
            mem.push_back(json!({"role": "user", "parts": [{"text": user_text}]}));
            if !full_response.is_empty() {
                mem.push_back(json!({"role": "model", "parts": [{"text": &full_response}]}));
            }
            while mem.len() > MAX_HISTORY_TURNS * 2 {
                mem.pop_front();
            }
        }

        Ok((full_response, start.elapsed().as_millis()))
    }
}

fn rough_token_count(text: &str) -> usize {
    let chars = text.chars().count();
    let words = text.split_whitespace().count();
    let is_code_heavy = text.contains("```") || text.contains("{") || text.contains("fn ");
    let factor = if is_code_heavy { 3.6 } else { 4.1 };
    ((chars as f64 / factor) + (words as f64 * 0.3)).ceil() as usize
}

fn estimate_message_tokens(msg: &Value) -> usize {
    let empty_vec = Vec::new();
    let parts = msg.pointer("/parts").and_then(|v| v.as_array()).unwrap_or(&empty_vec);
    parts
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|p| {
            p.get("text")
                .and_then(|t| t.as_str())
                .or_else(|| p.get("functionCall").and_then(|fc| fc.as_str()))
                .or_else(|| p.get("functionResponse").and_then(|fr| fr.as_str()))
        })
        .map(rough_token_count)
        .sum::<usize>()
        + 10
}

fn trim_history_token_aware(history: &mut Vec<Value>, current_user_msg: &str) {
    if history.is_empty() {
        return;
    }

    let current_tokens = rough_token_count(current_user_msg) + RESERVED_TOKENS;
    let mut total_tokens: usize = history.iter().map(estimate_message_tokens).sum();
    total_tokens += current_tokens;

    if total_tokens <= SOFT_TOKEN_LIMIT {
        return;
    }

    while total_tokens > SOFT_TOKEN_LIMIT && history.len() >= 4 {
        if let (Some(old_user), Some(old_model)) = (history.first(), history.get(1)) {
            let removed = estimate_message_tokens(old_user) + estimate_message_tokens(old_model);
            total_tokens = total_tokens.saturating_sub(removed);
        }
        history.drain(0..2);
    }

    while total_tokens > HARD_TOKEN_LIMIT && !history.is_empty() {
        let removed = estimate_message_tokens(&history[0]);
        total_tokens = total_tokens.saturating_sub(removed);
        history.remove(0);
    }
}

// ⚡ BLAS LINKER FIX: Force LLD to link the math libraries directly
#[link(name = "openblas")]
#[link(name = "cblas")]
extern "C" {}

// Project Nexus Singularity v3.0.0 — Sovereign Context Index
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::path::Path;
use turbovec::TurboQuantIndex;

pub struct VectorVault {
    index: TurboQuantIndex,
    id_map: HashMap<usize, u64>, // Maps TurboVec Index to SQLite ID!
    current_idx: usize,
    dimension: usize,
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    bit_width: usize,
}

impl VectorVault {
    pub fn new(dimension: usize, bit_width: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            index: TurboQuantIndex::new(dimension, bit_width),
            id_map: HashMap::new(),
            current_idx: 0,
            dimension,
            bit_width,
        }
    }

    pub fn commit_state(&mut self, id: u64, embedding: &[f32]) -> Result<(), String> {
        if embedding.len() != self.dimension {
            return Err(format!(
                "Dimension mismatch: expected {}, got {}",
                self.dimension,
                embedding.len()
            ));
        }
        self.index.add(embedding);
        self.id_map.insert(self.current_idx, id);
        self.current_idx += 1;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn recall_context(
        &self,
        query_embedding: &[f32],
        k: usize,
    ) -> Result<(Vec<f32>, Vec<u64>), String> {
        if query_embedding.len() != self.dimension {
            return Err("Query embedding mismatch".into());
        }
        let results = self.index.search(query_embedding, k);
        let mut resolved_ids = Vec::new();
        for raw_idx in results.indices {
            if let Some(&real_id) = self.id_map.get(&(raw_idx as usize)) {
                resolved_ids.push(real_id);
            }
        }
        Ok((results.scores, resolved_ids))
    }

    pub fn persist_vault<P: AsRef<Path>>(&self, path: P) -> Result<(), String> {
        let path_str = path.as_ref().to_string_lossy().into_owned();
        self.index.write(&path_str).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        Ok(())
    }
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum KernelError {
    VmoCloneFailed { parent: u32, size: usize },
    InvalidArgs,
    RegistryFull,
    AlignmentAnomaly { address: usize },
}

impl std::fmt::Display for KernelError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{:?}", self)
    }
}
impl std::error::Error for KernelError {}

pub mod error;
pub mod safe_kernel;
pub mod safe_vmo;
pub mod temp_vmo;

use dashmap::DashMap;
use once_cell::sync::Lazy;
use std::sync::atomic::AtomicU32;

// ⚡ 100% Safe Pure-Rust Virtual Memory Registry (Replaces Zig)
pub static VMO_REGISTRY: Lazy<DashMap<u32, Vec<u8>>> = Lazy::new(DashMap::new);
pub static NEXT_HANDLE: AtomicU32 = AtomicU32::new(100);

pub fn get_active_vmos() -> u32 {
    VMO_REGISTRY.len() as u32
}

pub fn calculate_entropy(handle: u32) -> f32 {
    if let Some(vmo) = VMO_REGISTRY.get(&handle) {
        let data = vmo.value();
        if data.is_empty() {
            return 0.0;
        }

        let mut counts = [0u32; 256];
        for &b in data {
            counts[b as usize] += 1;
        }

        let mut entropy = 0.0f32;
        let len = data.len() as f32;
        for &c in &counts {
            if c > 0 {
                let p = c as f32 / len;
                entropy -= p * p.log2();
            }
        }
        entropy / 8.0
    } else {
        0.0
    }
}

FILE: crates/nexus_substrate/src/kernel/safe_kernel.rs

use crate::kernel::error::KernelError;
use crate::kernel::{NEXT_HANDLE, VMO_REGISTRY};
use std::sync::atomic::Ordering;

pub struct SafeKernel;

impl SafeKernel {
    pub fn create_vmo(size: usize) -> Result<u32, KernelError> {
        if size == 0 || size > 16_777_216 {
            return Err(KernelError::InvalidArgs);
        }
        let handle = NEXT_HANDLE.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
        VMO_REGISTRY.insert(handle, vec![0u8; size]);
        Ok(handle)
    }

    pub fn close_handle(handle: u32) {
        VMO_REGISTRY.remove(&handle);
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/nexus_substrate/src/kernel/safe_vmo.rs 
=========================================
use crate::kernel::error::KernelError;
use crate::kernel::safe_kernel::SafeKernel;
use crate::kernel::VMO_REGISTRY;

pub struct SafeVmo {
    handle: u32,
    pub size: usize,
}

impl SafeVmo {
    pub fn new(size: usize) -> Result<Self, KernelError> {
        let handle = SafeKernel::create_vmo(size)?;
        Ok(Self { handle, size })
    }

    pub fn write(&self, offset: usize, data: &[u8]) -> Result<(), KernelError> {
        let end = offset.checked_add(data.len()).ok_or(KernelError::InvalidArgs)?;
        if end > self.size {
            return Err(KernelError::InvalidArgs);
        }

        if let Some(mut vmo) = VMO_REGISTRY.get_mut(&self.handle) {
            vmo[offset..end].copy_from_slice(data);
            Ok(())
        } else {
            Err(KernelError::InvalidArgs)
        }
    }

    pub fn read_into(&self, offset: usize, buffer: &mut [u8]) -> Result<(), KernelError> {
        let end = offset.checked_add(buffer.len()).ok_or(KernelError::InvalidArgs)?;
        if end > self.size {
            return Err(KernelError::InvalidArgs);
        }

        if let Some(vmo) = VMO_REGISTRY.get(&self.handle) {
            buffer.copy_from_slice(&vmo[offset..end]);
            Ok(())
        } else {
            Err(KernelError::InvalidArgs)
        }
    }

    pub fn handle(&self) -> u32 {
        self.handle
    }
}

impl Drop for SafeVmo {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        SafeKernel::close_handle(self.handle);
    }
}

use anyhow::Result;
use sqlx::{sqlite::SqlitePoolOptions, SqlitePool};

pub struct SqliteStore {
    pool: SqlitePool,
}

impl SqliteStore {
    pub async fn new(path: &str) -> Result<Self> {
        let pool = SqlitePoolOptions::new()
            .max_connections(5)
            .connect(&format!("sqlite:{}", path))
            .await?;

        sqlx::query("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;").execute(&pool).await?;
        sqlx::query("PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL;").execute(&pool).await?;

        sqlx::query(
            "
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events (
                seq INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                kind TEXT NOT NULL,
                object_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                ts DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            );
        ",
        )
        .execute(&pool)
        .await?;

        sqlx::query(
            "
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS artifacts (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                payload BLOB NOT NULL
            );
        ",
        )
        .execute(&pool)
        .await?;

        Ok(Self { pool })
    }

    pub async fn append_event(&self, kind: &str, object_id: &str) -> Result<()> {
        sqlx::query("INSERT INTO events (kind, object_id) VALUES (?, ?)")
            .bind(kind)
            .bind(object_id)
            .execute(&self.pool)
            .await?;
        Ok(())
    }
}

FILE: crates/nexus_substrate/src/genome_vault.rs

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::Mutex;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct GenomeRecord {
    pub objective: String,
    pub wasm_b64: String,
    pub composite_score: f32,
    pub execution_time_ms: u64,
    pub memory_delta_kb: f32,
    pub thermal_impact: f32,
    pub timestamp: i64,
}

pub struct GenomeVault {
    genomes: Arc<Mutex<HashMap<String, GenomeRecord>>>,
    max_retain: usize,
}

impl GenomeVault {
    pub fn new(max_retain: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            genomes: Arc::new(Mutex::new(HashMap::new())),
            max_retain,
        }
    }

    pub async fn store_high_fitness(&self, objective: &str, record: GenomeRecord) {
        let mut guard = self.genomes.lock().await;
        guard.insert(objective.to_string(), record);

        if guard.len() > self.max_retain {
            let mut sorted: Vec<_> = guard.drain().collect();
            sorted.sort_by(|a, b| b.1.composite_score.partial_cmp(&a.1.composite_score).unwrap());
            sorted.truncate(self.max_retain);
            *guard = sorted.into_iter().collect();
        }
    }

    pub async fn get_best_for_objective(&self, objective: &str) -> Option<GenomeRecord> {
        let guard = self.genomes.lock().await;
        guard.get(objective).cloned()
    }

    pub async fn get_global_best(&self) -> Option<GenomeRecord> {
        let guard = self.genomes.lock().await;
        guard.values()
            .max_by(|a, b| a.composite_score.partial_cmp(&b.composite_score).unwrap())
            .cloned()
    }
}

impl Default for GenomeVault {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new(50)
    }
}

use nexus_search::VectorVault;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use sqlx::{sqlite::SqlitePoolOptions, FromRow, Row, SqlitePool};
use std::path::Path;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::Mutex;

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct AgentMemory {
    pub id: i64,
    pub origin: String,
    pub directive: String,
    pub result: String,
    pub timestamp: String,
}

#[derive(FromRow, Debug, Clone)]
pub struct SutureRecoveryRecord {
    pub vmo_handle_id: i64,
    pub payload_blob: Vec<u8>,
    pub timestamp: i64,
}

pub struct MemoryPalace {
    pub pool: SqlitePool,
    pub vault: Arc<Mutex<VectorVault>>,
}

impl MemoryPalace {
    pub async fn new(db_path: &str) -> Result<Self, sqlx::Error> {
        if !Path::new(db_path).exists() {
            std::fs::File::create(db_path).ok();
        }
        let pool = SqlitePoolOptions::new()
            .max_connections(5)
            .connect(&format!("sqlite:{}", db_path))
            .await?;

        sqlx::query("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;").execute(&pool).await?;
        sqlx::query("PRAGMA cache_size=-16000;").execute(&pool).await?;
        sqlx::query("PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL;").execute(&pool).await?;

        // ⚡ Initialize Vector Vault for O(1) Scalable Semantic Recall
        // Gemini embedding dimension is 768, using 8-bit quantization
        let vault = Arc::new(Mutex::new(VectorVault::new(768, 4)));

        let palace = Self { pool, vault };
        palace.init_full_schema().await?;
        palace.hydrate_vault().await?;

        Ok(palace)
    }

    async fn hydrate_vault(&self) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
        let rows =
            sqlx::query("SELECT id, embedding FROM neural_context WHERE embedding IS NOT NULL")
                .fetch_all(&self.pool)
                .await?;

        let count = rows.len();
        if count > 0 {
            let mut vault_guard = self.vault.lock().await;
            for row in rows {
                let id: i64 = row.get("id");
                let emb_str: Option<String> = row.get("embedding");
                if let Some(s) = emb_str {
                    if let Ok(vec) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<f32>>(&s) {
                        let _ = vault_guard.commit_state(id as u64, &vec);
                    }
                }
            }
            tracing::info!(
                "[MEMORY PALACE] Scalable Vector Vault hydrated with {} strands.",
                count
            );
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn init_full_schema(&self) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
        sqlx::query(
            "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS neural_context (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                origin TEXT NOT NULL,
                directive TEXT NOT NULL,
                result TEXT NOT NULL,
                embedding TEXT,
                timestamp DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            );",
        )
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await?;

        sqlx::query(
            "CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memory_search USING fts5(
                directive, result, content='neural_context', content_rowid='id'
            );",
        )
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await?;

        sqlx::query(
            "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS system_insights (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                summary TEXT NOT NULL,
                timestamp DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            );",
        )
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await?;

        sqlx::query(
            "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cognition_provenance (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                vmo_handle_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
                payload_blob BLOB NOT NULL,
                timestamp INTEGER NOT NULL,
                thermal_at_suture REAL,
                reason TEXT NOT NULL
            );",
        )
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await?;

        // ⚡ V3 EXECUTIVE CORTEX SCHEMA
        sqlx::query(
            "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS plan_runs (
                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                root_directive TEXT NOT NULL,
                graph_json TEXT NOT NULL,
                status TEXT NOT NULL,
                created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
                updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
            );",
        )
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await?;

        // Dynamic schema evolution for legacy databases
        sqlx::query("ALTER TABLE plan_runs ADD COLUMN root_directive TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '';")
            .execute(&self.pool)
            .await
            .ok();
        sqlx::query("ALTER TABLE plan_runs ADD COLUMN graph_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '';")
            .execute(&self.pool)
            .await
            .ok();

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn store_memory(
        &self,
        origin: &str,
        directive: &str,
        result: &str,
        embedding: Option<Vec<f32>>,
    ) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
        let emb_str = embedding.as_ref().map(|v| serde_json::to_string(v).unwrap_or_default());
        let mut tx = self.pool.begin().await?;

        let res = sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO neural_context (origin, directive, result, embedding) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
        )
        .bind(origin)
        .bind(directive)
        .bind(result)
        .bind(emb_str)
        .execute(&mut *tx)
        .await?;
        let row_id = res.last_insert_rowid();

        sqlx::query("INSERT INTO memory_search (rowid, directive, result) VALUES (?, ?, ?)")
            .bind(row_id)
            .bind(directive)
            .bind(result)
            .execute(&mut *tx)
            .await?;

        tx.commit().await?;

        // ⚡ Inject into high-speed vector vault if embedding exists
        if let Some(vec) = embedding {
            let mut vault = self.vault.lock().await;
            let _ = vault.commit_state(row_id as u64, &vec);
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn store_insight(&self, summary: &str) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
        sqlx::query("INSERT INTO system_insights (summary) VALUES (?)")
            .bind(summary)
            .execute(&self.pool)
            .await?;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn fetch_all(&self) -> Result<Vec<AgentMemory>, sqlx::Error> {
        let rows = sqlx::query("SELECT id, origin, directive, result, timestamp FROM neural_context ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 50")
            .fetch_all(&self.pool).await?;
        Ok(rows
            .into_iter()
            .map(|row| AgentMemory {
                id: row.get("id"),
                origin: row.get("origin"),
                directive: row.get("directive"),
                result: row.get("result"),
                timestamp: row.get("timestamp"),
            })
            .collect())
    }

    pub async fn search_memories(&self, query: &str) -> Result<Vec<AgentMemory>, sqlx::Error> {
        let rows = sqlx::query("SELECT n.id, n.origin, n.directive, n.result, n.timestamp FROM neural_context n JOIN memory_search m ON n.id = m.rowid WHERE memory_search MATCH ? ORDER BY rank LIMIT 10")
            .bind(query).fetch_all(&self.pool).await?;
        Ok(rows
            .into_iter()
            .map(|row| AgentMemory {
                id: row.get(0),
                origin: row.get(1),
                directive: row.get(2),
                result: row.get(3),
                timestamp: row.get(4),
            })
            .collect())
    }

    pub async fn semantic_search(
        &self,
        query_vec: &[f32],
    ) -> Result<Vec<AgentMemory>, sqlx::Error> {
        let matched_ids = {
            let vault = self.vault.lock().await;
            match vault.recall_context(query_vec, 3) {
                Ok((_scores, ids)) => ids,
                Err(e) => {
                    tracing::warn!("[MEMORY PALACE] Vector recall failed: {}", e);
                    return Ok(vec![]);
                }
            }
        };

        if matched_ids.is_empty() {
            return Ok(vec![]);
        }

        let mut memories = Vec::new();
        for id in matched_ids {
            let row = sqlx::query(
                "SELECT id, origin, directive, result, timestamp FROM neural_context WHERE id = ?",
            )
            .bind(id as i64)
            .fetch_optional(&self.pool)
            .await?;

            if let Some(r) = row {
                memories.push(AgentMemory {
                    id: r.get("id"),
                    origin: r.get("origin"),
                    directive: r.get("directive"),
                    result: r.get("result"),
                    timestamp: r.get("timestamp"),
                });
            }
        }
        Ok(memories)
    }

    pub async fn purge_all(&self) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
        sqlx::query("DELETE FROM neural_context").execute(&self.pool).await?;
        sqlx::query("DELETE FROM memory_search").execute(&self.pool).await?;
        sqlx::query("DELETE FROM system_insights").execute(&self.pool).await?;

        let mut vault = self.vault.lock().await;
        *vault = VectorVault::new(768, 4); // Re-instantiate to clear RAM

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn fetch_random_strands(&self, limit: i64) -> Result<Vec<AgentMemory>, sqlx::Error> {
        let rows = sqlx::query("SELECT id, origin, directive, result, timestamp FROM neural_context ORDER BY RANDOM() LIMIT ?")
            .bind(limit).fetch_all(&self.pool).await?;
        Ok(rows
            .into_iter()
            .map(|row| AgentMemory {
                id: row.get("id"),
                origin: row.get("origin"),
                directive: row.get("directive"),
                result: row.get("result"),
                timestamp: row.get("timestamp"),
            })
            .collect())
    }

    // ── Provenance/suture methods ──
    pub async fn save_provenance(
        &self,
        handle: i64,
        payload: Vec<u8>,
        ts: u64,
    ) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
        sqlx::query("INSERT INTO cognition_provenance (vmo_handle_id, payload_blob, timestamp, thermal_at_suture, reason) VALUES (?, ?, ?, 78.0, 'EMERGENCY_SUTURE')")
            .bind(handle).bind(payload).bind(ts as i64).execute(&self.pool).await?;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn restore_provenance(&self) -> Result<Option<SutureRecoveryRecord>, sqlx::Error> {
        sqlx::query_as::<_, SutureRecoveryRecord>("SELECT vmo_handle_id, payload_blob, timestamp FROM cognition_provenance WHERE reason = 'EMERGENCY_SUTURE' ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT 1")
            .fetch_optional(&self.pool).await
    }

    pub async fn rotate_provenance_ledger(&self, max_retain: u32) -> Result<u64, sqlx::Error> {
        let result = sqlx::query("DELETE FROM cognition_provenance WHERE reason = 'EMERGENCY_SUTURE' AND timestamp < (SELECT MIN(timestamp) FROM (SELECT timestamp FROM cognition_provenance WHERE reason = 'EMERGENCY_SUTURE' ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT ?))")
            .bind(max_retain as i64).execute(&self.pool).await?;
        let rows = result.rows_affected();
        if rows > 0 {
            tracing::warn!(
                "[MEMORY PALACE] Storage rotation complete. Recycled {} stale eMMC rows.",
                rows
            );
        }
        Ok(rows)
    }

    // ── V3 EXECUTIVE CORTEX DURABLE STATE ──
    pub async fn upsert_plan_run(
        &self,
        id: &str,
        root_directive: &str,
        graph_json: &str,
        status: &str,
        created_at: i64,
    ) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
        let updated_at = std::time::SystemTime::now()
            .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
            .unwrap_or_default()
            .as_secs() as i64;
        sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO plan_runs (id, root_directive, graph_json, status, created_at, updated_at)
             VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
             ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET
                graph_json=excluded.graph_json,
                status=excluded.status,
                updated_at=excluded.updated_at",
        )
        .bind(id)
        .bind(root_directive)
        .bind(graph_json)
        .bind(status)
        .bind(created_at)
        .bind(updated_at)
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await?;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn recover_active_plans(&self) -> Result<Vec<(String, String, String)>, sqlx::Error> {
        let rows = sqlx::query("SELECT id, root_directive, graph_json FROM plan_runs WHERE status IN ('Active', 'Suspended') ORDER BY updated_at ASC")
            .fetch_all(&self.pool).await?;
        Ok(rows
            .into_iter()
            .map(|row| (row.get("id"), row.get("root_directive"), row.get("graph_json")))
            .collect())
    }
}

FILE: crates/nexus_substrate/src/token_ledger.rs

use once_cell::sync::Lazy;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::Mutex;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant, SystemTime};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ModelQuota {
    pub rpm_limit: u32,
    pub tpm_limit: u32,
}

impl ModelQuota {
    fn for_model(model: &str) -> Self {
        match model {
            m if m.contains("flash-lite") => Self { rpm_limit: 10, tpm_limit: 100_000 },
            m if m.contains("flash") => Self { rpm_limit: 5, tpm_limit: 60_000 },
            m if m.contains("31b") => Self { rpm_limit: 3, tpm_limit: 50_000 },
            m if m.contains("pro") => Self { rpm_limit: 2, tpm_limit: 30_000 },
            m if m.contains("research") => Self { rpm_limit: 1, tpm_limit: 10_000 },
            _ => Self { rpm_limit: 3, tpm_limit: 30_000 },
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug)]
struct WindowEntry {
    timestamp: Instant,
    tokens: u32,
}

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
struct ModelWindow {
    entries: Vec<WindowEntry>,
}

impl ModelWindow {
    fn purge_stale(&mut self) {
        let cutoff = Instant::now() - Duration::from_secs(60);
        self.entries.retain(|e| e.timestamp > cutoff);
    }
    fn token_sum(&self) -> u32 {
        self.entries.iter().map(|e| e.tokens).sum()
    }
    fn ms_until_slot_free(&self) -> u64 {
        if self.entries.is_empty() {
            return 0;
        }
        let oldest = self.entries.iter().map(|e| e.timestamp).min().unwrap();
        (Duration::from_secs(60).saturating_sub(oldest.elapsed())).as_millis() as u64 + 100
    }
}

pub struct TokenLedger {
    windows: Mutex<HashMap<String, ModelWindow>>,
}

impl TokenLedger {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { windows: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()) }
    }

    pub fn check_and_reserve(&self, model: &str, estimated_tokens: u32) -> Result<(), u64> {
        let quota = ModelQuota::for_model(model);
        let mut map = self.windows.lock().unwrap();
        let win = map.entry(model.to_string()).or_default();
        win.purge_stale();
        if win.entries.len() as u32 >= quota.rpm_limit {
            return Err(win.ms_until_slot_free());
        }
        if win.token_sum() + estimated_tokens > quota.tpm_limit {
            return Err(win.ms_until_slot_free());
        }
        win.entries.push(WindowEntry { timestamp: Instant::now(), tokens: estimated_tokens });
        Ok(())
    }
}

pub static LEDGER: Lazy<TokenLedger> = Lazy::new(TokenLedger::new);

/// Returns Ok(()) when a quota slot is reserved.
/// Returns Err(message) after MAX_ATTEMPTS — caller MUST NOT fire the API on Err.
pub async fn wait_for_quota(model: &str, estimated_tokens: u32) -> Result<(), String> {
    const MAX_ATTEMPTS: usize = 10;
    for attempt in 0..MAX_ATTEMPTS {
        match LEDGER.check_and_reserve(model, estimated_tokens) {
            Ok(()) => return Ok(()),
            Err(wait_ms) => {
                let jitter = generate_seed() % 500;
                tracing::warn!(
                    "[LEDGER] {} ceiling hit — waiting {}ms (attempt {}/{}).",
                    model,
                    wait_ms + jitter,
                    attempt + 1,
                    MAX_ATTEMPTS
                );
                tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(wait_ms + jitter)).await;
            }
        }
    }
    Err(format!("[LEDGER] Quota for '{}' unavailable after {} attempts.", model, MAX_ATTEMPTS))
}

fn http_rpm_limit() -> u32 {
    std::env::var("NEXUS_HTTP_RPM").ok().and_then(|v| v.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(60)
}

static HTTP_WINDOWS: Lazy<Mutex<HashMap<String, Vec<Instant>>>> =
    Lazy::new(|| Mutex::new(HashMap::new()));

pub fn http_check(ip: &str) -> Result<(), u64> {
    let limit = http_rpm_limit();
    let cutoff = Instant::now() - Duration::from_secs(60);
    let mut map = HTTP_WINDOWS.lock().unwrap();
    let win = map.entry(ip.to_string()).or_default();
    win.retain(|&ts| ts > cutoff);
    if win.len() as u32 >= limit {
        let oldest = *win.first().unwrap();
        return Err(
            (Duration::from_secs(60).saturating_sub(oldest.elapsed())).as_millis() as u64 + 100
        );
    }
    win.push(Instant::now());
    Ok(())
}

fn generate_seed() -> u64 {
    use std::hash::{BuildHasher, Hasher};
    let t = SystemTime::now().duration_since(SystemTime::UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap_or_default().as_nanos()
        as u64;
    let mut h = std::collections::hash_map::RandomState::new().build_hasher();
    h.write_u64(t);
    h.finish()
}

impl Default for TokenLedger {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

use anyhow::Result;
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::json;

pub async fn get_embedding(api_key: &str, text: &str) -> Result<Vec<f32>> {
    // 1. ATTEMPT CUTTING-EDGE LOCAL LITERT MINION (Reflex Embedder)
    // We use a fast timeout (800ms) so it doesn't block if the minion is offline
    let local_client = Client::builder().timeout(std::time::Duration::from_millis(800)).build()?;

    let local_url = "http://127.0.0.1:5001/embed";
    let local_payload = json!({ "text": text });

    if let Ok(res) = local_client.post(local_url).json(&local_payload).send().await {
        if res.status().is_success() {
            let body: serde_json::Value = res.json().await.unwrap_or_default();
            if let Some(values) = body.pointer("/embedding").and_then(|v| v.as_array()) {
                let mut vector = Vec::with_capacity(values.len());
                for v in values {
                    if let Some(f) = v.as_f64() {
                        vector.push(f as f32);
                    }
                }
                // ⚡ MINION SUCCESS: Return local vector immediately!
                return Ok(vector);
            }
        }
    }

    // 2. FALLBACK TO CLOUD IF LOCAL MINION IS OFFLINE
    // Wait for token quota to prevent Rate Limit bans
    let _ = crate::token_ledger::wait_for_quota("gemini-embedding-2", 500).await;

    let external_client = Client::new();
    let url =
        "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-embedding-2:embedContent";

    let payload = json!({
        "model": "models/gemini-embedding-2",
        "content": { "parts": [{ "text": text }] }
    });

    let res = external_client
        .post(url)
        .header("x-goog-api-key", api_key.trim())
        .json(&payload)
        .send()
        .await?;
    let body: serde_json::Value = res.json().await?;

    let mut vector = Vec::new();
    if let Some(values) = body.pointer("/embedding/values").and_then(|v| v.as_array()) {
        for v in values {
            if let Some(f) = v.as_f64() {
                vector.push(f as f32);
            }
        }
    } else {
        return Err(anyhow::anyhow!("API REJECTED EMBEDDING. Raw response: {}", body));
    }

    Ok(vector)
}

pub fn cosine_similarity(a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> f32 {
    if a.len() != b.len() || a.is_empty() {
        return 0.0;
    }
    let dot: f32 = a.iter().zip(b.iter()).map(|(x, y)| x * y).sum();
    let norm_a: f32 = a.iter().map(|x| x * x).sum::<f32>().sqrt();
    let norm_b: f32 = b.iter().map(|x| x * x).sum::<f32>().sqrt();
    if norm_a == 0.0 || norm_b == 0.0 {
        return 0.0;
    }
    dot / (norm_a * norm_b)
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/nexus_substrate/src/vmo_handler.rs 
=========================================
//! Lock-free VMO registry using DashMap.
//!
//! Previously used `RwLock<HashMap<VmoHandle, Arc<SafeVmo>>>` which serialised
//! every write (allocate/release) behind a single global lock. Under concurrent
//! agent workloads this became a hot contention point.
//!
//! DashMap shards the map into N buckets (default: 2× logical CPUs). Each
//! shard has its own RwLock, so concurrent operations on different handles
//! proceed in parallel with no cross-shard blocking.
//!
//! API is fully backward-compatible — callers need zero changes.

use dashmap::DashMap;
use std::sync::{
    atomic::{AtomicU32, Ordering},
    Arc,
};

pub type VmoHandle = u32;

pub struct SafeVmo {
    pub handle: VmoHandle,
    pub data: Vec<u8>,
    pub timestamp: u64,
}

pub struct VmoHandler {
    /// Sharded concurrent map — no global lock on reads or cross-shard writes.
    registry: DashMap<VmoHandle, Arc<SafeVmo>>,
    /// Atomic counter replaces the second RwLock entirely.
    next_handle: AtomicU32,
}

impl VmoHandler {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { registry: DashMap::new(), next_handle: AtomicU32::new(1) }
    }

    pub fn allocate_cognitive_window(&self, raw_data: &[u8]) -> Result<VmoHandle, String> {
        // fetch_add is lock-free — no mutex involved at all for handle allocation.
        let handle = self.next_handle.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
        let vmo = Arc::new(SafeVmo {
            handle,
            data: raw_data.to_vec(),
            timestamp: std::time::SystemTime::now()
                .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
                .unwrap_or_default()
                .as_secs(),
        });
        self.registry.insert(handle, vmo);
        Ok(handle)
    }

    pub fn read_cognitive_window(&self, handle: VmoHandle) -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
        self.registry
            .get(&handle)
            .map(|v| v.data.clone())
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("Invalid handle: {}", handle))
    }

    pub fn export_provenance_checkpoint(
        &self,
        handle: VmoHandle,
    ) -> Result<(VmoHandle, Vec<u8>, u64), String> {
        self.registry
            .get(&handle)
            .map(|v| (v.handle, v.data.clone(), v.timestamp))
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("Handle not found: {}", handle))
    }

    pub fn release_cognitive_window(&self, handle: VmoHandle) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.registry
            .remove(&handle)
            .map(|_| ())
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("Handle not found on release: {}", handle))
    }

    /// Returns the number of live VMO allocations.
    pub fn active_count(&self) -> usize {
        self.registry.len()
    }
}

impl Default for VmoHandler {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

#[derive(Error, Debug)]
pub enum NexusError {
    #[error("Execution fault: {0}")]
    Execution(String),
    #[error("IO fault: {0}")]
    Io(#[from] std::io::Error),
    #[error("Configuration fault: {0}")]
    Config(String),
    #[error("Orchestration fault: {0}")]
    Orchestration(String),
    #[error("Internal system fault: {0}")]
    Internal(String),
    #[error("Sandbox execution fault: {0}")]
    Sandbox(String),
    #[error("Legacy conversion fault: {0}")]
    Legacy(#[from] anyhow::Error),
}

pub type NexusResult<T> = Result<T, NexusError>;

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/agent_runtime/sandbox.rs

use bitflags::bitflags;

bitflags! {
    #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash)]
    pub struct Capabilities: u32 {
        const NONE     = 0;
        const READ     = 1 << 0;
        const WRITE    = 1 << 1;
        const EXEC     = 1 << 2;
        const NETWORK  = 1 << 3;
    }
}

pub struct Sandbox {
    caps: Capabilities,
}

impl Sandbox {
    pub fn new(caps: Capabilities) -> Self {
        Self { caps }
    }
    pub fn can_read(&self) -> bool {
        self.caps.contains(Capabilities::READ)
    }
    pub fn can_write(&self) -> bool {
        self.caps.contains(Capabilities::WRITE)
    }
    pub fn can_exec(&self) -> bool {
        self.caps.contains(Capabilities::EXEC)
    }
    pub fn can_network(&self) -> bool {
        self.caps.contains(Capabilities::NETWORK)
    }
}

//! Auditor Agent — Code quality & security auditor

use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use nexus_substrate::kernel::temp_vmo::TempVmoFs;
use tree_sitter::Parser;

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct AuditorAgent {
    sandbox: TempVmoFs,
}

impl AuditorAgent {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    pub fn audit_file(&self, path: &str) -> Result<String> {
        println!("[AUDITOR] Auditing: {}", path);

        let mut parser = Parser::new();
        let language = tree_sitter_rust::language();
        parser
            .set_language(&language)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("Failed to set Rust language: {}", e))?;

        // Read file via sandbox
        let content = self.sandbox.read_file(path).map_err(|e| anyhow!("Read failed: {}", e))?;

        let content_str = String::from_utf8_lossy(&content);

        Ok(format!("[AUDIT_OK] {} — {} bytes analyzed. Parser ready.", path, content_str.len()))
    }
}

use crate::agents::LogicAgent;
use anyhow::Result;
use async_trait::async_trait;

pub struct ScoutAgent;

impl ScoutAgent {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self
    }
}
impl Default for ScoutAgent {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

#[async_trait]
impl LogicAgent for ScoutAgent {
    fn name(&self) -> String {
        "ScoutAgent".to_string()
    }

    fn can_handle(&self, command: &str) -> bool {
        let c = command.to_lowercase();
        c.starts_with("ls") || c.starts_with("find") || c.starts_with("grep")
    }

    async fn execute(&self, command: &str, _ctx: &SubstrateContext) -> Result<String> {
        let out = crate::podman::PodmanShield::execute(command)?;
        Ok(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).into_owned())
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/agents/memory_actor.rs

use tokio::sync::mpsc;
use tokio::task::JoinHandle;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum MemoryEvent {
    Store(String),
    Recall(String),
    Shutdown,
}

pub struct MemoryActor {
    tx: mpsc::Sender<MemoryEvent>,
}

impl MemoryActor {
    pub fn start() -> (Self, JoinHandle<()>) {
        let (tx, mut rx) = mpsc::channel::<MemoryEvent>(64);

        let handle = tokio::spawn(async move {
            while let Some(event) = rx.recv().await {
                match event {
                    MemoryEvent::Store(data) => {
                        println!("[MEMORY] storing: {}", data);
                    }

                    MemoryEvent::Recall(query) => {
                        println!("[MEMORY] recalling: {}", query);
                    }

                    MemoryEvent::Shutdown => {
                        println!("[MEMORY] shutdown");
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
        });

        (Self { tx }, handle)
    }

    pub async fn send(
        &self,
        event: MemoryEvent,
    ) -> Result<(), mpsc::error::SendError<MemoryEvent>> {
        self.tx.send(event).await
    }
}

use crate::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler;
use crate::memory_cortex::MemoryCortex;
use crate::shared_state::VmoState;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::Mutex;

pub struct SubstrateContext {
    pub memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>,
    pub vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>,
    pub dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
}

use tokio::sync::{mpsc, oneshot};

pub type RuntimeResult<T> = Result<T, Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>>;

pub enum AgentCommand {
    DispatchTask { prompt: String, respond_to: oneshot::Sender<RuntimeResult<()>> },
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct AgenticRuntime {
    cmd_sender: mpsc::Sender<AgentCommand>,
}

impl AgenticRuntime {
    pub fn new(execution_budget_ms: u64) -> Self {
        let (cmd_sender, mut cmd_receiver) = mpsc::channel::<AgentCommand>(32);
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            while let Some(cmd) = cmd_receiver.recv().await {
                match cmd {
                    AgentCommand::DispatchTask { prompt, respond_to } => {
                        let result =
                            Self::process_execution_loop(&prompt, execution_budget_ms).await;
                        let _ = respond_to.send(result);
                    }
                }
            }
        });
        Self { cmd_sender }
    }

    pub async fn dispatch(&self, prompt: &str) -> RuntimeResult<()> {
        let (tx, rx) = oneshot::channel();
        self.cmd_sender
            .send(AgentCommand::DispatchTask { prompt: prompt.to_string(), respond_to: tx })
            .await
            .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to queue task: {}", e))?;
        rx.await.map_err(|e| format!("Runtime channel dropped: {}", e))?
    }

    async fn process_execution_loop(prompt: &str, _budget: u64) -> RuntimeResult<()> {
        let raw_source = "<html><body><div id='editor' contenteditable='true'></div></body></html>";
        let _compressed_context = DomBridge::minify_dom(raw_source)?;
        tracing::info!("[COGNITIVE FSM] Step evaluation processed: {}", prompt);
        Ok(())
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/ast_editor.rs 
=========================================
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use std::fs;
use tree_sitter::{Parser, Query, QueryCursor};

pub struct AstEditor;

impl AstEditor {
    /// update_function: Finds a function by name and replaces its entire body.
    /// This is the "Semantic Precision" upgrade to replace simple string diffs.
    pub fn read_function(path: &str, function_name: &str) -> Result<String> {
        let source_code = fs::read_to_string(path)?;
        let mut parser = Parser::new();
        parser.set_language(&tree_sitter_rust::language())?;

        let tree = parser
            .parse(&source_code, None)
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("AST_PARSE_FAIL: Could not parse {}", path))?;

        let query_str = format!(
            "(function_item name: (identifier) @name (#eq? @name \"{}\")) @func",
            function_name
        );
        let query = Query::new(&tree_sitter_rust::language(), &query_str)?;
        let mut cursor = QueryCursor::new();
        let matches = cursor.matches(&query, tree.root_node(), source_code.as_bytes());
        for m in matches {
            for capture in m.captures {
                if capture.index == 1 {
                    let node = capture.node;
                    let start = node.start_byte();
                    let end = node.end_byte();
                    return Ok(source_code[start..end].to_string());
                }
            }
        }

        Err(anyhow!("FUNCTION_NOT_FOUND: Could not find function '{}' in {}", function_name, path))
    }

    pub fn update_function(path: &str, function_name: &str, new_body: &str) -> Result<String> {
        let source_code = fs::read_to_string(path)?;
        let mut parser = Parser::new();
        parser.set_language(&tree_sitter_rust::language())?;

        let tree = parser
            .parse(&source_code, None)
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("AST_PARSE_FAIL: Could not parse {}", path))?;

        // Query to find the specific function node
        let query_str = format!(
            "(function_item name: (identifier) @name (#eq? @name \"{}\")) @func",
            function_name
        );
        let query = Query::new(&tree_sitter_rust::language(), &query_str)?;
        let mut cursor = QueryCursor::new();
        let matches = cursor.matches(&query, tree.root_node(), source_code.as_bytes());
        for m in matches {
            for capture in m.captures {
                if capture.index == 1 {
                    // The @func capture
                    let node = capture.node;
                    let start = node.start_byte();
                    let end = node.end_byte();

                    let mut new_code = source_code.clone();
                    new_code.replace_range(start..end, new_body);
                    return Ok(new_code);
                }
            }
        }

        Err(anyhow!("FUNCTION_NOT_FOUND: Could not find function '{}' in {}", function_name, path))
    }
}

// cluster.rs — v1.1
// FIX: duration_since().unwrap() → unwrap_or_default() to survive NTP jumps.
use parking_lot::RwLock;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

#[derive(Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, Debug)]
pub struct ClusterNode {
    pub id: String,
    pub host: String,
    pub port: u16,
    pub cpu: f32,
    pub ram: f32,
    pub temp: f32,
    pub last_seen: u64,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct ClusterMesh {
    nodes: Arc<RwLock<Vec<ClusterNode>>>,
}

fn now_secs() -> u64 {
    SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap_or_default().as_secs()
}

impl ClusterMesh {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { nodes: Arc::new(RwLock::new(vec![])) }
    }

    pub fn register(&self, mut node: ClusterNode) {
        node.last_seen = now_secs();
        let mut guard = self.nodes.write();
        if let Some(existing) = guard.iter_mut().find(|n| n.id == node.id) {
            *existing = node;
        } else {
            guard.push(node);
        }
    }

    pub fn get_all_nodes(&self) -> Vec<ClusterNode> {
        self.nodes.read().clone()
    }

    pub fn best_node(&self) -> Option<ClusterNode> {
        let now = now_secs();
        self.nodes.read().iter().filter(|n| now.saturating_sub(n.last_seen) < 40).cloned().min_by(
            |a, b| {
                (a.cpu + a.ram).partial_cmp(&(b.cpu + b.ram)).unwrap_or(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal)
            },
        )
    }
}

impl Default for ClusterMesh {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

use crate::orchestrator_v2::task_graph::TaskGraph;

pub struct CriticAgent;

impl CriticAgent {
    pub fn evaluate(graph: &TaskGraph) -> f32 {
        if graph.nodes.is_empty() { return 0.0; }
        let mut score = 1.0_f32;
        for node in graph.nodes.values() {
            if node.deps.is_empty() && graph.nodes.len() > 1 { score *= 0.9; }
            if node.required_agent.trim().is_empty() { score *= 0.6; }
            if node.priority == 0 { score *= 0.85; }
        }
        score.clamp(0.0, 1.0)
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/diff.rs

/**
 * NEXUS Diff Engine — v2.0 [SURGICAL + FUZZY]
 * Copyright 2026 Donald Ball
 * Handles precise edits even when LLM mangles whitespace or line order.
 */
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};

pub struct DiffEngine;

impl DiffEngine {
    /// Primary entry point: tries exact match first, then fuzzy/indentation-agnostic.
    pub fn apply(original: &str, search: &str, replace: &str) -> Result<String> {
        if search.trim().is_empty() {
            return Ok(original.to_string());
        }

        // ⚡ IDEMPOTENCY GUARD: Prevent the agent from injecting the same code multiple times
        // If the replacement block is substantial and already exists in the file (ignoring indentation), reject it.
        if replace.trim().len() > 15 && Self::apply_agnostic(original, replace, "").is_some() {
            return Err(anyhow!(
                "PATCH_REJECTED_IDEMPOTENT: The replacement text is already fully present in the file. Do not duplicate it."
            ));
        }

        // Tier 1: Exact match (fastest, most precise)
        if let Some(pos) = original.find(search) {
            return Ok(replace_first_occurrence(original, pos, search.len(), replace));
        }

        // Tier 2: Indentation-agnostic matching (handles LLM whitespace issues)
        if let Some(result) = Self::apply_agnostic(original, search, replace) {
            return Ok(result);
        }

        // Tier 3: Fuzzy line-based matching (for larger blocks)
        if let Some(result) = Self::apply_fuzzy_lines(original, search, replace) {
            return Ok(result);
        }

        Err(anyhow!(
            "PATTERN_NOT_FOUND: Could not locate search block.\n\
            Search preview (first 120 chars):\n{}\n\n\
            Hint: Try making the search string more unique or shorter.",
            search.chars().take(120).collect::<String>()
        ))
    }

    fn apply_agnostic(original: &str, search: &str, replace: &str) -> Option<String> {
        let search_lines: Vec<&str> = search.lines().map(|l| l.trim_start()).collect();
        let orig_lines: Vec<&str> = original.lines().collect();
        let search_len = search_lines.len();

        if search_len == 0 || orig_lines.len() < search_len {
            return None;
        }

        for i in 0..=(orig_lines.len() - search_len) {
            let window = &orig_lines[i..i + search_len];
            let window_trimmed: Vec<&str> = window.iter().map(|l| l.trim_start()).collect();

            if window_trimmed == search_lines {
                let mut result = orig_lines[..i].join("\n");
                if !result.is_empty() {
                    result.push('\n');
                }
                result.push_str(replace);
                if i + search_len < orig_lines.len() {
                    result.push('\n');
                    result.push_str(&orig_lines[i + search_len..].join("\n"));
                }
                return Some(result);
            }
        }
        None
    }

    fn apply_fuzzy_lines(original: &str, search: &str, replace: &str) -> Option<String> {
        // Very coarse fallback: if search is long, try to match by first and last line
        let search_lines: Vec<&str> = search.lines().collect();
        if search_lines.len() < 3 {
            return None;
        }

        let first = search_lines[0].trim();
        let last = search_lines.last().unwrap().trim();

        let orig_lines: Vec<&str> = original.lines().collect();

        for i in 0..orig_lines.len() {
            if orig_lines[i].trim() == first {
                for j in (i + 2)..orig_lines.len() {
                    if orig_lines[j].trim() == last {
                        let mut result = orig_lines[..i].join("\n");
                        if !result.is_empty() {
                            result.push('\n');
                        }
                        result.push_str(replace);
                        if j + 1 < orig_lines.len() {
                            result.push('\n');
                            result.push_str(&orig_lines[j + 1..].join("\n"));
                        }
                        return Some(result);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        None
    }

    pub fn unified_diff(_old: &str, _new: &str, path: &str) -> String {
        format!("--- a/{}\n+++ b/{}\n[MODIFICATION APPLIED]", path, path)
    }
}

fn replace_first_occurrence(s: &str, pos: usize, old_len: usize, new: &str) -> String {
    let mut result = String::with_capacity(s.len() - old_len + new.len());
    result.push_str(&s[..pos]);
    result.push_str(new);
    result.push_str(&s[pos + old_len..]);
    result
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_exact_match() {
        let src = "fn old() {}\nfn keep() {}";
        let result = DiffEngine::apply(src, "fn old() {}", "fn new() {}").unwrap();
        assert_eq!(result, "fn new() {}\nfn keep() {}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_agnostic_whitespace() {
        let src = "    fn foo() {\n        println!(\"hi\");\n    }";
        let search = "fn foo() {\n    println!(\"hi\");\n}";
        let replace = "fn bar() {\n    println!(\"hello\");\n}";
        let result = DiffEngine::apply(src, search, replace).unwrap();
        assert!(result.contains("fn bar()"));
    }
}

// src/distributed_scheduler.rs — v2.0 [PRE-EMPTIVE FORGE OFFLOAD]
use crate::cluster::ClusterMesh;
use crate::wasm_runtime::WasmRuntime;
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use base64::{engine::general_purpose, Engine as _};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::sync::Arc;

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct DistributedScheduler {
    pub mesh: Arc<ClusterMesh>,
}

impl DistributedScheduler {
    pub fn new(mesh: Arc<ClusterMesh>) -> Self {
        Self { mesh }
    }

    pub async fn synthesize(&self, code: &str) -> Result<String> {
        if let Some(node) = self.mesh.best_node() {
            if node.cpu < 85.0 && node.temp < 68.0 {
                println!(
                    "\x1b[35m>> [MESH_ROUTING] Offloading WASM Synthesis to {} (Temp: {:.1}°C Load: {:.1}%)\x1b[0m",
                    node.id, node.temp, node.cpu
                );
                return self.remote_synthesize(&node.host, node.port, code).await;
            }
        }

        println!(
            "\x1b[33m>> [LOCAL_EXEC] Substrate utilized for WASM synthesis (Heavy Load)\x1b[0m"
        );
        crate::wasm_forge::WasmForge::synthesize_cell(code).await
    }

    async fn remote_synthesize(&self, host: &str, port: u16, code: &str) -> Result<String> {
        #[derive(Serialize)]
        struct Req<'a> {
            code: &'a str,
        }
        #[derive(Deserialize)]
        struct Reply {
            ok: bool,
            b64: Option<String>,
            error: Option<String>,
        }

        let url = format!("http://{}:{}/api/cluster/forge", host, port);
        let res = reqwest::Client::new()
            .post(&url)
            .header("Authorization", "SOVEREIGN")
            .json(&Req { code })
            .send()
            .await?;

        let reply: Reply = res.json().await?;
        if reply.ok {
            Ok(reply.b64.unwrap_or_default())
        } else {
            Err(anyhow!(reply.error.unwrap_or_else(|| "Remote forge error".into())))
        }
    }

    pub async fn execute(&self, wasm: Vec<u8>, func_name: &str, input: f32) -> Result<f32> {
        if let Some(node) = self.mesh.best_node() {
            if node.cpu < 85.0 && node.temp < 68.0 {
                println!(">> [MESH_ROUTING] Offloading execution of {} to {}", func_name, node.id);
                return self.remote_call(&node.host, node.port, wasm, func_name, input).await;
            }
        }

        println!(">> [LOCAL_EXEC] Substrate utilized for {}", func_name);
        WasmRuntime::run_bytes(&wasm, func_name, input)
    }

    async fn remote_call(
        &self,
        host: &str,
        port: u16,
        wasm: Vec<u8>,
        func_name: &str,
        input: f32,
    ) -> Result<f32> {
        #[derive(Serialize)]
        struct Req<'a> {
            wasm_b64: String,
            func_name: &'a str,
            input: f32,
        }
        #[derive(Deserialize)]
        struct Reply {
            ok: bool,
            result: f32,
            error: Option<String>,
        }

        let url = format!("http://{}:{}/api/cluster/run", host, port);
        let res = reqwest::Client::new()
            .post(&url)
            .header("Authorization", "SOVEREIGN")
            .json(&Req { wasm_b64: general_purpose::STANDARD.encode(&wasm), func_name, input })
            .send()
            .await?;

        let reply: Reply = res.json().await?;
        if reply.ok {
            Ok(reply.result)
        } else {
            Err(anyhow!(reply.error.unwrap_or_else(|| "remote error".into())))
        }
    }
}

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use tokio::sync::broadcast;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum EventKind {
    TaskCreated,
    TaskStarted,
    TaskCompleted,
    AgentCrashed,
    ArtifactCommitted,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct EventRef {
    pub seq: u64,
    pub kind: EventKind,
    pub object_id: String,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct EventBus {
    tx: broadcast::Sender<EventRef>,
}

impl EventBus {
    pub fn new(size: usize) -> Self {
        let (tx, _) = broadcast::channel(size);
        Self { tx }
    }

    pub fn emit(&self, evt: EventRef) {
        let _ = self.tx.send(evt);
    }

    pub fn subscribe(&self) -> broadcast::Receiver<EventRef> {
        self.tx.subscribe()
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/fitness_evaluator.rs 
=========================================
use crate::wasm_runtime::WasmRuntime;
use anyhow::Result;
use std::time::Instant;
use sysinfo::{CpuRefreshKind, MemoryRefreshKind, RefreshKind, System};

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct FitnessReport {
    pub execution_time_ms: u128,
    pub memory_delta_kb: f32,
    pub thermal_impact: f32,
    pub composite_score: f32,
}

pub struct FitnessEvaluator;

impl FitnessEvaluator {
    pub fn evaluate_wasm_cell(
        wasm_bytes: &[u8],
        func_name: &str,
        payload: &[u8],
    ) -> Result<FitnessReport> {
        let mut sys = System::new_with_specifics(
            RefreshKind::new()
                .with_cpu(CpuRefreshKind::everything())
                .with_memory(MemoryRefreshKind::everything()),
        );
        sys.refresh_all();

        // 1. Capture ARM64 Baseline Metrics
        let mem_before = sys.used_memory() as f32 / 1024.0;
        let temp_before = std::fs::read_to_string("/sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone0/temp")
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| "45000".to_string())
            .trim()
            .parse::<f32>()
            .unwrap_or(45000.0)
            / 1000.0;

        // 2. Execute the Generated WASM Payload
        let start = Instant::now();
        let _result = WasmRuntime::run_with_buffer(wasm_bytes, func_name, payload)?;
        let duration = start.elapsed().as_millis();

        // 3. Capture Post-Execution Metrics
        sys.refresh_all();
        let mem_after = sys.used_memory() as f32 / 1024.0;
        let temp_after = std::fs::read_to_string("/sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone0/temp")
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| "45000".to_string())
            .trim()
            .parse::<f32>()
            .unwrap_or(45000.0)
            / 1000.0;

        // Ensure we don't divide by zero
        let memory_delta = (mem_after - mem_before).max(0.1);
        let thermal_delta = (temp_after - temp_before).max(0.1);

        // 4. Calculate Mathematical Fitness Score (Higher is Better)
        // Inverse relations: Lower latency/memory/heat yields a higher score
        // Weights: 50% Speed, 30% Memory Efficiency, 20% Thermal Stability
        let speed_score = 1000.0 / (duration as f32 + 1.0);
        let mem_score = 1000.0 / memory_delta;
        let heat_score = 1000.0 / thermal_delta;

        let composite_score = (speed_score * 0.5) + (mem_score * 0.3) + (heat_score * 0.2);

        Ok(FitnessReport {
            execution_time_ms: duration,
            memory_delta_kb: memory_delta,
            thermal_impact: thermal_delta,
            composite_score,
        })
    }
}

// governance/mod.rs — v2.0 [MERKLE_CHAIN]
// Incremental Merkle root: each action hashes into a running chain.
// Provides a tamper-evident audit trail of every agent action.
pub mod protocol;

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/governance/protocol.rs 
=========================================
// governance/protocol.rs — v2.0 [MERKLE_CHAIN]
use crate::agent_protocol::AgentAction;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::hash_map::DefaultHasher;
use std::hash::{Hash, Hasher};
use std::sync::{Arc, LazyLock};
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
use tokio::sync::Mutex;
use tracing::info;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct MerkleLeaf {
    pub action: AgentAction,
    pub actor_id: String,
    pub timestamp: u64,
    pub hash: u64,
}

impl MerkleLeaf {
    pub fn new(action: AgentAction, actor_id: &str) -> Self {
        let ts = SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap_or_default().as_secs();
        let mut hasher = DefaultHasher::new();
        serde_json::to_vec(&action).unwrap_or_default().hash(&mut hasher);
        actor_id.hash(&mut hasher);
        ts.hash(&mut hasher);
        Self { action, actor_id: actor_id.to_string(), timestamp: ts, hash: hasher.finish() }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, Default)]
pub struct GovernanceLog {
    pub history: Vec<MerkleLeaf>,
    pub root_hash: u64,
}

impl GovernanceLog {
    pub fn init() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    pub fn init_shared() -> Arc<Mutex<Self>> {
        Arc::new(Mutex::new(Self::init()))
    }

    /// record: Chains a new leaf into the Merkle root.
    /// Root = Hash(OldRoot ++ NewLeaf)
    pub fn record(&mut self, action: AgentAction, actor_id: &str) {
        let leaf = MerkleLeaf::new(action, actor_id);

        let mut hasher = DefaultHasher::new();
        self.root_hash.hash(&mut hasher);
        leaf.hash.hash(&mut hasher);
        self.root_hash = hasher.finish();

        self.history.push(leaf);

        if self.history.len().is_multiple_of(10) {
            info!(
                "[34m[GOVERNANCE] Checkpoint: Root=0x{:x} (Size: {})[0m",
                self.root_hash,
                self.history.len()
            );
        }
    }

    pub fn root(&self) -> u64 {
        self.root_hash
    }

    pub fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.history.len()
    }

    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.history.is_empty()
    }
}

// Process-wide persistent governance log. Callers should use this
// instead of constructing their own GovernanceLog, or records get
// discarded along with the throwaway instance.
pub static GOVERNANCE: LazyLock<Mutex<GovernanceLog>> =
    LazyLock::new(|| Mutex::new(GovernanceLog::init()));

use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use reqwest::Client;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Hypothesis {
    pub description: String,
    pub probability_score: f32,
    pub proposed_fix_action: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct GoTResolution {
    pub root_cause_analysis: String,
    pub hypotheses: Vec<Hypothesis>,
    pub selected_path: Hypothesis,
}

pub struct HypothesisEngine;

impl HypothesisEngine {
    pub async fn evaluate_fault(
        client: &Client,
        api_key: &str,
        objective: &str,
        fault_log: &str,
    ) -> Result<GoTResolution> {
        let system_instruction =
            "You are the NEXUS Graph of Thoughts (GoT) Engine.\n\
             A task has failed. You must perform non-linear reasoning:\n\
             1. Analyze the fault.\n\
             2. Generate exactly 3 distinct hypotheses for WHY it failed.\n\
             3. Assign a mathematical probability score (0.0 to 1.0) to each.\n\
             4. Select the highest probability hypothesis as the 'selected_path'.\n\
             Output ONLY valid JSON matching this schema:\n\
             { \"root_cause_analysis\": \"...\", \"hypotheses\": [ { \"description\": \"...\", \"probability_score\": 0.9, \"proposed_fix_action\": \"...\" } ], \"selected_path\": { ... } }";

        // ⚡ FIX 4: Removed responseMimeType here too for Gemma compatibility.
        let payload = serde_json::json!({
            "systemInstruction": { "parts": [{ "text": system_instruction }] },
            "contents": [
                {
                    "role": "user",
                    "parts": [{ "text": format!("OBJECTIVE:\n{}\n\nFAULT TRACE:\n{}", objective, fault_log) }]
                }
            ],
            "generationConfig": {
                "temperature": 0.3
            }
        });

        let base_url = std::env::var("NEXUS_API_GATEWAY")
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com".to_string());

        let url =
            format!("{}/v1beta/models/gemma-4-31b-it:generateContent?key={}", base_url, api_key);

        let res = client.post(&url).json(&payload).send().await?;
        let body: serde_json::Value = res.json().await?;

        if let Some(err) = body.pointer("/error/message") {
            return Err(anyhow!("GoT API Error: {}", err));
        }

        let text = body
            .pointer("/candidates/0/content/parts/0/text")
            .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
            .unwrap_or("{}")
            .replace("```json", "")
            .replace("```", "");

        let resolution: GoTResolution =
            serde_json::from_str(text.trim()).map_err(|e| anyhow!("GoT Schema Fracture: {}", e))?;

        Ok(resolution)
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/lazarus_vault.rs 
=========================================
use anyhow::Result;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::path::Path;
use tokio::fs;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Snapshot {
    pub id: String,
    pub path: String,
    pub hash: String,
    pub directive: String,
    pub timestamp: String,
}

const CATALOG: &str = ".nexus_snapshots.json";

pub async fn list_snapshots() -> Result<Vec<Snapshot>> {
    if !Path::new(CATALOG).exists() {
        return Ok(vec![]);
    }
    let raw = fs::read_to_string(CATALOG).await?;
    let snapshots: Vec<Snapshot> = serde_json::from_str(&raw)?;
    Ok(snapshots)
}

pub async fn take_snapshot(path: &str, directive: &str) -> Result<Snapshot> {
    let content = fs::read(path).await?;
    let hash = fnv_hex(&content);
    let snap_path = format!("{}.{}.snap", path, &hash[..8]);
    fs::write(&snap_path, &content).await?;

    let snap = Snapshot {
        id: uuid_v4(),
        path: path.to_string(),
        hash: hash.clone(),
        directive: directive.to_string(),
        timestamp: epoch_secs().to_string(),
    };

    let mut catalog = list_snapshots().await.unwrap_or_default();
    catalog.push(snap.clone());
    if catalog.len() > 200 {
        catalog.remove(0);
    }
    fs::write(CATALOG, serde_json::to_string_pretty(&catalog)?).await?;
    Ok(snap)
}

fn fnv_hex(data: &[u8]) -> String {
    let mut h: u64 = 14695981039346656037;
    for &b in data {
        h ^= b as u64;
        h = h.wrapping_mul(1099511628211);
    }
    format!("{:016x}", h)
}

fn epoch_secs() -> u64 {
    std::time::SystemTime::now().duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap_or_default().as_secs()
}

fn uuid_v4() -> String {
    format!(
        "{:x}",
        std::time::SystemTime::now().duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap().as_nanos()
    )
}
