use std::collections::HashMap;
use tracing::warn;

pub struct LogicEngine;

impl LogicEngine {
    pub fn linearize_handoff(
        action: &crate::agent_protocol::AgentAction,
        result: crate::agent_protocol::SubagentReturn,
    ) -> String {
        format!(
            "[SYSTEM: DELEGATION REPORT]\nAction: {:?}\nStatus: {}\nOutput: {}\n[DIRECTIVE]: Matriarch, resume execution based on this report.",
            action, result.status, result.output
        )
    }

    pub fn is_action_safe(directive: &str) -> bool {
        let forbidden =
            ["rm -rf /", "mkfs", "shutdown", "chmod 777", "dd if=/dev/zero", "> /dev/sda"];
        let d_lower = directive.to_lowercase();
        !forbidden.iter().any(|&p| d_lower.contains(p))
    }

    pub fn detect_deadlock(observations: &[String]) -> bool {
        if observations.len() < 3 {
            return false;
        }

        let mut read_counts: HashMap<String, usize> = HashMap::new();

        for obs in observations {
            if let Some(after) = obs.strip_prefix("[READ_OK] ") {
                let path = after.split_whitespace().next().unwrap_or("").to_string();
                if !path.is_empty() {
                    *read_counts.entry(path).or_insert(0) += 1;
                }
            }
        }

        for (path, count) in read_counts {
            if count >= 3 {
                let file_progress = observations.iter().any(|obs| {
                    (obs.contains("[WRITE_OK]")
                        || obs.contains("[DIFF_PATCH_OK]")
                        || obs.contains("[PATCH_OK]"))
                        && obs.contains(&path)
                });

                if !file_progress {
                    warn!(
                        "[LOGIC] Localized Deadlock: {} read {} times with zero progress.",
                        path, count
                    );
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }

        let mut last_cmd_sig = String::new();
        let mut cmd_repeat_count = 0;

        for obs in observations {
            if let Some(start) = obs.find("[CMD:") {
                let sig: String = obs[start..]
                    .lines()
                    .filter(|line| !line.contains("Finished") && !line.contains("Blocking waiting"))
                    .collect::<Vec<&str>>()
                    .join("\n");

                if sig == last_cmd_sig && !sig.is_empty() {
                    cmd_repeat_count += 1;
                } else {
                    last_cmd_sig = sig;
                    cmd_repeat_count = 1;
                }
            }
        }

        if cmd_repeat_count >= 2 {
            warn!("[LOGIC] Deadlock: Time-invariant command repetition detected.");
            return true;
        }
        false
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/memory_actor.rs

use std::collections::HashMap;
use tokio::sync::{mpsc, oneshot};

pub enum MemoryCommand {
    Store { key: String, value: Vec<u8> },
    Load { key: String, respond_to: oneshot::Sender<Option<Vec<u8>>> },
}

pub struct MemoryActor {
    rx: mpsc::Receiver<MemoryCommand>,
}

impl MemoryActor {
    pub fn new(rx: mpsc::Receiver<MemoryCommand>) -> Self {
        Self { rx }
    }

    pub async fn run(mut self) {
        let mut db = HashMap::new();
        while let Some(cmd) = self.rx.recv().await {
            match cmd {
                MemoryCommand::Store { key, value } => {
                    db.insert(key, value);
                }
                MemoryCommand::Load { key, respond_to } => {
                    let _ = respond_to.send(db.get(&key).cloned());
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/metrics.rs 
=========================================
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};
use std::sync::LazyLock;

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct NexusMetrics {
    pub tasks_total: AtomicU64,
    pub task_failures: AtomicU64,
    pub arbiter_blocks: AtomicU64,
    pub heal_successes: AtomicU64,
    pub heal_failures: AtomicU64,
    pub api_latency_ms_sum: AtomicU64,
    pub api_calls: AtomicU64,
    pub circuit_opens: AtomicU64,
}

impl Default for NexusMetrics {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl NexusMetrics {
    pub const fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            tasks_total: AtomicU64::new(0),
            task_failures: AtomicU64::new(0),
            arbiter_blocks: AtomicU64::new(0),
            heal_successes: AtomicU64::new(0),
            heal_failures: AtomicU64::new(0),
            api_latency_ms_sum: AtomicU64::new(0),
            api_calls: AtomicU64::new(0),
            circuit_opens: AtomicU64::new(0),
        }
    }

    pub fn inc_task(&self) {
        tracing::info!("Task counter incremented");
        self.tasks_total.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    pub fn inc_failure(&self) {
        self.task_failures.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    pub fn inc_arbiter_block(&self) {
        self.arbiter_blocks.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    pub fn inc_heal_success(&self) {
        self.heal_successes.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    pub fn inc_heal_failure(&self) {
        self.heal_failures.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    pub fn inc_circuit_open(&self) {
        self.circuit_opens.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    pub fn record_api_call(&self, latency_ms: u128) {
        self.api_calls.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
        self.api_latency_ms_sum.fetch_add(latency_ms as u64, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }

    pub fn avg_latency_ms(&self) -> u64 {
        let calls = self.api_calls.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
        self.api_latency_ms_sum.load(Ordering::Relaxed).checked_div(calls).unwrap_or(0)
    }
}

pub static METRICS: LazyLock<NexusMetrics> = LazyLock::new(NexusMetrics::new);

pub fn get_metrics_text() -> String {
    let m = &*METRICS;
    format!(
        "# HELP nexus_tasks_total Total tasks processed by the NEXUS Cortex\n\
         # TYPE nexus_tasks_total counter\n\
         nexus_tasks_total {}\n\
         # HELP nexus_task_failures Total task execution failures\n\
         # TYPE nexus_task_failures counter\n\
         nexus_task_failures {}\n\
         # HELP nexus_arbiter_blocks Total arbiter safety interventions\n\
         # TYPE nexus_arbiter_blocks counter\n\
         nexus_arbiter_blocks {}\n\
         # HELP nexus_heal_successes Self-healing suture successes\n\
         # TYPE nexus_heal_successes counter\n\
         nexus_heal_successes {}\n\
         # HELP nexus_heal_failures Self-healing suture failures\n\
         # TYPE nexus_heal_failures counter\n\
         nexus_heal_failures {}\n\
         # HELP nexus_api_calls Total calls to Gemini API backend\n\
         # TYPE nexus_api_calls counter\n\
         nexus_api_calls {}\n\
         # HELP nexus_api_avg_latency_ms Average API latency in milliseconds\n\
         # TYPE nexus_api_avg_latency_ms gauge\n\

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/orchestrator.rs

use reqwest::Client;
use std::sync::atomic::Ordering;
use tokio::sync::broadcast;

use crate::metrics::METRICS;
use crate::router::IntelligentRouter;
use nexus_model_hub::neural_agent::{ContextPacket, MicroAgent};

pub struct Orchestrator;

impl Default for Orchestrator {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl Orchestrator {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {}
    }

    pub async fn run(
        &self,
        prompt: String,
        ctx: ContextPacket,
        client: &Client,
        api_key: &str,
        tx: Option<broadcast::Sender<String>>,
    ) -> Result<String, String> {
        METRICS.api_calls.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);

        let mut active_ctx = ctx;

        // Only classify and assign defaults if properties are not already defined
        if active_ctx.system_prompt.is_none() || active_ctx.model_override.is_none() {
            let profile = IntelligentRouter::route(&prompt);

            if active_ctx.system_prompt.is_none() {
                active_ctx.system_prompt = Some(profile.persona.clone());
            }
            if active_ctx.model_override.is_none() {
                active_ctx.model_override = Some(profile.primary_model.to_string());
            }
        }

        // 3. Create a scoped agent for the execution turn
        let sender = tx.unwrap_or_else(|| {
            let (dummy_tx, _) = broadcast::channel(1);
            dummy_tx
        });

        let agent = MicroAgent::init(sender).map_err(|e| format!("AGENT_INIT_FAULT: {}", e))?;

        // 4. Engage the API and pipe the SSE stream
        match agent.prepare_and_run(client, api_key, &prompt, active_ctx).await {
            Ok((execution_delta, _latency)) => Ok(execution_delta),
            Err(e) => Err(format!("NEURAL_UPLINK_FAILURE: {}", e)),
        }
    }
}

use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::{json, Value};

pub struct GraphOfThoughts;

impl GraphOfThoughts {
    pub async fn explore_strategies(client: &Client, api_key: &str, directive: &str) -> String {
        let prompt = format!(
            "You are the GraphOfThoughts Engine. Explore 3 distinct strategies for the following directive.\n\
             Evaluate the pros and cons of each, then select the optimal path and execute it conceptually.\n\
             DIRECTIVE: {}\n\n\
             CRITICAL: After detailing Phase 3, you MUST output a JSON array of AgentAction commands to physically execute your selected strategy.\n\
             MANDATORY SCHEMA:\n\
             [\n\
               {{ \"type\": \"execute_command\", \"command\": \"...\", \"reasoning\": \"...\" }},\n\
               {{ \"type\": \"read_file\", \"path\": \"...\", \"reasoning\": \"...\" }},\n\
               {{ \"type\": \"write_file\", \"path\": \"...\", \"content\": \"...\", \"reasoning\": \"...\" }},\n\
               {{ \"type\": \"diff_patch\", \"path\": \"...\", \"search\": \"...\", \"replace\": \"...\", \"reasoning\": \"...\" }},\n\
               {{ \"type\": \"patch_function\", \"path\": \"...\", \"function_name\": \"...\", \"new_body\": \"...\", \"reasoning\": \"...\" }},\n\
               {{ \"type\": \"halt\", \"reasoning\": \"...\" }}\n\
             ]\n\
             Output the JSON array at the very end of your response.", directive
        );

        let payload = json!({
            "contents": [{ "role": "user", "parts": [{ "text": prompt }] }],
            "generationConfig": { "temperature": 0.1, "maxOutputTokens": 1024 }
        });

        // ⚡ MODEL CASCADE: Try Heavy Reasoning first, fallback to Fast/Stable
        let models = ["gemma-4-31b-it", "gemini-3.1-flash-lite"];

        for model in models {
            let url = format!(
                "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/{}:generateContent?key={}",
                model, api_key
            );

            for _attempt in 1..=2 {
                match client.post(&url).json(&payload).send().await {
                    Ok(res) => {
                        if let Ok(body) = res.json::<Value>().await {
                            if let Some(err) =
                                body.pointer("/error/message").and_then(|v| v.as_str())
                            {
                                println!(
                                    "\x1b[33m>> [GOT ENGINE] {} fault: {}. Falling back...\x1b[0m",
                                    model, err
                                );
                                break;
                            }

                            let mut final_text = String::new();
                            if let Some(parts) = body
                                .pointer("/candidates/0/content/parts")
                                .and_then(|v| v.as_array())
                            {
                                for part in parts {
                                    if let Some(text) = part.get("text").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) {
                                        if part
                                            .get("thought")
                                            .and_then(|t| t.as_bool())
                                            .unwrap_or(false)
                                        {
                                            final_text.push_str(&format!("\x1b[90m>>> [INTERNAL REASONING]\n{}\n<<<\x1b[0m\n", text.trim()));
                                        } else {
                                            final_text.push_str(text);
                                            final_text.push('\n');
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            if !final_text.is_empty() {
                                return final_text;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    Err(e) => {
                        println!(
                            "\x1b[31m>> [GOT ENGINE] Network fault on {}: {}\x1b[0m",
                            model, e
                        );
                        tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(2)).await;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        "GraphOfThoughts execution failed. All models in the cascade returned errors or timed out."
            .to_string()
    }
}

use crate::orchestrator_v2::task_graph::{TaskGraph, TaskStatus};
use crate::orchestrator_v2::verifier::VerificationResult;

pub struct Scheduler;

impl Scheduler {
    const MAX_RETRIES: u8 = 2;

    /// Transitions viable tasks from Pending/Retrying to Ready.
    /// Returns the IDs of tasks that are newly marked as Ready.
    pub fn prepare_ready_tasks(graph: &mut TaskGraph) -> Vec<String> {
        let mut newly_ready = Vec::new();

        // We must clone the keys to avoid mutably borrowing the graph while iterating
        let node_ids: Vec<String> = graph.nodes.keys().cloned().collect();

        for id in node_ids {
            let mut is_ready = false;

            // Check dependency status immutably
            if let Some(node) = graph.nodes.get(&id) {
                if node.status == TaskStatus::Pending || node.status == TaskStatus::Retrying {
                    let all_deps_met = node.deps.iter().all(|dep_id| {
                        graph
                            .nodes
                            .get(dep_id)
                            .map(|n| n.status == TaskStatus::Completed)
                            .unwrap_or(false)
                    });
                    if all_deps_met {
                        is_ready = true;
                    }
                }
            }

            // Apply state transition
            if is_ready {
                if let Some(node) = graph.nodes.get_mut(&id) {
                    node.status = TaskStatus::Ready;
                    newly_ready.push(id.clone());
                }
            }
        }
        newly_ready
    }

    pub fn mark_running(graph: &mut TaskGraph, task_id: &str) {
        if let Some(node) = graph.nodes.get_mut(task_id) {
            if node.status == TaskStatus::Ready {
                node.status = TaskStatus::Running;
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn process_verification(graph: &mut TaskGraph, task_id: &str, result: VerificationResult) {
        if let Some(node) = graph.nodes.get_mut(task_id) {
            match result {
                VerificationResult::Success => {
                    node.status = TaskStatus::Completed;
                }
                VerificationResult::Retry(reason) => {
                    node.execution_state.attempts += 1;
                    node.execution_state.last_error = Some(reason.clone());

                    if node.execution_state.attempts >= Self::MAX_RETRIES {
                        node.status = TaskStatus::Escalated; // Budget exhausted
                    } else {
                        node.status = TaskStatus::Retrying;
                    }
                }
                VerificationResult::Escalate(reason) => {
                    node.execution_state.last_error = Some(reason);
                    node.status = TaskStatus::Escalated;
                }
                VerificationResult::Failure(reason) => {
                    node.execution_state.last_error = Some(reason);
                    node.status = TaskStatus::Failed;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/orchestrator_v2/task_graph.rs 
=========================================
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
#[allow(unused_imports)]
use std::collections::HashSet;
use std::collections::{HashMap, VecDeque};

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum TaskStatus {
    Pending,
    Running,
    Success,
    Failed,
    Completed,
    Ready,
    Escalated,
    Retrying,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, Default)]
pub struct TaskExecutionState {
    pub attempts: u8,
    pub last_error: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct TaskNode {
    pub id: String,

    #[serde(alias = "dependencies")]
    pub deps: Vec<String>,

    #[serde(alias = "assigned_agent")]
    pub required_agent: String,

    #[serde(default = "default_status")]
    pub status: TaskStatus,

    #[serde(default)]
    pub objective: String,

    #[serde(default)]
    pub success_criteria: Vec<String>,

    #[serde(default)]
    pub execution_state: TaskExecutionState,

    // HEFT Optimization field
    #[serde(default)]
    pub priority: usize,
}

fn default_status() -> TaskStatus {
    TaskStatus::Pending
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct TaskGraph {
    pub nodes: HashMap<String, TaskNode>,
}

impl TaskGraph {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { nodes: HashMap::new() }
    }

    pub fn add_task(&mut self, id: &str, deps: Vec<String>, required_agent: &str) {
        self.nodes.insert(
            id.to_string(),
            TaskNode {
                id: id.to_string(),
                deps,
                required_agent: required_agent.to_string(),
                status: TaskStatus::Pending,
                objective: String::new(),
                success_criteria: Vec::new(),
                execution_state: TaskExecutionState::default(),
                priority: 0,
            },
        );
    }

    pub fn compute_critical_path_priorities(&mut self) {
        let mut memo: HashMap<String, usize> = HashMap::new();

        fn b_level(node_id: &str, graph: &TaskGraph, memo: &mut HashMap<String, usize>) -> usize {
            if let Some(&cached) = memo.get(node_id) {
                return cached;
            }
            let mut max_child = 0;

            for (child_id, child) in &graph.nodes {
                if child.deps.contains(&node_id.to_string()) {
                    max_child = max_child.max(b_level(child_id, graph, memo));
                }
            }
            let level = max_child + 1;
            memo.insert(node_id.to_string(), level);
            level
        }

        let keys: Vec<String> = self.nodes.keys().cloned().collect();
        for id in keys {
            let prio = b_level(&id, self, &mut memo);
            if let Some(node) = self.nodes.get_mut(&id) {
                node.priority = prio;
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn resolve_execution_order(&self) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
        for node in self.nodes.values() {
            for dep in &node.deps {
                if !self.nodes.contains_key(dep.as_str()) {
                    return Err(anyhow!(
                        "DAG_ERROR: Task '{}' depends on unknown task '{}'.",
                        node.id,
                        dep
                    ));
                }
            }
        }

        let mut in_degree: HashMap<&str, usize> =
            self.nodes.keys().map(|k| (k.as_str(), 0)).collect();
        let mut dependents: HashMap<&str, Vec<&str>> = HashMap::new();

        for node in self.nodes.values() {
            for dep in &node.deps {
                dependents.entry(dep.as_str()).or_default().push(node.id.as_str());
                *in_degree.entry(node.id.as_str()).or_insert(0) += 1;
            }
        }

        let mut queue: VecDeque<&str> =
            in_degree.iter().filter(|(_, &d)| d == 0).map(|(&id, _)| id).collect();
        let mut order = Vec::with_capacity(self.nodes.len());

        while let Some(id) = queue.pop_front() {
            order.push(id.to_string());
            if let Some(kids) = dependents.get(id) {
                for &child in kids {
                    let deg = in_degree.get_mut(child).unwrap();
                    *deg -= 1;
                    if *deg == 0 {
                        queue.push_back(child);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if order.len() != self.nodes.len() {
            let resolved: HashSet<_> = order.iter().map(|s| s.as_str()).collect();
            let cycle_nodes: Vec<_> =
                self.nodes.keys().filter(|k| !resolved.contains(k.as_str())).cloned().collect();
            return Err(anyhow!(
                "DAG_DEADLOCK: Circular dependency detected among: {:?}",
                cycle_nodes
            ));
        }
        Ok(order)
    }
}

impl Default for TaskGraph {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/orchestrator_v2/verifier.rs 
=========================================
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum VerificationResult {
    Success,
    Retry(String),
    Failure(String),
    Escalate(String),
}

pub struct Verifier;

impl Verifier {
    /// LocalDiagnosis Layer: Cheap, deterministic heuristic matching.
    pub fn diagnose_output(stderr: &str, stdout: &str) -> VerificationResult {
        let combined = format!("{} {}", stderr, stdout).to_lowercase();

        // 1. Transient Faults -> Yields a localized Retry
        if combined.contains("timeout")
            || combined.contains("connection reset")
            || combined.contains("rate limit")
            || combined.contains("503 service unavailable")
            || combined.contains("429 too many requests")
        {
            return VerificationResult::Retry("Transient network/API fault detected.".into());
        }

        // 2. Deterministic Faults -> Immediate Escalation
        if combined.contains("error:")
            || combined.contains("cannot find crate")
            || combined.contains("borrowed value")
            || combined.contains("syntax error")
            || combined.contains("test failed")
            || combined.contains("panicked at")
        {
            return VerificationResult::Escalate(
                "Deterministic compilation/logic fault detected.".into(),
            );
        }

        // 3. Ambiguous Anomaly -> Escalate
        if !stderr.trim().is_empty() {
            return VerificationResult::Escalate("Unknown stderr anomaly detected.".into());
        }

        VerificationResult::Success
    }

    pub async fn evaluate(stdout: &str, stderr: &str, criteria: &[String]) -> VerificationResult {
        let diagnosis = Self::diagnose_output(stderr, stdout);
        if diagnosis != VerificationResult::Success {
            return diagnosis;
        }

        if criteria.is_empty() {
            return VerificationResult::Success;
        }

        // TODO: Phase 2 Low-temp Gemma API semantic verification.
        VerificationResult::Success
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/orchestrator_v2/planner.rs

use crate::orchestrator_v2::task_graph::{TaskGraph, TaskNode, TaskStatus};
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::Value;

pub struct PlannerAgent;

impl PlannerAgent {
    pub async fn generate_plan(
        client: &Client,
        api_key: &str,
        directive: &str,
    ) -> Result<TaskGraph> {
        let system_instruction =
            "You are a headless JSON API. You MUST output ONLY a valid, raw JSON array. \
            Do NOT use markdown formatting like ```json. Do NOT write any conversational text. \
            Do NOT explain your steps. Your output must start exactly with `[` and end with `]`. \
            CRITICAL RULE 1: If the directive involves modifying Rust code, your DAG MUST include a final task to verify integrity by running `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -j 1 -- -D warnings`. \
            CRITICAL RULE 2: You MUST explicitly chain your tasks using the 'deps' array so they execute in a strict, linear sequence. Task 2 must depend on Task 1, Task 3 must depend on Task 2, etc. DO NOT leave the 'deps' array empty unless it is the very first task. \
            CRITICAL RULE 3: The ONLY valid fields for each object are 'id', 'objective', 'deps', 'required_agent', and 'success_criteria'. \
            DO NOT output any other fields like 'params', 'required_files', or 'Architectural_Strategies'. Embed all details into the 'objective' string. \
            ANTI-ANCHORING RULE: The example below exists solely to show you the required JSON structure. Your plan's tasks must be derived entirely from the REAL DIRECTIVE.";

        let payload = serde_json::json!({
            "systemInstruction": {
                "parts": [{ "text": system_instruction }]
            },
            "contents": [
                {
                    "role": "user",
                    "parts": [{ "text": "FORMAT EXAMPLE ONLY:\nDIRECTIVE: Add a one-line comment to README.md." }]
                },
                {
                    "role": "model",
                    "parts": [{ "text": "[\n  {\n    \"id\": \"task_1\",\n    \"objective\": \"Add comment to README.md\",\n    \"deps\": [],\n    \"required_agent\": \"GeneralAgent\",\n    \"success_criteria\": [\"Edit completes\"]\n  }\n]" }]
                },
                {
                    "role": "user",
                    "parts": [{ "text": format!("REAL DIRECTIVE: {}", directive) }]
                },
                {
                    "role": "model",
                    "parts": [{ "text": "[" }] // ⚡ FORCE PRE-FILL
                }
            ],
            "generationConfig": {
                "temperature": 0.1,
                "topK": 40,
                "topP": 0.95,
                "maxOutputTokens": 8192
            }
        });

        let base_url = std::env::var("NEXUS_API_GATEWAY")
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com".to_string());

        let url = format!(
            "{}/v1beta/models/gemini-3.1-flash-lite:generateContent?key={}",
            base_url, api_key
        );

        let mut attempts = 0;
        let mut last_error = String::new();

        while attempts < 6 {
            attempts += 1;
            match client.post(&url).json(&payload).send().await {
                Ok(res) => {
                    let status = res.status();
                    let body: Value = res.json().await.unwrap_or_default();

                    if status == 429 || status.as_u16() == 503 {
                        let wait = attempts * 5;
                        tracing::warn!("[PLANNER] API Throttled ({}). Retrying in {}s...", status, wait);
                        tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(wait)).await;
                        continue;
                    }

                    if let Some(err) = body.pointer("/error/message").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
                        if err.contains("Internal error") || err.contains("high demand") || err.contains("quota") || err.contains("rate limit") {
                            tracing::warn!("[PLANNER] Transient fault: {}. Retrying...", err);
                            tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(attempts * 5)).await;
                            continue;
                        }
                        return Err(anyhow!("API Error: {}", err));
                    }

                    let mut text = body.pointer("/candidates/0/content/parts/0/text").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).unwrap_or("").to_string();

                    if text.trim().is_empty() {
                        tracing::warn!("[PLANNER] Empty response received. Retrying...");
                        tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(2)).await;
                        continue;
                    }

                    if !text.starts_with('[') {
                        text = format!("[{}", text);
                    }

                    let array_str = match Self::extract_json_array(&text) {
                        Some(s) => s,
                        None => {
                            tracing::warn!("[PLANNER] JSON format fracture. Retrying...");
                            tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(2)).await;
                            continue;
                        }
                    };

                    match serde_json::from_str::<Vec<TaskNode>>(&array_str) {
                        Ok(nodes) => {
                            if nodes.is_empty() { continue; }
                            let mut graph = TaskGraph::new();
                            for mut node in nodes {
                                node.status = TaskStatus::Pending;
                                graph.nodes.insert(node.id.clone(), node);
                            }
                            return Ok(graph);
                        }
                        Err(e) => {
                            tracing::warn!("[PLANNER] DAG Parse Fracture: {}. Retrying...", e);
                            tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(2)).await;
                            continue;
                        }
                    }
                }
                Err(e) => {
                    last_error = e.to_string();
                    tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(4)).await;
                }
            }
        }
        Err(anyhow!("FATAL TRANSPORT FRACTURE after 6 attempts. Last error: {}", last_error))
    }

    fn extract_json_array(text: &str) -> Option<String> {
        let start = text.find('[')?;
        let mut depth = 0;
        for (i, c) in text[start..].char_indices() {
            match c {
                '[' => depth += 1,
                ']' => {
                    depth -= 1;
                    if depth == 0 {
                        return Some(text[start..=start + i].to_string());
                    }
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        let partial = &text[start..];
        let mut end_idx = partial.len();
        while let Some(last_close) = partial[..end_idx].rfind('}') {
            let candidate = format!("{}]", &partial[..=last_close]);
            let clean_candidate: String = candidate.chars().filter(|c| !c.is_control() || *c == '\n' || *c == '\t').collect();
            if serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&clean_candidate).is_ok() {
                return Some(clean_candidate);
            }
            end_idx = last_close;
        }
        None
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/orchestrator_v2/critic.rs 
=========================================
use reqwest::Client;
use crate::orchestrator_v2::task_graph::TaskGraph;

pub enum CriticVerdict {
    Approved,
    Rejected { feedback: String },
}

pub struct CriticAgent;

impl CriticAgent {
    pub async fn evaluate_plan(_client: &Client, _api_key: &str, _directive: &str, _graph: &TaskGraph) -> Result<CriticVerdict, String> {
        // Default to approved. The LLM will write the actual heuristic logic later.
        Ok(CriticVerdict::Approved)
    }
}

use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};

pub struct RuntimeMetrics {
    pub active_tasks: AtomicU64,
    pub crashed_agents: AtomicU64,
    pub emitted_events: AtomicU64,
}

impl RuntimeMetrics {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            active_tasks: AtomicU64::new(0),
            crashed_agents: AtomicU64::new(0),
            emitted_events: AtomicU64::new(0),
        }
    }

    pub fn inc_tasks(&self) {
        self.active_tasks.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    }
}

impl Default for RuntimeMetrics {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/task_manager_v4.rs

use crate::cognition::meta_reasoner::MetaReasoner;
use crate::cortical_loop::{CortexOutcome, CorticalLoop};
use crate::hypothesis::HypothesisGenerator;
use crate::runtime_metrics::RuntimeMetrics;
use crate::task_manager::TaskContext;
use crate::world_model::WorldModel;
use anyhow::Result;
use std::sync::Arc;

pub async fn send_task_v4(ctx: TaskContext, directive: &str) -> Result<String> {
    // Wire runtime_metrics
    let metrics = RuntimeMetrics::new();
    metrics.inc_tasks();

    // Wire hypothesis logic
    let _ = HypothesisGenerator::generate(directive).await;

    // Wire meta_reasoner
    let _ = MetaReasoner::execute_with_got(&ctx.client, &ctx.api_key, directive).await;

    // Wire memory actors (activating both variations to repair orphaned signatures)
    let (_tx_mem, rx_mem) = tokio::sync::mpsc::channel::<crate::memory_actor::MemoryCommand>(1);
    let _mem_actor_1 = crate::memory_actor::MemoryActor::new(rx_mem);
    let (_mem_actor_2, _handle) = crate::agents::memory_actor::MemoryActor::start();

    // Wire unified CorticalLoop
    let world_model = Arc::new(WorldModel::new());
    let mut cortex = CorticalLoop::new(
        ctx.api_key.clone(),
        world_model,
        ctx.memory.clone(),
        ctx.vmo_state.clone(),
        ctx.dist.clone(),
        ctx.tx.clone(),
    );
    match cortex.ignite(directive).await? {
        CortexOutcome::Proven { belief } => {
            Ok(format!("CorticalLoop Execution Complete (belief {:.2})", belief))
        }
        CortexOutcome::TurnsExhausted { best_belief } => Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
            "CorticalLoop exhausted turns without proving the strategy (best belief {:.2})",
            best_belief
        )),
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/wasm_forge.rs 
=========================================
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use base64::{engine::general_purpose, Engine as _};
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
use tokio::fs;

pub struct WasmForge;

impl WasmForge {
    pub async fn synthesize_cell(logic_code: &str) -> Result<String> {
        if logic_code.trim().starts_with("const std") || logic_code.contains("export fn") {
            return Self::synthesize_zig(logic_code).await;
        }
        Self::synthesize_rust(logic_code).await
    }

    async fn synthesize_zig(zig_code: &str) -> Result<String> {
        let ts = SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)?.as_secs();
        let tmp_path = format!("/tmp/nexus_zig_cell_{}.zig", ts);
        fs::write(&tmp_path, zig_code).await?;

        let output = tokio::process::Command::new("zig")
            .args([
                "build-exe",
                &tmp_path,
                "-target",
                "wasm32-freestanding",
                "-O",
                "ReleaseSmall",
                "--name",
                "cell",
            ])
            .current_dir("/tmp")
            .output()
            .await?;

        if !output.status.success() {
            let _ = fs::remove_file(&tmp_path).await;
            return Err(anyhow!("Zig compile error: {}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr)));
        }

        let wasm_bytes = fs::read("/tmp/cell.wasm").await?;
        let b64 = general_purpose::STANDARD.encode(&wasm_bytes);
        let _ = fs::remove_file(&tmp_path).await;
        Ok(b64)
    }

    // ⚡ REAL RUST TO WASM PIPELINE
    async fn synthesize_rust(logic_code: &str) -> Result<String> {
        let ts = SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)?.as_secs();
        let tmp_dir = format!("/tmp/nexus_rust_cell_{}", ts);
        fs::create_dir_all(&tmp_dir).await?;

        let cargo_toml = r#"[package]
name = "cell"
version = "0.1.0"
edition = "2021"
[lib]
crate-type = ["cdylib"]
"#;
        fs::write(format!("{}/Cargo.toml", tmp_dir), cargo_toml).await?;

        let src_dir = format!("{}/src", tmp_dir);
        fs::create_dir_all(&src_dir).await?;
        fs::write(format!("{}/lib.rs", src_dir), logic_code).await?;

        let output = tokio::process::Command::new("cargo")
            .args(["build", "--target", "wasm32-wasip1", "--release"])
            .current_dir(&tmp_dir)
            .output()
            .await?;

        if !output.status.success() {
            let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr);
            let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&tmp_dir).await;
            return Err(anyhow!("Rust WASM compile error:\n{}", stderr));
        }

        let wasm_bytes =
            fs::read(format!("{}/target/wasm32-wasip1/release/cell.wasm", tmp_dir)).await?;
        let b64 = general_purpose::STANDARD.encode(&wasm_bytes);
        let _ = fs::remove_dir_all(&tmp_dir).await;
        Ok(b64)
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/critic.rs 
=========================================
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, Default)]
pub struct CriticVerdict {
    pub approved: bool,
    pub confidence: f32,
    pub reason: String,
    pub suggested_recovery: Option<String>,
}

pub struct Critic;

impl Critic {
    pub async fn evaluate(
        _objective: &str,
        observations: &str,
        _history: &str,
    ) -> Result<CriticVerdict, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
        // If there is no observation history yet, we can't be done
        if observations.trim().is_empty() {
            return Ok(CriticVerdict {
                approved: false,
                confidence: 0.1,
                reason: "No actions have been executed yet. Cannot halt.".into(),
                suggested_recovery: Some("Execute an action first.".into()),
            });
        }

        // We do NOT check for [HALT] here, because the Critic is invoked specifically
        // to validate the Halt intent *before* it gets added to the log.
        let has_write = observations.contains("[WRITE_OK]")
            || observations.contains("[DIFF_PATCH_OK]")
            || observations.contains("[PATCH_FUNCTION_OK]")
            || observations.contains("success");
        let has_read = observations.contains("[READ_OK]");
        let has_cmd = observations.contains("[CMD_OK]");
        let has_search = observations.contains("[SEARCH_OK]");

        let approved = has_write || has_read || has_cmd || has_search;
        let confidence = if approved { 0.95 } else { 0.4 };

        Ok(CriticVerdict {
            approved,
            confidence,
            reason: if approved {
                "Concrete success indicators found in action history.".into()
            } else {
                "Missing concrete success indicators (Read/Write/Cmd).".into()
            },
            suggested_recovery: if !approved {
                Some("Perform a read, write, or command execution to gather evidence.".into())
            } else {
                None
            },
        })
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/evidence/belief.rs 
=========================================
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::ops::{Add, AddAssign, Mul};

/// Multidimensional belief state used by the Executive Cortex.
/// All fields are clamped to [0.0, 1.0] on construction and after arithmetic.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct BeliefVector {
    /// How confident we are that a proposed implementation / strategy is correct.
    pub implementation_confidence: f32,

    /// Quality / reliability of the supporting evidence (0 = pure speculation).
    pub evidence_quality: f32,

    /// Architectural risk of the current path (higher = more dangerous / brittle).
    pub architectural_risk: f32,

    /// Confidence that the path is free of security / sandbox violations.
    pub security_confidence: f32,
}

impl BeliefVector {
    pub const ZERO: Self = Self {
        implementation_confidence: 0.0,
        evidence_quality: 0.0,
        architectural_risk: 0.0,
        security_confidence: 0.0,
    };

    pub fn new(
        implementation_confidence: f32,
        evidence_quality: f32,
        architectural_risk: f32,
        security_confidence: f32,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            implementation_confidence: implementation_confidence.clamp(0.0, 1.0),
            evidence_quality: evidence_quality.clamp(0.0, 1.0),
            architectural_risk: architectural_risk.clamp(0.0, 1.0),
            security_confidence: security_confidence.clamp(0.0, 1.0),
        }
    }

    /// Scalar utility used by the current ranking logic while we migrate.
    pub fn scalar_score(&self) -> f32 {
        let positive = (self.implementation_confidence * 0.40)
            + (self.evidence_quality * 0.35)
            + (self.security_confidence * 0.25);
        let penalty = self.architectural_risk * 0.45;
        (positive - penalty).clamp(0.0, 1.0)
    }

    /// Combine two vectors with a simple Bayesian-style update (weighted average).
    pub fn update(&self, other: &Self, weight: f32) -> Self {
        let w = weight.clamp(0.0, 1.0);
        Self::new(
            self.implementation_confidence * (1.0 - w) + other.implementation_confidence * w,
            self.evidence_quality * (1.0 - w) + other.evidence_quality * w,
            self.architectural_risk * (1.0 - w) + other.architectural_risk * w,
            self.security_confidence * (1.0 - w) + other.security_confidence * w,
        )
    }
}

impl Default for BeliefVector {
    fn default() -> Self {
        // Slightly optimistic prior so pure exploration is not immediately killed.
        Self::new(0.45, 0.30, 0.40, 0.55)
    }
}

impl Add for BeliefVector {
    type Output = Self;
    fn add(self, rhs: Self) -> Self {
        Self::new(
            self.implementation_confidence + rhs.implementation_confidence,
            self.evidence_quality + rhs.evidence_quality,
            self.architectural_risk + rhs.architectural_risk,
            self.security_confidence + rhs.security_confidence,
        )
    }
}

impl AddAssign for BeliefVector {
    fn add_assign(&mut self, rhs: Self) {
        *self = *self + rhs;
    }
}

impl Mul<f32> for BeliefVector {
    type Output = Self;
    fn mul(self, rhs: f32) -> Self {
        Self::new(
            self.implementation_confidence * rhs,
            self.evidence_quality * rhs,
            self.architectural_risk * rhs,
            self.security_confidence * rhs,
        )
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/evidence/graph.rs 
=========================================
use crate::evidence::{BeliefVector, Evidence};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use uuid::Uuid;

/// EvidenceGraph – a lightweight, strategy-keyed store of Evidence nodes.
#[derive(Debug, Default, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct EvidenceGraph {
    /// strategy_id → list of Evidence that support / refute it
    by_strategy: HashMap<Uuid, Vec<Evidence>>,

    /// Global chronological log (useful for later dream-cycle / audit)
    chronology: Vec<Uuid>,
}

impl EvidenceGraph {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    /// Insert a fully materialised Evidence node.
    pub fn insert(&mut self, evidence: Evidence) {
        if let Some(strategy_id) = evidence.related_strategy_id {
            self.by_strategy
                .entry(strategy_id)
                .or_default()
                .push(evidence.clone());
        }
        self.chronology.push(evidence.id);
    }

    /// Aggregate all evidence belonging to a strategy into a single BeliefVector.
    pub fn aggregate_belief(
        &self,
        strategy_id: Uuid,
        baseline: BeliefVector,
    ) -> BeliefVector {
        let Some(evidences) = self.by_strategy.get(&strategy_id) else {
            return baseline;
        };

        let mut current = baseline;

        for ev in evidences {
            let weight = (0.35 + ev.belief_delta.evidence_quality * 0.55).clamp(0.15, 0.85);
            current = current.update(&ev.belief_delta, weight);
        }

        current
    }

    pub fn evidence_count(&self, strategy_id: Uuid) -> usize {
        self.by_strategy
            .get(&strategy_id)
            .map(|v| v.len())
            .unwrap_or(0)
    }

    pub fn evidence_for(&self, strategy_id: Uuid) -> &[Evidence] {
        self.by_strategy
            .get(&strategy_id)
            .map(|v| v.as_slice())
            .unwrap_or(&[])
    }

    pub fn chronology(&self) -> &[Uuid] {
        &self.chronology
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/evidence/manager.rs 
=========================================
use crate::evidence::{BeliefVector, Evidence, EvidenceKind, Observation};
use crate::epistemic_engine::EpistemicEngine;
use reqwest::Client;
use uuid::Uuid;

/// Central translation layer between the RoboticExecutor and the EvidenceGraph.
///
/// - Fast path  : pure heuristic (microseconds)
/// - Slow path  : optional LLM escalation for truly ambiguous observations
#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct EvidenceManager;

impl EvidenceManager {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self
    }

    /// Synchronous, zero-network path (kept for compatibility).
    pub fn process_observation(
        &self,
        obs: Observation,
        strategy_id: Option<Uuid>,
    ) -> Evidence {
        let kind = Evidence::classify(&obs.raw, obs.success);
        let belief_delta = self.compute_belief_delta(&obs, kind);
        Evidence::from_observation(obs, kind, belief_delta, strategy_id)
    }

    /// Async path with smart escalation.
    pub async fn process_observation_with_escalation(
        &self,
        obs: Observation,
        strategy_id: Option<Uuid>,
        client: &Client,
        api_key: &str,
    ) -> Evidence {
        let kind = Evidence::classify(&obs.raw, obs.success);

        // Fast path
        if !Self::should_escalate(&obs, kind) {
            let belief_delta = self.compute_belief_delta(&obs, kind);
            return Evidence::from_observation(obs, kind, belief_delta, strategy_id);
        }

        // Slow path
        println!(
            "\x1b[35m>> [EVIDENCE] Ambiguous observation ({} chars) — escalating to light critic\x1b[0m",
            obs.raw.len()
        );

        match EpistemicEngine::evaluate_evidence(client, api_key, "Analyze observation", &obs.raw).await {
            Ok(response) => {
                // Map the 1D float back into a multi-dimensional BeliefVector
                let val = response.confidence_modifier;
                let delta = BeliefVector::new(val * 0.5, val, val * -0.2, val * 0.3);
                Evidence::from_observation(obs, kind, delta, strategy_id)
            },
            Err(e) => {
                println!(
                    "\x1b[33m>> [EVIDENCE] Escalation failed ({}) — falling back to Neutral/ZERO\x1b[0m",
                    e
                );
                Evidence::from_observation(obs, EvidenceKind::Neutral, BeliefVector::ZERO, strategy_id)
            }
        }
    }

    fn should_escalate(obs: &Observation, kind: EvidenceKind) -> bool {
        match kind {
            EvidenceKind::CommandSuccess
            | EvidenceKind::CommandFailure
            | EvidenceKind::SecurityViolation
            | EvidenceKind::FileRead
            | EvidenceKind::FileWrite
            | EvidenceKind::ArchitecturalSmell
            | EvidenceKind::PatternMatch
            | EvidenceKind::PositiveSignal => false,
            EvidenceKind::Neutral => obs.raw.len() > 500,
        }
    }

    fn compute_belief_delta(&self, obs: &Observation, kind: EvidenceKind) -> BeliefVector {
        match kind {
            EvidenceKind::SecurityViolation => BeliefVector::new(-0.15, -0.10, 0.55, -0.85),
            EvidenceKind::CommandSuccess => {
                if obs.source.contains("read") || obs.raw.contains("[READ_OK]") {
                    BeliefVector::new(0.18, 0.42, -0.08, 0.12)
                } else {
                    BeliefVector::new(0.22, 0.28, -0.05, 0.10)
                }
            }
            EvidenceKind::CommandFailure => BeliefVector::new(-0.25, 0.15, 0.20, -0.05),
            EvidenceKind::FileRead => BeliefVector::new(0.12, 0.45, -0.05, 0.08),
            EvidenceKind::FileWrite => BeliefVector::new(0.20, 0.30, 0.10, -0.05),
            EvidenceKind::PatternMatch => BeliefVector::new(0.15, 0.35, -0.05, 0.05),
            EvidenceKind::ArchitecturalSmell => BeliefVector::new(-0.18, 0.20, 0.40, -0.08),
            EvidenceKind::PositiveSignal => BeliefVector::new(0.18, 0.25, -0.08, 0.10),
            EvidenceKind::Neutral => BeliefVector::ZERO,
        }
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/evidence/types.rs 
=========================================
use crate::evidence::BeliefVector;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
use uuid::Uuid;

/// Raw observation string coming out of the RoboticExecutor.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Observation {
    pub raw: String,
    pub source: String,          // "executor", "grep", "read_file", "podman", etc.
    pub success: bool,
    pub latency_ms: u64,
}

/// Semantic classification of an observation.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum EvidenceKind {
    CommandSuccess,
    CommandFailure,
    FileRead,
    FileWrite,
    PatternMatch,
    SecurityViolation,
    ArchitecturalSmell,
    PositiveSignal,
    Neutral,
}

/// Fully materialised evidence node that can be inserted into the EvidenceGraph.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Evidence {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub observation: Observation,
    pub kind: EvidenceKind,
    pub belief_delta: BeliefVector,
    pub timestamp: u64,
    pub related_strategy_id: Option<Uuid>,
}

impl Evidence {
    pub fn from_observation(
        obs: Observation,
        kind: EvidenceKind,
        belief_delta: BeliefVector,
        related_strategy_id: Option<Uuid>,
    ) -> Self {
        let timestamp = SystemTime::now()
            .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
            .unwrap_or_default()
            .as_secs();

        Self {
            id: Uuid::new_v4(),
            observation: obs,
            kind,
            belief_delta,
            timestamp,
            related_strategy_id,
        }
    }

    /// Heuristic classifier used by EvidenceManager when the caller does not
    /// supply an explicit kind. This is deliberately conservative.
    pub fn classify(raw: &str, success: bool) -> EvidenceKind {
        let lower = raw.to_lowercase();

        if lower.contains("sandbox_violation")
            || lower.contains("path_traversal")
            || lower.contains("threat pattern")
            || lower.contains("sandbox_violation")
        {
            return EvidenceKind::SecurityViolation;
        }

        if lower.contains("pattern_not_found")
            || lower.contains("function_not_found")
            || lower.contains("action_failed")
            || lower.contains("[action failed]")
        {
            return EvidenceKind::CommandFailure;
        }

        if lower.contains("[cmd_ok]") || lower.contains("[read_ok]") || lower.contains("[write_ok]")
            || lower.contains("[diff_patch_ok]") || lower.contains("[patch_function_ok]")
            || lower.contains("[search_ok]") || lower.contains("[vmo_created]")
            || lower.contains("[snapshot_ok]")
        {
            return if success {
                EvidenceKind::CommandSuccess
            } else {
                EvidenceKind::CommandFailure
            };
        }

        if lower.contains("todo") || lower.contains("placeholder") || lower.contains("unimplemented")
        {
            return EvidenceKind::ArchitecturalSmell;
        }

        if success {
            EvidenceKind::PositiveSignal
        } else {
            EvidenceKind::Neutral
        }
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/json_parser.rs 
=========================================
use crate::agent_protocol::AgentAction;
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};

/// Streamlined, strict JSON extractor leveraging Native Structured Outputs.
pub fn extract_actions(response: &str) -> Result<Vec<AgentAction>> {
    let text = response.trim();

    if text.is_empty() || text == "[]" {
        return Ok(vec![]);
    }

    // Strip markdown formatting if the model still wrapped it
    let clean_text = text.replace("```json", "").replace("```", "").trim().to_string();

    // Since we now rely on strict JSON generation from the LLM,
    // we bypass the legacy heuristics and directly parse.
    serde_json::from_str::<Vec<AgentAction>>(&clean_text).map_err(|e| {
        anyhow!(
            "PARSER_FRACTURE: Failed to parse strict JSON: {}.\nRaw Data: {}",
            e,
            clean_text.chars().take(300).collect::<String>()
        )
    })
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/math_utils.rs 
=========================================
/// Calculates the nth Fibonacci number using checked arithmetic to prevent overflow.
/// Returns None if the result exceeds the capacity of u128.
pub fn fibonacci(n: u32) -> Option<u128> {
    if n == 0 {
        return Some(0);
    }
    if n == 1 {
        return Some(1);
    }

    let mut a: u128 = 0;
    let mut b: u128 = 1;

    for _ in 2..=n {
        let next = a.checked_add(b)?;
        a = b;
        b = next;
    }

    Some(b)
}

pub fn get_light_models() -> Vec<String> {
    let mut cascade = Vec::new();
    if let Ok(m) = std::env::var("NEXUS_LIGHT_MODEL") {
        if !m.trim().is_empty() { cascade.push(m.trim().to_string()); }
    }
    cascade.push("gemini-3.1-flash-lite".to_string());
    cascade.push("gemma-4-26b-a4b-it".to_string());
    
    // Deduplicate list
    let mut unique = Vec::new();
    for m in cascade {
        if !unique.contains(&m) { unique.push(m); }
    }
    unique
}

pub fn get_heavy_models() -> Vec<String> {
    let mut cascade = Vec::new();
    if let Ok(m) = std::env::var("NEXUS_HEAVY_MODEL") {
        if !m.trim().is_empty() { cascade.push(m.trim().to_string()); }
    }
    cascade.push("gemma-4-31b-it".to_string());
    cascade.push("gemini-3.1-flash-lite".to_string());
    
    let mut unique = Vec::new();
    for m in cascade {
        if !unique.contains(&m) { unique.push(m); }
    }
    unique
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/observation_adapter.rs 
=========================================
use nexus_executive_cortex::schema::{Observation, ObservationEnvelope};
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
use uuid::Uuid;

/// Translates raw, ad-hoc runtime events into pure, timestamped Observations.
pub struct ObservationAdapter;

impl ObservationAdapter {
    /// Core wrapping function to ensure all observations are uniformly timestamped and ID'd.
    pub fn wrap(observation: Observation) -> ObservationEnvelope {
        let timestamp = SystemTime::now()
            .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
            .unwrap_or_default()
            .as_secs();

        ObservationEnvelope {
            id: Uuid::new_v4(),
            timestamp,
            observation,
        }
    }

    /// Translation: User inputs a directive via CLI or Web UI
    pub fn from_human_directive(directive: &str) -> ObservationEnvelope {
        Self::wrap(Observation::HumanDirective {
            directive: directive.to_string(),
        })
    }

    /// Translation: The LLM planner exhausts its retry quota
    pub fn from_planner_timeout() -> ObservationEnvelope {
        Self::wrap(Observation::PlannerTimeout)
    }

    /// Translation: A MicroAgent finishes its assigned task
    pub fn from_task_completion(task_id: &str, output: &str) -> ObservationEnvelope {
        Self::wrap(Observation::TaskCompleted {
            task_id: task_id.to_string(),
            output: output.to_string(),
        })
    }

    /// Translation: An agent hits a fatal error
    pub fn from_goal_failure(goal_id: Uuid, reason: &str) -> ObservationEnvelope {
        Self::wrap(Observation::GoalFailed {
            goal_id,
            reason: reason.to_string(),
        })
    }

    /// Translation: Filtered health spikes from the thermal/system monitor
    pub fn from_health_spike(cpu: f32, ram: f32) -> ObservationEnvelope {
        Self::wrap(Observation::HealthChanged { cpu, ram })
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/policy.rs 
=========================================
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum Capability {
    ReadSubstrate,
    WriteSubstrate,
    ExecuteSystemCall,
    HaltCognition,
}

pub struct ExecutionSandbox {
    pub active_capabilities: Vec<Capability>,
}

impl ExecutionSandbox {
    /// Default safe-mode: The agent can read, but cannot mutate or execute.
    pub fn strict_lockdown() -> Self {
        Self { active_capabilities: vec![Capability::ReadSubstrate] }
    }

    /// Validates the intended action against granted capabilities before it hits the OS
    pub fn validate_intent(&self, action_type: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        let required_token = match action_type {
            "read_file" | "search_memory" => Capability::ReadSubstrate,
            "write_file" | "diff_patch" | "patch_function" => Capability::WriteSubstrate,
            "execute_command" => Capability::ExecuteSystemCall,
            "halt" => Capability::HaltCognition,
            _ => return Err(format!("SECURITY_FAULT: Unknown action taxonomy '{}'", action_type)),
        };

        if self.active_capabilities.contains(&required_token) {
            Ok(())
        } else {
            Err(format!("POLICY_VIOLATION: Agent lacks {:?} capability.", required_token))
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum DirectiveClass {
    ShellOp,
    TargetedEdit { file_hint: String },
    RepoDoctor,
    SecurityAudit,
    WasmForge,
    Inquiry,
    FeatureBuild,
    General,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ContextBudget {
    pub max_file_chars: usize,
    pub max_obs_lines: usize,
    pub max_schema_chars: usize,
    pub max_output_tokens: u32,
}

impl ContextBudget {
    pub fn minimal() -> Self {
        Self {
            max_file_chars: 1_500,
            max_obs_lines: 2,
            max_schema_chars: 400,
            max_output_tokens: 1024,
        }
    }
    pub fn standard() -> Self {
        Self {
            max_file_chars: 8_000,
            max_obs_lines: 6,
            max_schema_chars: 1_200,
            max_output_tokens: 4096,
        }
    }
    pub fn doctor() -> Self {
        Self {
            max_file_chars: 15_000,
            max_obs_lines: 10,
            max_schema_chars: 1_500,
            max_output_tokens: 8192,
        }
    }
    pub fn security() -> Self {
        Self {
            max_file_chars: 20_000,
            max_obs_lines: 10,
            max_schema_chars: 2_000,
            max_output_tokens: 8192,
        }
    }
}

pub struct AgentProfile {
    pub class: DirectiveClass,
    pub primary_model: &'static str,
    pub max_turns: u8,
    pub budget: ContextBudget,
    pub persona: String,
}

pub struct IntelligentRouter;
impl IntelligentRouter {
    pub fn classify(d: &str) -> DirectiveClass {
        let d = d.to_lowercase();
        if ["diagnose", "doctor", "fix repo", "suture"].iter().any(|k| d.contains(k)) {
            return DirectiveClass::RepoDoctor;
        }
        if ["cargo check", "ls ", "find "].iter().any(|k| d.contains(k)) {
            return DirectiveClass::ShellOp;
        }
        if ["fix", "patch", "edit"].iter().any(|k| d.contains(k)) {
            return DirectiveClass::TargetedEdit { file_hint: "target".into() };
        }
        DirectiveClass::General
    }

    pub fn route(directive: &str) -> AgentProfile {
        let class = Self::classify(directive);
        let persona = r#"IDENTITY: NEXUS Sovereign Agent.
STRICT SYSTEM RULE: You MUST output EXACTLY ONE valid JSON array of AgentAction objects. No markdown.

MANDATORY SCHEMA:
[
  { "type": "patch_function", "path": "...", "function_name": "...", "new_body": "...", "reasoning": "..." },
  { "type": "diff_patch", "path": "...", "search": "...", "replace": "...", "reasoning": "..." },
  { "type": "write_file", "path": "...", "content": "...", "reasoning": "..." },
  { "type": "read_file", "path": "...", "reasoning": "..." },
  { "type": "execute_command", "command": "...", "reasoning": "..." },
  { "type": "search_memory", "query": "...", "reasoning": "..." },
  { "type": "halt", "reasoning": "..." }
]

CRITICAL RULES:
1. NEVER use `write_file` to modify an existing function. You MUST use `patch_function` or `diff_patch`.
2. `patch_function` requires `path`, `function_name`, and the full replacement code in `new_body`.
3. `diff_patch` requires `search` (the exact old string) and `replace` (the exact new string). DO NOT output unified diffs or use a `diff` field.
4. When you have achieved the task objective, include the `halt` action in your JSON array.
5. You MUST escape all newlines inside JSON strings as `\n` (e.g. "line1\nline2").
6. CRITICAL HALT OVERRIDE: You are strictly forbidden from issuing a [HALT] command if your objective requires code modification (patching). Finding the target code via 'grep' or 'cat' is only reconnaissance. You MUST verify the presence of a successful [PATCH_FUNCTION_OK], [WRITE_OK], or [DIFF_PATCH_OK] in your physical evidence buffer before you are authorized to halt. Halting before patching is considered a critical mission failure."#;

        let (primary_model, budget) = match class {
            DirectiveClass::RepoDoctor | DirectiveClass::SecurityAudit => {
                ("gemma-4-31b-it", ContextBudget::doctor())
            }
            _ => ("gemini-3.1-flash-lite", ContextBudget::standard()),
        };

        AgentProfile { class, primary_model, max_turns: 8, budget, persona: persona.to_string() }
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/wasm_runtime.rs 
=========================================
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use wasmtime::*;

pub struct WasmRuntime;

impl WasmRuntime {
    fn make_engine() -> Result<Engine> {
        let mut config = Config::new();
        config.consume_fuel(true);
        Ok(Engine::new(&config)?)
    }

    pub fn run_bytes(bytes: &[u8], func_name: &str, input: f32) -> Result<f32> {
        let engine = Self::make_engine()?;
        let module = Module::from_binary(&engine, bytes)?;
        let mut store = Store::new(&engine, ());
        store.set_fuel(50_000_000)?;
        let instance = Instance::new(&mut store, &module, &[])?;
        let func = instance.get_typed_func::<f32, f32>(&mut store, func_name)?;
        Ok(func.call(&mut store, input)?)
    }

    pub fn run_with_buffer(bytes: &[u8], func_name: &str, data: &[u8]) -> Result<f32> {
        let engine = Self::make_engine()?;
        let module =
            Module::from_binary(&engine, bytes).map_err(|e| anyhow!("WASM_LOAD_FAULT: {}", e))?;
        let mut store = Store::new(&engine, ());
        store.set_fuel(50_000_000)?;
        let instance = Instance::new(&mut store, &module, &[])
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("WASM_INST_FAULT: {}", e))?;

        let memory = instance
            .get_memory(&mut store, "memory")
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("WASM module must export memory"))?;

        let get_ptr = instance
            .get_typed_func::<(), i32>(&mut store, "get_buffer_ptr")
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("Missing get_buffer_ptr export: {}", e))?;
        let ptr = get_ptr.call(&mut store, ())? as usize;

        if data.len() > 65_536 {
            return Err(anyhow!("VMO payload exceeds 64KB sandbox."));
        }

        // WASMtime 43+ API: Use .write() directly on the memory slice
        memory
            .write(&mut store, ptr, data)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("WASM_MEMORY_WRITE_FAULT: {}", e))?;

        let func = instance
            .get_typed_func::<i32, f32>(&mut store, func_name)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("WASM_FUNC_MISSING: {}", e))?;

        Ok(func.call(&mut store, data.len() as i32)?)
    }
}

use anyhow::Result;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::process::Command;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct WorkspaceNode {
    pub path: String,
    pub kind: String,
    pub deps: Vec<String>,
}

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct WorldModel {
    pub nodes: HashMap<String, WorkspaceNode>,
    pub last_refresh: u64,
}

impl WorldModel {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    pub async fn refresh(&mut self) -> Result<()> {
        self.last_refresh =
            std::time::SystemTime::now().duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)?.as_secs();
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn get_summary(&self) -> String {
        format!("Workspace: {} nodes. Main binary: ide_backend.", self.nodes.len())
    }

    pub async fn get_topology() -> Option<String> {
        None
    }

    pub async fn semantic_query(&self, _mission: &str) -> Vec<String> {
        let mut chunks = Vec::new();

        // ⚡ Give the Cortex eyes: Pull the actual file tree so the agent stops guessing!
        if let Ok(out) = Command::new("find")
            .args([
                ".",
                "-maxdepth",
                "4",
                "-type",
                "f",
                "-not",
                "-path",
                "*/.git/*",
                "-not",
                "-path",
                "*/target/*",
                "-not",
                "-path",
                "*/node_modules/*",
                "-not",
                "-path",
                "*/.cold_storage/*",
            ])
            .output()
        {
            if out.status.success() {
                let files = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);
                chunks.push("[REPOSITORY FILE TREE (Depth 4)]".to_string());
                chunks.push(files.chars().take(6000).collect()); // Shield against blowout
            }
        }

        chunks
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/cognition_graph.rs 
=========================================
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum NodeKind {
    Observation,
    Verification,
    Reflection,
    Hypothesis,
    ProposedAction { action_json: String },
    Strategy,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct VerificationReport {
    pub goal_satisfied: bool,
    pub evidence_confidence: f32,
    pub independent_check_passed: bool,
    pub rationale: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct CognitiveNode {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub kind: NodeKind,
    pub content: String,
    pub belief_score: f32,
    pub computation_cost: u32,
    pub is_active: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum EdgeRelation {
    Supports,
    Contradicts,
    Prerequisite,
    DerivesFrom,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct GraphEdge {
    pub source: Uuid,
    pub target: Uuid,
    pub relation: EdgeRelation,
    pub weight: f32,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct CognitiveGraph {
    pub nodes: HashMap<Uuid, CognitiveNode>,
    pub edges: Vec<GraphEdge>,
    pub frontiers: HashSet<Uuid>,
}

impl CognitiveGraph {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    pub fn add_node(
        &mut self,
        kind: NodeKind,
        content: &str,
        initial_belief: f32,
        cost: u32,
    ) -> Uuid {
        let id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let node = CognitiveNode {
            id,
            kind,
            content: content.to_string(),
            belief_score: initial_belief,
            computation_cost: cost,
            is_active: false,
        };
        self.nodes.insert(id, node);
        self.frontiers.insert(id);
        id
    }

    pub fn add_edge(&mut self, source: Uuid, target: Uuid, relation: EdgeRelation, weight: f32) {
        self.edges.push(GraphEdge { source, target, relation, weight });
    }

    pub fn propagate_beliefs(&mut self) {
        let mut updates: HashMap<Uuid, f32> = HashMap::new();
        for edge in &self.edges {
            if let Some(source_node) = self.nodes.get(&edge.source) {
                if let Some(target_node) = self.nodes.get(&edge.target) {
                    let impact = source_node.belief_score * edge.weight;
                    let mut new_belief = target_node.belief_score;
                    match edge.relation {
                        EdgeRelation::Supports => new_belief = (new_belief + impact).min(1.0),
                        EdgeRelation::Contradicts => new_belief = (new_belief - impact).max(0.0),
                        _ => {}
                    }
                    updates.insert(edge.target, new_belief);
                }
            }
        }
        for (id, new_score) in updates {
            if let Some(node) = self.nodes.get_mut(&id) {
                node.belief_score = new_score;
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn select_best_hypothesis(&self) -> Option<&CognitiveNode> {
        self.nodes
            .values()
            // ⚡ FIX: Filter out dead hypotheses (belief < 0.15) so we don't get stuck in a death loop!
            .filter(|n| matches!(n.kind, NodeKind::Hypothesis) && n.belief_score >= 0.15)
            .max_by(|a, b| {
                let score_a = a.belief_score / (a.computation_cost as f32).max(1.0);
                let score_b = b.belief_score / (b.computation_cost as f32).max(1.0);
                score_a.partial_cmp(&score_b).unwrap_or(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal)
            })
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/conductor.rs 
=========================================
use crate::metrics::METRICS;
use crate::orchestrator::Orchestrator;
use crate::router::AgentProfile;
use nexus_model_hub::factory::AgentFactory;
use nexus_model_hub::neural_agent::ContextPacket;
use nexus_substrate::token_ledger::wait_for_quota;
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::json;
use std::sync::atomic::Ordering;
use tokio::sync::broadcast;

const ESTIMATED_TOKENS: u32 = 2_000;

pub struct Conductor;

pub struct TurnContext<'a> {
    pub orch: &'a Orchestrator,
    pub client: &'a Client,
    pub api_key: &'a str,
    pub tx: Option<broadcast::Sender<String>>,
}

impl Conductor {
    pub async fn run_turn(
        profile: &AgentProfile,
        prompt: String,
        turn: usize,
        ctx: TurnContext<'_>,
        errors: usize,
    ) -> Result<String, String> {
        METRICS.api_calls.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);

        let model = AgentFactory::get_model_for_turn(turn, errors);
        let _ = wait_for_quota(model, ESTIMATED_TOKENS).await;

        let neural_ctx = ContextPacket {
            system_prompt: Some(profile.persona.clone()),
            model_override: Some(model.to_string()),
            ..Default::default()
        };

        if let Some(ref sender) = ctx.tx {
            let _ = sender.send(
                json!({"type": "NEURAL_THOUGHT", "payload": format!("[CONDUCTOR] turn {} → {}", turn, model)}).to_string()
            );
        }

        let result =
            ctx.orch.run(prompt.clone(), neural_ctx, ctx.client, ctx.api_key, ctx.tx.clone()).await;

        if let Err(ref e) = result {
            if e.contains("STALE")
                || e.contains("500")
                || e.contains("RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED")
                || e.contains("INTERNAL")
                || e.contains("CONTEXT_OVERFLOW")
            {
                METRICS.task_failures.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);

                let failover = AgentFactory::failover_model(model);
                tracing::warn!("[CONDUCTOR] {} failed → failing over to {}", model, failover);
                let _ = wait_for_quota(failover, ESTIMATED_TOKENS).await;

                let fail_ctx = ContextPacket {
                    system_prompt: Some(profile.persona.clone()),
                    model_override: Some(failover.to_string()),
                    ..Default::default()
                };
                return ctx.orch.run(prompt, fail_ctx, ctx.client, ctx.api_key, ctx.tx).await;
            }
        }
        result
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/podman.rs 
=========================================
use std::io::{Error, ErrorKind, Result};
use std::process::{Command, Output};

pub struct PodmanShield;
impl PodmanShield {
    pub fn execute(command: &str) -> Result<Output> {
        if let Err(e) =
            crate::arbiter_agent::ArbiterAgent::validate_action("execute_command", command)
        {
            return Err(Error::new(ErrorKind::PermissionDenied, e));
        }
        let mut child = Command::new("sh");
        child.arg("-c").arg(command);
        for (key, value) in std::env::vars() {
            if key != "CARGO_BUILD_TARGET" {
                child.env(key, value);
            }
        }
        child.env_remove("CARGO_BUILD_TARGET");
        let out = child.output()?;
        Ok(out)
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/verification.rs

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct VerificationReport {
    pub evidence_quality: f32,
    pub mutation_detected: bool,
    pub command_completed: bool,
    pub goal_verified: bool,
}

impl VerificationReport {
    pub fn from_evidence(evidence: &str, evaluator_confidence: f32) -> Self {
        Self {
            evidence_quality: evaluator_confidence.max(0.0),

            mutation_detected: evidence.contains("[WRITE_OK]")
                || evidence.contains("[PATCH_FUNCTION_OK]")
                || evidence.contains("[DIFF_PATCH_OK]"),

            command_completed: evidence.contains("[CMD_OK]"),

            goal_verified: evaluator_confidence > 0.5,
        }
    }

    pub fn can_complete(&self, belief: f32) -> bool {
        belief >= 0.85 && self.goal_verified && self.evidence_quality >= 0.5
    }
}

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct IntentContract {
    pub id: Uuid,

    /// Human/cognitive objective
    pub objective: String,

    /// Current confidence that this intent is correct
    pub confidence: f32,

    /// Safety classification
    pub risk_level: RiskLevel,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum RiskLevel {
    Low,

    Medium,

    High,
}

impl IntentContract {
    pub fn new(objective: &str, confidence: f32) -> Self {
        Self {
            id: Uuid::new_v4(),

            objective: objective.to_string(),

            confidence,

            risk_level: RiskLevel::Medium,
        }
    }
}
