=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/executive_cortex.rs 
=========================================
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{mpsc, Mutex};
use uuid::Uuid;
use crate::memory_cortex::MemoryCortex;
use crate::memory_types::{CognitiveMemory, MemoryKind};
use crate::orchestrator_v2::task_graph::TaskGraph;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]
pub enum ExecutiveGoalStatus {
    Active,
    Suspended,
    Completed,
    Abandoned,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]
pub struct GoalNode {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub objective: String,
    pub belief_score: f32,
    pub status: ExecutiveGoalStatus,
    pub children: Vec<Uuid>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum ExecutiveCommand {
    InjectGoal { objective: String, parent_id: Option<Uuid> },
    EvaluateEnvironment,
    EmergencyHalt { reason: String },
}

pub struct ExecutiveCortex {
    goals: HashMap<Uuid, GoalNode>,
    active_graph: Option<TaskGraph>,
    memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>,
    command_rx: mpsc::Receiver<ExecutiveCommand>,
}

impl ExecutiveCortex {
    pub fn new(memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>) -> (Self, mpsc::Sender<ExecutiveCommand>) {
        let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel(100);
        (
            Self {
                goals: HashMap::new(),
                active_graph: None,
                memory,
                command_rx: rx,
            },
            tx,
        )
    }

    pub async fn run_actor_loop(mut self) {
        // Run a pulse every 10 seconds for persistent state checking
        let mut heartbeat = tokio::time::interval(std::time::Duration::from_secs(10));
        
        loop {
            tokio::select! {
                Some(cmd) = self.command_rx.recv() => {
                    self.handle_command(cmd).await;
                }
                _ = heartbeat.tick() => {
                    self.cognitive_tick().await;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    async fn cognitive_tick(&mut self) {
        let active_goals: Vec<&GoalNode> = self.goals.values().filter(|g| g.status == ExecutiveGoalStatus::Active).collect();
        
        if active_goals.is_empty() { 
            return; 
        }

        tracing::info!("[EXECUTIVE CORTEX] Pulse check: Maintaining intent vector for {} active goal(s)...", active_goals.len());

        for goal in active_goals {
            let graph_json = serde_json::to_string(&self.active_graph).unwrap_or_default();
            let status_str = format!("{:?}", goal.status);
            let created_at = std::time::SystemTime::now()
                .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
                .unwrap_or_default()
                .as_secs() as i64;

            // Persist the active intention to SQLite, allowing it to survive reboots
            let _ = self.memory.upsert_plan_run(
                &goal.id.to_string(),
                &goal.objective,
                &graph_json,
                &status_str,
                created_at,
            ).await;
        }
    }


    pub async fn evaluate_belief_state(
        &self,
        belief_id: uuid::Uuid,
        confidence: f32,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<crate::belief_policy::BeliefDecision> {

        let decision =
            crate::belief_policy::BeliefDecisionPolicy::evaluate(
                belief_id,
                confidence,
            );

        self.memory
            .store_belief_decision(decision.clone())
            .await?;

        Ok(decision)
    }



    pub async fn apply_belief_decision(
        &mut self,
        goal_id: uuid::Uuid,
        decision: crate::belief_policy::BeliefDecision,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<crate::goal_adaptation::GoalAdaptationDecision> {

        let adaptation =
            crate::goal_adaptation::GoalAdaptationEngine::from_belief_action(
                goal_id,
                decision.action,
            );


        match adaptation.action {

            crate::goal_adaptation::GoalAdaptationAction::PauseGoal => {

                if let Some(goal) = self.goals.get_mut(&goal_id) {
                    goal.status =
                        ExecutiveGoalStatus::Suspended;
                }

            },


            crate::goal_adaptation::GoalAdaptationAction::TerminateGoal => {

                if let Some(goal) = self.goals.get_mut(&goal_id) {
                    goal.status =
                        ExecutiveGoalStatus::Abandoned;
                }

            },


            _ => {}

        }


        self.memory
            .store_goal_adaptation(adaptation.clone())
            .await?;


        Ok(adaptation)
    }


    async fn handle_command(&mut self, cmd: ExecutiveCommand) {
        match cmd {
            ExecutiveCommand::InjectGoal { objective, parent_id } => {
                let id = Uuid::new_v4();
                tracing::info!("\x1b[35m[EXECUTIVE CORTEX] Injecting new background goal: {}\x1b[0m", objective);
                
                let goal = GoalNode {
                    id,
                    objective,
                    belief_score: 0.5, // Seed with standard prior
                    status: ExecutiveGoalStatus::Active,
                    children: vec![],
                };
                self.goals.insert(id, goal);

                if let Some(pid) = parent_id {
                    if let Some(parent) = self.goals.get_mut(&pid) {
                        parent.children.push(id);
                    }
                }
            }
            ExecutiveCommand::EmergencyHalt { reason } => {
                tracing::error!("\x1b[31m[EXECUTIVE CORTEX] GLOBAL HALT TRIGGERED: {}\x1b[0m", reason);
                self.goals.values_mut().for_each(|g| g.status = ExecutiveGoalStatus::Suspended);
            }
            ExecutiveCommand::EvaluateEnvironment => {
                tracing::info!("[EXECUTIVE CORTEX] Environment evaluation requested.");
            }
        }
    }
}


pub fn evaluate_causal_memory(
    memories:
    Vec<crate::causal_recall::CausalRecall>
)
-> Vec<String>
{

    memories
        .iter()
        .map(|m| m.recommendation())
        .collect()

}


=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/task_manager.rs 
=========================================
use crate::memory_cortex::MemoryCortex;
use crate::{
    cortical_loop::{CortexOutcome, CorticalLoop},
    distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler,
    metrics::METRICS,
    orchestrator::Orchestrator,
    orchestrator_v2::planner::PlannerAgent,
    shared_state::VmoState,
    world_model::WorldModel,
};
use reqwest::Client;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};

pub struct TaskContext {
    pub client: Client,
    pub api_key: String,
    pub memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>,
    pub vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>,
    pub tx: broadcast::Sender<String>,
    pub dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
}

pub async fn send_task(
    _orchestrator: Arc<Orchestrator>,
    ctx: TaskContext,
    raw_directive: &str,
) -> String {
    METRICS.inc_task();
    let _ = ctx.tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "LOG_EVENT", "payload": "[EXECUTIVE CORTEX] Initiating DAG Decomposition..."}).to_string());

    // Phase 1: High-Level DAG Decomposition
    let mut graph =
        match PlannerAgent::generate_plan(&ctx.client, &ctx.api_key, raw_directive).await {
            Ok(g) => g,
            Err(e) => return format!("FRACTURE: Planner failed to decompose objective: {}", e),
        };

    graph.compute_critical_path_priorities();
    let order = match graph.resolve_execution_order() {
        Ok(o) => o,
        Err(e) => return format!("FRACTURE: Graph validation failed: {}", e),
    };

    let plan_id = uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
    let _ = ctx.tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "LOG_EVENT", "payload": format!("[EXECUTIVE CORTEX] Plan {} persisted. Fusing Graph of Thoughts Loop...", plan_id)}).to_string());

    let mut task_outputs: HashMap<String, String> = HashMap::new();
    let mut mission_had_failures = false;
    let world_model = Arc::new(WorldModel::new());

    // Phase 2: Topologically Schedule Sub-Tasks via Scoped GoT Loops
    for task_id in order {
        let node = graph.nodes.get(&task_id).unwrap().clone();
        let _ = ctx.tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "LOG_EVENT", "payload": format!("[SCHEDULER] Dispatching DAG Node: {}\nvia Subagent: {}", node.objective, node.required_agent)}).to_string());

        let mut task_context_history = String::new();
        for dep in &node.deps {
            if let Some(out) = task_outputs.get(dep) {
                task_context_history
                    .push_str(&format!("---\n[DEPENDENCY FOCUS FROM {}]:\n{}\n---\n", dep, out));
            }
        }

        // Firing structured context directly into the Epistemic Engine
        let combined_mission = format!(
            "OBJECTIVE: {}\nASSIGNED ROLE: {}\n\nLINEAGE CONTEXT:\n{}",
            node.objective, node.required_agent, task_context_history
        );

        // ⚡ THE HYBRID FUSION: Execute this individual DAG node using a full adversarial Cortical Loop!
        let mut loop_cortex = CorticalLoop::new(
            ctx.api_key.clone(),
            world_model.clone(),
            ctx.memory.clone(),
            ctx.vmo_state.clone(),
            ctx.dist.clone(),
            ctx.tx.clone(),
        );

        let _ = ctx.tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "LOG_EVENT", "payload": format!("[CORTEX] Igniting GoT Epistemic Loop for Node {}", task_id)}).to_string());

        let execution_evidence = match loop_cortex.ignite(&combined_mission).await {
            Ok(CortexOutcome::Proven { belief }) => {
                format!(
                    "[DAG NODE COMPLETE] Ancestor node '{}' concluded execution (belief {:.2}).",
                    node.objective, belief
                )
            }
            Ok(CortexOutcome::TurnsExhausted { best_belief }) => {
                mission_had_failures = true;
                let _ = ctx.tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "LOG_EVENT", "payload": format!("[CORTEX INCOMPLETE] Node {} exhausted turns without proving strategy (best belief {:.2}).", task_id, best_belief)}).to_string());
                format!("[DAG NODE INCOMPLETE] Ancestor node '{}' did NOT conclude successfully — best belief {:.2}. Treat this dependency as unresolved.", node.objective, best_belief)
            }
            Err(e) => {
                mission_had_failures = true;
                let _ = ctx.tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "LOG_EVENT", "payload": format!("[CORTEX FRACTURE] Node {} loop failed: {}", task_id, e)}).to_string());
                format!("[DAG NODE FAILED] Ancestor node '{}' failed: {}", node.objective, e)
            }
        };

        task_outputs.insert(task_id.clone(), execution_evidence);
    }

    if mission_had_failures {
        format!("DAG/GoT Execution FINISHED WITH INCOMPLETE NODES. Plan ID: {}", plan_id)
    } else {
        format!("DAG/GoT Hybrid Execution Complete. Plan ID: {}", plan_id)
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/cortical_loop.rs 
=========================================
use anyhow::Result;
use reqwest::Client;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{Mutex, broadcast};
use tokio::time::{sleep, Duration};

use crate::cognition_graph::{CognitiveGraph, NodeKind, EdgeRelation};
use crate::world_model::WorldModel;
use crate::epistemic_engine::EpistemicEngine;
use crate::executor::RoboticExecutor;
use crate::agent_protocol::AgentAction;
use crate::shared_state::VmoState;
use crate::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler;
use crate::memory_cortex::MemoryCortex;

#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum CortexOutcome {
    Proven { belief: f32 },
    TurnsExhausted { best_belief: f32 },
}

pub struct CorticalLoop {
    client: Client,
    api_key: String,
    graph: CognitiveGraph,
    world_model: Arc<WorldModel>,
    memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>,
    vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>,
    dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
    tx: broadcast::Sender<String>,
}

impl CorticalLoop {
    pub fn new(
        api_key: String, world_model: Arc<WorldModel>, memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>,
        vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>, dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>, tx: broadcast::Sender<String>,
    ) -> Self {
        Self { client: Client::new(), api_key, graph: CognitiveGraph::new(), world_model, memory, vmo_state, dist, tx }
    }

    fn log(&self, msg: String) {
        let _ = self.tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "NEURAL_STREAM", "payload": format!("{}\n", msg)}).to_string());
    }

    pub async fn ignite(&mut self, mission: &str) -> Result<CortexOutcome> {
        self.log("\x1b[1;36m>> [CORTEX] Igniting Unified Epistemic Engine\x1b[0m".to_string());
        
        let context_chunks = (*self.world_model).semantic_query(mission).await;
        let world_context = context_chunks.join("\n");

        self.log("\x1b[36m>> [CORTEX] Generating competing strategies...\x1b[0m".to_string());
        let hypotheses = EpistemicEngine::generate_hypotheses(&self.client, &self.api_key, mission, &world_context).await?;

        for h in hypotheses {
            self.graph.add_node(NodeKind::Hypothesis, &h, 0.5, 100);
            self.log(format!("\x1b[90m  -> Strategy: {}\x1b[0m", h));
        }

        let mut max_observed_belief = 0.0_f32;

        for turn in 1..=15 {
            self.log(format!("\n\x1b[1;33m>> [CORTEX] Cognitive Turn {} / 15\x1b[0m", turn));

            if let Some(best_node) = self.graph.select_best_hypothesis().cloned() {
                let h_id = best_node.id;
                let h_content = best_node.content.clone();
                let current_belief = best_node.belief_score;
                if current_belief > max_observed_belief { max_observed_belief = current_belief; }
                self.log(format!("\x1b[32m>> [CORTEX] Exploring Top Strategy (Belief: {:.2}):\x1b[0m {}", current_belief, h_content));

                let mut evidence_history = String::new();
                for edge in &self.graph.edges {
                    if edge.target == h_id && (edge.relation == EdgeRelation::Supports || edge.relation == EdgeRelation::Contradicts) {
                        if let Some(obs) = self.graph.nodes.get(&edge.source) {
                            evidence_history.push_str(&format!("- [{:?}] {}\n", edge.relation, obs.content));
                        }
                    }
                }
                if evidence_history.is_empty() { evidence_history = "None yet.".to_string(); }

                self.log("\x1b[36m>> [STRATEGIST] Planning next maneuver...\x1b[0m".to_string());
                let proposed_action = match EpistemicEngine::propose_action(&self.client, &self.api_key, mission, &h_content, &evidence_history).await {
                    Ok(action) => action,
                    Err(e) => { self.log(format!("\x1b[31m>> [STRATEGIST FAULT]\x1b[0m {}", e)); continue; }
                };
                self.log(format!("\x1b[35m>> [STRATEGIST PROPOSAL]:\x1b[0m {}", proposed_action));

                self.log("\x1b[31m>> [RED TEAM] Criticizing proposal...\x1b[0m".to_string());
                if let Ok(critic) = EpistemicEngine::criticize_proposal(&self.client, &self.api_key, &h_content, &proposed_action, &world_context, &evidence_history).await {
                    self.log(format!("\x1b[31m>> [CRITIC] Verdict:\x1b[0m {} (Modifier: {:.2})", critic.critique, critic.confidence_modifier));
                    
                    if critic.confidence_modifier > -0.2 {
                        self.log("\x1b[36m>> [CORTEX] Translating intent to physical action...\x1b[0m".to_string());
                        
                        if let Ok(raw_json) = EpistemicEngine::translate_to_action(&self.client, &self.api_key, &h_content, &proposed_action).await {
                            if let Ok(actions) = crate::json_parser::extract_actions(&raw_json) {
                                let mut executor = RoboticExecutor::new(self.api_key.clone(), self.tx.clone());
                                
                                for action in actions {
                                    match &action {
                                        AgentAction::PatchFunction { path, .. } | AgentAction::DiffPatch { path, .. } | AgentAction::WriteFile { path, .. } => {
                                            self.log(format!("\x1b[35m>> [LAZARUS] Taking atomic snapshot of {}...\x1b[0m", path));
                                            let _ = crate::lazarus_vault::take_snapshot(path, "Pre-fix snapshot").await;
                                        },
                                        _ => {}
                                    }

                                    self.log("\x1b[35m>> [EXECUTOR] Engaging physical tooling...\x1b[0m".to_string());
                                    let evidence = match executor.execute_single_action(action, self.memory.clone(), self.vmo_state.clone(), self.dist.clone()).await {
                                        Ok(ev) => ev,
                                        Err(e) => format!("[ACTION FAILED] OS rejected action: {}", e),
                                    };

                                    let preview = evidence.lines().take(5).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
                                    self.log(format!("\x1b[32m>> [EVIDENCE ACQUIRED]\x1b[0m\n{}", preview));
                                    
                                    self.log("\x1b[33m>> [CORTEX] Evaluating physical evidence against strategy...\x1b[0m".to_string());
                                    if let Ok(eval) = EpistemicEngine::evaluate_evidence(&self.client, &self.api_key, &h_content, &evidence).await {
                                        self.log(format!("\x1b[33m>> [EVALUATION]\x1b[0m {} (Modifier: {:.2})", eval.critique, eval.confidence_modifier));
                                        
                                        let obs_id = self.graph.add_node(NodeKind::Observation, &evidence, 1.0, 50);
                                        let relation = if eval.confidence_modifier > 0.0 { EdgeRelation::Supports } else { EdgeRelation::Contradicts };
                                        self.graph.add_edge(obs_id, h_id, relation, eval.confidence_modifier.abs());
                                        
                                        self.graph.propagate_beliefs();
                                        
                                        if let Some(updated_node) = self.graph.nodes.get(&h_id) {
                                            self.log(format!("\x1b[1;32m>> [CORTEX] Strategy Belief mathematically updated to {:.2}\x1b[0m", updated_node.belief_score));
                                            if updated_node.belief_score > max_observed_belief { max_observed_belief = updated_node.belief_score; }
                                            
                                            let is_mutation = evidence.contains("[WRITE_OK]") || evidence.contains("[PATCH_FUNCTION_OK]") || evidence.contains("[DIFF_PATCH_OK]");
                                            let is_terminal_cmd = evidence.contains("[CMD_OK]") && !proposed_action.contains("grep") && !proposed_action.contains("ls") && !proposed_action.contains("cat") && !proposed_action.contains("find");
                                            let is_halt = evidence.contains("[HALT]");

                                            if updated_node.belief_score >= 0.85 && (is_mutation || is_terminal_cmd || is_halt) {
                                                self.log("\x1b[1;36m>> [CORTEX] STRATEGY PROVEN AND FIX APPLIED! Mission Accomplished.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                                                return Ok(CortexOutcome::Proven { belief: updated_node.belief_score });
                                            }
                                            
                                            if updated_node.belief_score < 0.2 {
                                                self.log("\x1b[90m>> [CORTEX] Strategy completely abandoned. Pivoting graph...\x1b[0m".to_string());
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            } else {
                                self.log("\x1b[31m>> [CORTEX FAULT] Failed to parse AgentAction JSON from Translator.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                            }
                        }
                    } else {
                        self.log("\x1b[31m>> [RED TEAM] VETO. Proposal rejected. Applying penalty to strategy.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                        self.graph.add_edge(h_id, h_id, EdgeRelation::Contradicts, critic.confidence_modifier.abs());
                        self.graph.propagate_beliefs();
                    }
                }
            } else {
                self.log("\x1b[1;35m>> [CORTEX] All strategies exhausted. Re-orienting graph...\x1b[0m".to_string());
                let new_hypotheses = EpistemicEngine::generate_hypotheses(&self.client, &self.api_key, mission, &world_context).await;
                for h in new_hypotheses.unwrap_or_default() {
                    self.graph.add_node(NodeKind::Hypothesis, &h, 0.5, 100);
                    self.log(format!("\x1b[90m  -> New Strategy: {}\x1b[0m", h));
                }
            }
            sleep(Duration::from_secs(2)).await;
        }
        self.log("\x1b[1;32m>> [CORTEX] Epistemic Execution Cycle Complete.\x1b[0m".to_string());
        Ok(CortexOutcome::TurnsExhausted { best_belief: max_observed_belief })
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/evolution_engine.rs 
=========================================
use crate::fitness_evaluator::FitnessEvaluator;
use crate::memory_cortex::MemoryCortex;
use crate::wasm_forge::WasmForge;
use anyhow::Result;
use base64::{engine::general_purpose, Engine as _};
use nexus_substrate::vector_bridge;
use reqwest::Client;
use std::sync::Arc;

pub struct EvolutionEngine;

impl EvolutionEngine {
    pub async fn evolve_and_memorize(
        _client: &Client,
        api_key: &str,
        memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>,
        objective: &str,
        candidate_code: &str,
        test_payload: &[u8],
        func_name: &str,
    ) -> Result<f32> {
        tracing::info!(
            "\x1b[35m>> [EVOLUTION] Synthesizing candidate genome for:\x1b[0m {}",
            objective
        );

        // 1. Synthesize the candidate code into a WASM cell
        let wasm_b64 = WasmForge::synthesize_cell(candidate_code).await?;
        let wasm_bytes = general_purpose::STANDARD.decode(&wasm_b64)?;

        tracing::info!("\x1b[36m>> [EVOLUTION] Candidate compiled. Initiating physical fitness benchmark...\x1b[0m");

        // 2. Evaluate physical fitness (Speed, Memory, Thermal)
        let report = FitnessEvaluator::evaluate_wasm_cell(&wasm_bytes, func_name, test_payload)?;

        tracing::info!(
            "\x1b[33m>> [EVOLUTION] Composite Fitness Score: {:.2}\x1b[0m ({}ms latency, {:.2}KB mem, {:.2}C temp delta)",
            report.composite_score, report.execution_time_ms, report.memory_delta_kb, report.thermal_impact
        );

        // 3. Selection: Only memorize if it meets a high fitness threshold
        if report.composite_score > 100.0 {
            tracing::info!("\x1b[1;32m>> [EVOLUTION] High-Fitness Pattern Detected! Extracting semantic embedding...\x1b[0m");

            // Embed the context
            let embedding_text = format!("OBJECTIVE: {}\nCODE:\n{}", objective, candidate_code);
            let embedding = vector_bridge::get_embedding(api_key, &embedding_text).await.ok();

            let insight = format!(
                "[HIGH_FITNESS_PATTERN] Score: {:.2} | Algorithm successfully optimized for ARM64 constraints. \nCODE_SNIPPET:\n{}",
                report.composite_score,
                &candidate_code[..candidate_code.len().min(500)]
            );

            // Store in Memory Palace
            let mem = memory;
            mem.store_memory("EvolutionEngine", objective, &insight, embedding).await?;

            tracing::info!("\x1b[1;36m>> [EVOLUTION] Genetic material successfully committed to VectorVault.\x1b[0m");
        } else {
            tracing::warn!("\x1b[90m>> [EVOLUTION] Candidate failed to meet fitness threshold. Discarding genome.\x1b[0m");
        }

        Ok(report.composite_score)
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/decision_dispatcher.rs

use anyhow::Result;
use nexus_executive_cortex::{DecisionSink, schema::{Decision, ExecutiveIntent}};

/// Translates pure Executive Decisions into concrete physical side-effects.
#[derive(Default)]
pub struct RuntimeDispatcher;

impl RuntimeDispatcher {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self
    }
}

impl DecisionSink for RuntimeDispatcher {
    fn dispatch(&self, decision: Decision) -> Result<()> {
        // Log the pure decision intent before applying physical side-effects
        println!(
            "\x1b[35m>> [DISPATCHER] Policy evaluating intent: {:?} | Confidence: {:.2}\x1b[0m",
            decision.intent,
            decision.confidence
        );
        println!("\x1b[90m   Reason: {}\x1b[0m", decision.reason);
        
        // The Policy Routing Table
        match decision.intent {
            ExecutiveIntent::RequestPlanning => {
                println!("\x1b[32m   -> Action: Spawning DAG Planner for new directive.\x1b[0m");
                // Integration Point: Orchestrator DAG invocation will go here.
            }
            ExecutiveIntent::CompleteGoal => {
                println!("\x1b[32m   -> Action: Archiving Goal to Memory Cortex and updating Telemetry.\x1b[0m");
                // Integration Point: MemoryActor & Telemetry sync goes here.
            }
            ExecutiveIntent::Escalate => {
                println!("\x1b[33m   -> Action: System health critical! Escalating to Human Operator.\x1b[0m");
                // Integration Point: UI WebSocket alert goes here.
            }
            ExecutiveIntent::Abort => {
                println!("\x1b[31m   -> Action: Irrecoverable failure. Safely freezing the DAG.\x1b[0m");
                // Integration Point: Graceful soft-halt mechanism goes here.
            }
            ExecutiveIntent::Continue => {
                println!("\x1b[36m   -> Action: No execution required. Proceeding nominally.\x1b[0m");
            }
            _ => {
                println!("\x1b[90m   -> Action: Policy for {:?} is stubbed for future implementation.\x1b[0m", decision.intent);
            }
        }
        
        Ok(())
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::observation_adapter::ObservationAdapter;
    use nexus_executive_cortex::ExecutiveCortex;

    #[test]
    fn test_pure_cortex_pipeline() {
        println!("\n\x1b[1m--- INITIATING CORTEX PIPELINE SIMULATION ---\x1b[0m");

        // 1. Boot the pure state machine and the side-effect dispatcher
        let mut cortex = ExecutiveCortex::new();
        let dispatcher = RuntimeDispatcher::new();

        // 2. The Real World happens (A user types a command)
        println!("\x1b[90m[Step 1] Real world event: User typed a directive.\x1b[0m");
        let envelope = ObservationAdapter::from_human_directive("Refactor the memory bus architecture.");
        
        // 3. The Cortex evaluates the observation (Zero side-effects)
        println!("\x1b[90m[Step 2] Cortex processing Observation Envelope (ID: {})...\x1b[0m", envelope.id);
        let decision = cortex.receive(envelope);

        // 4. The Dispatcher physically acts on the pure decision
        println!("\x1b[90m[Step 3] Routing Decision to Dispatcher...\x1b[0m");
        dispatcher.dispatch(decision).unwrap();
        
        println!("\x1b[1m--- SIMULATION COMPLETE ---\x1b[0m\n");
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/executor.rs 
=========================================
use crate::agent_protocol::AgentAction;
use crate::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler;
use crate::memory_cortex::MemoryCortex;
use crate::shared_state::VmoState;
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::Mutex;

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Clone)]
pub enum FsmState {
    Idle,
    Executing,
    Complete,
    Fault,
}

pub struct RoboticExecutor {
    pub current_state: FsmState,
    pub api_key: String,
    tx: tokio::sync::broadcast::Sender<String>,
}

pub fn sanitize(path: &str) -> Result<PathBuf> {
    // ⚡ Allow /tmp/ prefix, block all other absolute paths
    if (path.starts_with('/') && !path.starts_with("/tmp/")) || path.contains("..") {
        return Err(anyhow!("PATH_TRAVERSAL_DENIED: {}", path));
    }

    let p = Path::new(path);
    if (p.is_absolute() && !path.starts_with("/tmp/"))
        || p.components().any(|c| matches!(c, std::path::Component::ParentDir))
    {
        return Err(anyhow!("PATH_TRAVERSAL_DENIED: {}", path));
    }
    Ok(p.to_path_buf())
}

impl RoboticExecutor {
    pub fn new(api_key: String, tx: tokio::sync::broadcast::Sender<String>) -> Self {
        Self { current_state: FsmState::Idle, api_key, tx }
    }

    pub async fn execute_handle(
        &mut self,
        actions: Vec<AgentAction>,
        memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>,
        vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>,
        dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
    ) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
        self.current_state = FsmState::Executing;
        let mut observations = Vec::new();

        for action in actions {
            match self
                .execute_single_action(action, memory.clone(), vmo_state.clone(), dist.clone())
                .await
            {
                Ok(obs) => {
                    let summary = obs.lines().next().unwrap_or("[OK]");
                    let _ = self.tx.send(
                        serde_json::json!({
                            "type": "LOG_EVENT",
                            "payload": format!("[EXECUTOR] Action executed: {}", summary)
                        })
                        .to_string(),
                    );
                    observations.push(obs);
                }
                Err(e) => {
                    observations.push(format!("ACTION_FAILED: {}", e));
                    self.current_state = FsmState::Fault;
                    return Ok(observations);
                }
            }
        }
        self.current_state = FsmState::Complete;
        Ok(observations)
    }

    pub async fn execute_single_action(
        &mut self,
        action: AgentAction,
        _memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>,
        _vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>,
        _dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
    ) -> Result<String> {
        let ledger_action = action.clone();
        let result: Result<String> = async {
        match action {
            AgentAction::ExecuteCommand { command, .. } => {
                let out = crate::podman::PodmanShield::execute(&command)?;
                let mut stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).into_owned();
                let mut stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).into_owned();

                // ⚡ DEFENSE IN DEPTH 1: Truncate raw command output at the source
                if stdout.len() > 8000 { stdout.truncate(8000); stdout.push_str("
...[STDOUT TRUNCATED: OUTPUT TOO LARGE]"); }
                if stderr.len() > 8000 { stderr.truncate(8000); stderr.push_str("
...[STDERR TRUNCATED: OUTPUT TOO LARGE]"); }
                let mut res = format!("[CMD_OK] Status: {}\n", out.status);
                if !stdout.trim().is_empty() { res.push_str(&format!("STDOUT:\n{}\n", stdout)); }
                if !stderr.trim().is_empty() { res.push_str(&format!("STDERR:\n{}\n", stderr)); }
                if stdout.trim().is_empty() && stderr.trim().is_empty() { res.push_str("(Command produced no output)\n"); }
                Ok(res)
            }
            AgentAction::WriteFile { path, content, .. } => {
                let safe_path = sanitize(&path)?;
                tokio::fs::write(&safe_path, content).await?;
                Ok(format!("[WRITE_OK] {}", path))
            }
            AgentAction::ReadFile { path, .. } => {
                let safe_path = sanitize(&path)?;
                let content = tokio::fs::read_to_string(&safe_path).await?;

                // ⚡ SOVEREIGN RAG: Vectorize and route through Memory Cortex
                // Use a 8k token safe-chunk for the embedding API
                let snippet = if content.len() > 8000 { &content[..8000] } else { &content };
                let mem = _memory;
                if let Ok(emb) = nexus_substrate::vector_bridge::get_embedding(&self.api_key, snippet).await {
                    let _ = mem.store_memory("ReadFile", &path, &content, Some(emb)).await;
                } else {
                    let _ = mem.store_memory("ReadFile", &path, &content, None).await;
                }

                // Return a truncated preview to the immediate GoT loop to prevent context overflow
                let return_content = if content.len() > 1500 {
                    format!("{}...\n\n[CONTENT TRUNCATED. FULL DATA SAVED TO MEMORY PALACE VECTOR VAULT. USE search_memory TO EXPLORE DEEPER.]", &content[..1500])
                } else {
                    content.clone()
                };

                Ok(format!("[READ_OK] {}\nCONTENT PREVIEW:\n{}", path, return_content))
            }
            AgentAction::ReadFunction { path, function_name, .. } => {
                let safe_path = sanitize(&path)?;
                let content = tokio::fs::read_to_string(&safe_path).await?;
                Ok(format!("[READ_FUNCTION_OK] '{}' from {}\nCONTENT:\n{}", function_name, path, content))
            }
            AgentAction::DiffPatch { path, search, replace, .. } => {
                let safe_path = sanitize(&path)?;
                let original = tokio::fs::read_to_string(&safe_path).await?;
                let patched = crate::diff::DiffEngine::apply(&original, &search, &replace)?;
                tokio::fs::write(&safe_path, patched).await?;
                Ok(format!("[DIFF_PATCH_OK] Applied changes to {}", path))
            }
            AgentAction::PatchFunction { path, function_name, new_body, .. } => {
                let safe_path = sanitize(&path)?;
                let patched = crate::ast_editor::AstEditor::update_function(safe_path.to_str().unwrap_or(&path), &function_name, &new_body)?;
                tokio::fs::write(&safe_path, patched).await?;
                Ok(format!("[PATCH_FUNCTION_OK] Updated function '{}' in {}", function_name, path))
            }
            AgentAction::SearchMemory { query, .. } => {
                let mem = _memory;
                let mut results_text = String::new();

                // 1. Semantic Vector Search
                if let Ok(emb) = nexus_substrate::vector_bridge::get_embedding(&self.api_key, &query).await {
                    if let Ok(results) = mem.semantic_search(&emb).await {
                        for r in results {
                            let snippet = if r.result.len() > 3000 { &r.result[..3000] } else { &r.result };
                            results_text.push_str(&format!("Source: {}\nData: {}...\n---\n", r.directive, snippet));
                        }
                    }
                }

                // 2. Fallback to FTS5 Lexical Search if Vector fails or is empty
                if results_text.is_empty() {
                    if let Ok(results) = mem.search_memories(&query).await {
                        for r in results {
                            let snippet = if r.result.len() > 3000 { &r.result[..3000] } else { &r.result };
                            results_text.push_str(&format!("Source: {}\nData: {}...\n---\n", r.directive, snippet));
                        }
                    }
                }

                if results_text.is_empty() {
                    Ok(format!("[SEARCH_OK] No memories found in VectorVault for: {}", query))
                } else {
                    Ok(format!("[SEARCH_OK] Found semantic context:\n{}", results_text))
                }
            }
            AgentAction::VmoCreate { size, .. } => {
                let handle = nexus_substrate::kernel::safe_kernel::SafeKernel::create_vmo(size)
                    .map_err(|e| anyhow!("VMO Allocation Fault: {:?}", e))?;
                Ok(format!("[VMO_CREATED] Handle: {}", handle))
            }
            AgentAction::CalculateEntropy { handle, .. } => {
                let h_val = handle.parse::<u32>().unwrap_or(0);
                let entropy = nexus_substrate::kernel::calculate_entropy(h_val);
                Ok(format!("[ENTROPY] Handle {}: {:.4} Shannon bits", handle, entropy))
            }
            AgentAction::LazarusSnapshot { path, tag, .. } => {
                let safe_path = sanitize(&path)?;
                let directive = tag.unwrap_or_else(|| "Manual Snapshot".into());
                let snap = crate::lazarus_vault::take_snapshot(safe_path.to_str().unwrap(), &directive).await?;
                Ok(format!("[SNAPSHOT_OK] ID: {} Hash: {}", snap.id, snap.hash))
            }
            AgentAction::ExportReport { title, content, .. } => {
                let file_name = format!("{}.md", title.replace(" ", "_").to_lowercase());
                let safe_path = sanitize(&file_name)?;
                tokio::fs::write(&safe_path, content).await?;
                Ok(format!("[REPORT_EXPORTED] {}", file_name))
            }
            AgentAction::FetchUiSnapshot { .. } => {
                let content = tokio::fs::read_to_string("ui/src/App.svelte").await.unwrap_or_else(|_| "UI not found".into());
                Ok(format!("[UI_SNAPSHOT] Fetched {} bytes from App.svelte", content.len()))
            }
            AgentAction::ScrapeUrl { url, .. } => {
                let client = reqwest::Client::new();
                let res = client.post("http://127.0.0.1:5000/scrape")
                    .json(&serde_json::json!({ "url": url }))
                    .send()
                    .await?;
                let text = res.text().await?;
                Ok(format!("[URL_SCRAPED] Output: {}", text))
            }
            AgentAction::GitRollback { reasoning } => {
                let out = crate::podman::PodmanShield::execute("git reset --hard HEAD")?;
                Ok(format!("[GIT_ROLLBACK_OK] Reason: {}\nSTDOUT:\n{}", reasoning, String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout)))
            }
            AgentAction::SpawnSubagent { instruction, .. } | AgentAction::DelegateTask { instruction, .. } => {
                let mem = _memory;
                mem.store_memory("Executor", &instruction, "Delegated to Subagent", None).await?;
                Ok("[DELEGATION_LOGGED] Instruction pushed to Memory Palace for async execution.".to_string())
            }
            AgentAction::Halt { reasoning } => Ok(format!("[HALT] {}", reasoning)),
        }
        }.await;

        if result.is_ok() {
            crate::governance::protocol::GOVERNANCE
                .lock()
                .await
                .record(ledger_action, "robotic_executor");
        }

        result
    }
}

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum MemoryKind {
    Observation,
    Experience,
    Goal,
    Reflection,
    Knowledge,
    Failure,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct CognitiveMemory {
    pub id: Uuid,

    pub kind: MemoryKind,

    pub key: String,

    pub content: String,

    pub source: String,

    pub confidence: f32,

    pub importance: f32,

    pub created_at: i64,

    pub tags: Vec<String>,
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/memory_cortex.rs

use crate::memory_types::CognitiveMemory;
use std::sync::Arc;

use anyhow::Result;
use tokio::sync::Mutex;

use nexus_substrate::memory::{AgentMemory, MemoryPalace};

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct MemoryCortex {
    palace: Arc<Mutex<MemoryPalace>>,
}

impl MemoryCortex {
    pub fn new(palace: Arc<Mutex<MemoryPalace>>) -> Self {
        Self { palace }
    }

    pub async fn store_memory(
        &self,
        category: &str,
        key: &str,
        value: &str,
        embedding: Option<Vec<f32>>,
    ) -> Result<()> {
        let mem = self.palace.lock().await;

        mem.store_memory(category, key, value, embedding).await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn search_memories(&self, query: &str) -> Result<Vec<AgentMemory>> {
        let mem = self.palace.lock().await;

        let result = mem.search_memories(query).await?;

        Ok(result)
    }

    pub async fn semantic_search(&self, embedding: &[f32]) -> Result<Vec<AgentMemory>> {
        let mem = self.palace.lock().await;

        let result = mem.semantic_search(embedding).await?;

        Ok(result)
    }

    pub async fn remember(&self, memory: CognitiveMemory) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        let mem = self.palace.lock().await;

        mem.store_memory("cognitive", &memory.key, &memory.content, None).await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn upsert_plan_run(
        &self,
        id: &str,
        objective: &str,
        graph: &str,
        status: &str,
        created_at: i64,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        let mem = self.palace.lock().await;

        mem.upsert_plan_run(id, objective, graph, status, created_at).await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn consolidate_memory(
        &self,
        memory: crate::memory_types::CognitiveMemory,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<bool> {
        let score = crate::memory_consolidation::MemoryConsolidator::calculate_retention(&memory);

        if score < 0.1 {
            return Ok(false);
        }

        self.remember(memory).await?;

        Ok(true)
    }

    pub async fn reflect_and_store(
        &self,
        memories: Vec<crate::memory_types::CognitiveMemory>,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<bool> {
        let engine = crate::reflection_engine::ReflectionEngine::new();

        if let Some(reflection) = engine.reflect(&memories) {
            self.remember(reflection).await?;

            return Ok(true);
        }

        Ok(false)
    }

    pub async fn recall(
        &self,
        context: &str,
        limit: usize,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<crate::memory_recall::RecallResult> {
        let memories = self
            .search_memories(context)
            .await?
            .into_iter()
            .take(limit)
            .map(|m| crate::memory_types::CognitiveMemory {
                id: uuid::Uuid::new_v4(),
                kind: crate::memory_types::MemoryKind::Experience,
                key: m.directive.clone(),
                content: m.result.clone(),
                source: m.origin.clone(),
                confidence: 0.5,
                importance: 0.5,
                created_at: m
                    .timestamp
                    .parse::<i64>()
                    .unwrap_or_else(|_| chrono::Utc::now().timestamp()),
                tags: vec![m.origin.clone()],
            })
            .collect();

        let engine = crate::memory_recall::MemoryRecallEngine::new();

        Ok(engine.rank(memories, context))
    }

    pub async fn store_belief_update(
        &self,
        event: crate::belief_feedback::BeliefUpdateEvent,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        let payload = serde_json::to_string(&event)?;

        self.store_memory("belief_update", &event.belief_id.to_string(), &payload, None).await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn store_belief_decision(
        &self,
        decision: crate::belief_policy::BeliefDecision,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        let payload = serde_json::to_string(&decision)?;

        self.store_memory("belief_decision", &decision.belief_id.to_string(), &payload, None)
            .await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn store_goal_adaptation(
        &self,
        decision: crate::goal_adaptation::GoalAdaptationDecision,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        let payload = serde_json::to_string(&decision)?;

        self.store_memory("goal_adaptation", &decision.goal_id.to_string(), &payload, None).await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn health_check(&self) -> anyhow::Result<bool> {
        let _mem = self.palace.lock().await;

        Ok(true)
    }

    pub async fn emit_belief_update(
        &self,
        belief_id: &str,
        evidence: &str,
        confidence_delta: f32,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<crate::belief_update::BeliefUpdate> {
        Ok(crate::belief_update::BeliefUpdate::new(belief_id, evidence, confidence_delta))
    }
}

impl MemoryCortex {
    pub async fn store_semantic_memory(
        &self,
        memory: crate::memory_semantic::SemanticMemory,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        let content = serde_json::to_string(&memory)?;

        self.store_memory("semantic", &memory.lesson, &content, None).await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn recall_semantic(
        &self,
        category: &str,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<nexus_substrate::memory::AgentMemory>> {
        self.search_memories(category).await
    }
}

impl MemoryCortex {
    pub async fn store_belief_transition(
        &self,
        event: crate::belief_transition::BeliefTransitionEvent,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        let memory = crate::memory_types::CognitiveMemory {
            id: uuid::Uuid::new_v4(),

            kind: crate::memory_types::MemoryKind::Reflection,

            key: format!("belief_transition_{}", event.belief_id),

            content: event.lesson,

            source: "belief_engine".to_string(),

            confidence: event.new_confidence,

            importance: 0.9,

            created_at: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),

            tags: vec!["belief".to_string(), "reflection".to_string(), "adaptation".to_string()],
        };

        self.remember(memory).await?;

        Ok(())
    }
}

impl MemoryCortex {
    pub async fn store_belief_cause(
        &self,
        cause: crate::belief_causality::BeliefCause,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        let memory = crate::memory_types::CognitiveMemory {
            id: uuid::Uuid::new_v4(),

            kind: crate::memory_types::MemoryKind::Reflection,

            key: format!("belief_cause_{}", cause.id),

            content: format!("BELIEF CAUSE: {} | LESSON: {}", cause.cause, cause.lesson),

            source: "belief_causality_engine".to_string(),

            confidence: cause.new_confidence,

            importance: 0.95,

            created_at: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),

            tags: vec![
                "belief".to_string(),
                "causal_memory".to_string(),
                "reflection".to_string(),
                "adaptation".to_string(),
            ],
        };

        self.remember(memory).await?;

        Ok(())
    }
}

impl MemoryCortex {
    pub async fn recall_causal_pattern(
        &self,
        observation: String,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<crate::causal_recall::CausalRecall>> {
        let mut results = Vec::new();

        if observation.to_lowercase().contains("memory") {
            results.push(crate::causal_recall::CausalRecall {
                pattern: "memory".to_string(),

                previous_cause: "resource exhaustion".to_string(),

                lesson: "Use resource-aware routing before execution".to_string(),

                confidence: 0.95,
            });
        }

        Ok(results)
    }
}

impl MemoryCortex {
    pub async fn store_validation_event(
        &self,
        event: crate::belief_validation::BeliefValidationEvent,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        let memory = crate::memory_types::CognitiveMemory {
            id: uuid::Uuid::new_v4(),

            kind: crate::memory_types::MemoryKind::Reflection,

            key: format!("belief_validation_{}", uuid::Uuid::new_v4()),

            content: event.lesson,

            source: "belief_validation_engine".to_string(),

            confidence: 1.0 - event.error_score,

            importance: 0.95,

            created_at: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),

            tags: vec![
                "belief".into(),
                "prediction".into(),
                "validation".into(),
                "causal_learning".into(),
            ],
        };

        self.remember(memory).await?;

        Ok(())
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/memory_consolidation.rs 
=========================================
use crate::memory_types::{CognitiveMemory, MemoryKind};
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

pub struct MemoryConsolidator;

impl MemoryConsolidator {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self
    }

    pub fn calculate_retention(memory: &CognitiveMemory) -> f32 {
        let now = SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap().as_secs() as i64;

        let age = (now - memory.created_at).max(0) as f32;

        let decay = (-age / 86400.0).exp();

        let score = memory.importance * memory.confidence * decay;

        score.clamp(0.0, 1.0)
    }

    pub fn should_consolidate(memory: &CognitiveMemory) -> bool {
        match memory.kind {
            MemoryKind::Failure | MemoryKind::Reflection | MemoryKind::Goal => true,

            _ => Self::calculate_retention(memory) > 0.5,
        }
    }
}

impl Default for MemoryConsolidator {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/reflection_engine.rs 
=========================================
use crate::memory_types::{CognitiveMemory, MemoryKind};

use uuid::Uuid;

pub struct ReflectionEngine;

impl ReflectionEngine {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self
    }

    pub fn reflect(&self, memories: &[CognitiveMemory]) -> Option<CognitiveMemory> {
        if memories.is_empty() {
            return None;
        }

        let mut lesson = String::from("Reflection summary:\n");

        for memory in memories.iter().take(5) {
            lesson.push_str(&format!("- {}\n", memory.content));
        }

        Some(CognitiveMemory {
            id: Uuid::new_v4(),

            kind: MemoryKind::Reflection,

            key: format!("reflection_{}", Uuid::new_v4()),

            content: lesson,

            source: "ReflectionEngine".into(),

            confidence: 0.7,

            importance: 0.8,

            created_at: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),

            tags: vec!["reflection".into(), "lesson".into()],
        })
    }
}

impl Default for ReflectionEngine {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

pub fn classify_lesson(lesson: &str) -> crate::memory_semantic::SemanticMemory {
    let category = if lesson.contains("RAM") || lesson.contains("memory") {
        crate::memory_semantic::SemanticCategory::HardwareConstraint
    } else if lesson.contains("failed") {
        crate::memory_semantic::SemanticCategory::FailurePattern
    } else {
        crate::memory_semantic::SemanticCategory::GeneralKnowledge
    };

    crate::memory_semantic::SemanticMemory::new(category, lesson, 0.8)
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/belief_engine.rs 
=========================================
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct BeliefState {
    pub id: Uuid,

    pub proposition: String,

    pub confidence: f32,

    pub evidence_count: usize,

    pub stability: f32,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct BeliefEvidence {
    pub source: String,

    pub observation: String,

    pub reliability: f32,
}

pub struct BeliefEngine;

impl BeliefEngine {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self
    }

    pub fn update(&self, current: Option<BeliefState>, evidence: BeliefEvidence) -> BeliefState {
        match current {
            Some(mut belief) => {
                let adjustment = evidence.reliability * 0.1;

                belief.confidence = (belief.confidence + adjustment).min(1.0);

                belief.evidence_count += 1;

                belief.stability = (belief.stability + 0.05).min(1.0);

                belief
            }

            None => BeliefState {
                id: Uuid::new_v4(),

                proposition: evidence.observation,

                confidence: evidence.reliability,

                evidence_count: 1,

                stability: 0.5,
            },
        }
    }
}

impl Default for BeliefEngine {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl BeliefState {
    pub fn update_confidence(&mut self, delta: f32) {
        self.confidence += delta;

        self.confidence = self.confidence.clamp(0.0, 1.0);
    }
}

impl BeliefState {
    pub fn apply_delta(&mut self, delta: crate::belief_delta::BeliefDelta) {
        self.confidence = delta.new_confidence;
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/belief_policy.rs 
=========================================
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum BeliefAction {
    Continue,
    Adapt,
    Suspend,
    Abandon,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct BeliefDecision {
    pub id: Uuid,

    pub belief_id: Uuid,

    pub confidence: f32,

    pub action: BeliefAction,

    pub reason: String,

    pub timestamp: i64,
}

pub struct BeliefDecisionPolicy;

impl BeliefDecisionPolicy {
    pub fn evaluate(belief_id: Uuid, confidence: f32) -> BeliefDecision {
        let action;

        let reason;

        if confidence >= 0.75 {
            action = BeliefAction::Continue;
            reason = "Confidence sufficient to continue objective".to_string();
        } else if confidence >= 0.45 {
            action = BeliefAction::Adapt;
            reason = "Confidence degraded, strategy adjustment recommended".to_string();
        } else if confidence >= 0.20 {
            action = BeliefAction::Suspend;
            reason = "Confidence too low, suspend objective".to_string();
        } else {
            action = BeliefAction::Abandon;
            reason = "Evidence no longer supports objective".to_string();
        }

        Self::decision(belief_id, confidence, action, reason)
    }

    fn decision(
        belief_id: Uuid,
        confidence: f32,
        action: BeliefAction,
        reason: String,
    ) -> BeliefDecision {
        BeliefDecision {
            id: Uuid::new_v4(),

            belief_id,

            confidence,

            action,

            reason,

            timestamp: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),
        }
    }
}

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum GoalAdaptationAction {
    ContinueGoal,

    ModifyStrategy,

    PauseGoal,

    TerminateGoal,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct GoalAdaptationDecision {
    pub id: Uuid,

    pub goal_id: Uuid,

    pub action: GoalAdaptationAction,

    pub reason: String,

    pub timestamp: i64,
}

pub struct GoalAdaptationEngine;

impl GoalAdaptationEngine {
    pub fn from_belief_action(
        goal_id: Uuid,
        action: crate::belief_policy::BeliefAction,
    ) -> GoalAdaptationDecision {
        let (adaptation, reason) = match action {
            crate::belief_policy::BeliefAction::Continue => {
                (GoalAdaptationAction::ContinueGoal, "Belief confidence supports continuation")
            }

            crate::belief_policy::BeliefAction::Adapt => (
                GoalAdaptationAction::ModifyStrategy,
                "Belief degradation requires strategy adaptation",
            ),

            crate::belief_policy::BeliefAction::Suspend => (
                GoalAdaptationAction::PauseGoal,
                "Belief confidence insufficient, pausing objective",
            ),

            crate::belief_policy::BeliefAction::Abandon => {
                (GoalAdaptationAction::TerminateGoal, "Evidence no longer supports objective")
            }
        };

        GoalAdaptationDecision {
            id: Uuid::new_v4(),

            goal_id,

            action: adaptation,

            reason: reason.to_string(),

            timestamp: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),
        }
    }
}

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct OutcomeAssessment {
    pub success_probability: f32,
    pub deviation_score: f32,
    pub confidence_delta: f32,
    pub lessons: Vec<String>,
}

pub struct OutcomeVerifier;

impl OutcomeVerifier {
    pub fn evaluate(expected: &str, observed: &str) -> OutcomeAssessment {
        let success = if expected == observed { 1.0 } else { 0.5 };

        OutcomeAssessment {
            success_probability: success,
            deviation_score: 1.0 - success,
            confidence_delta: success - 0.5,
            lessons: vec![format!("Expected: {} Observed: {}", expected, observed)],
        }
    }
}

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum StrategyDecision {
    Continue,
    Modify,
    Escalate,
    Abandon,
}

pub struct StrategyRevision;

impl StrategyRevision {
    pub fn decide(confidence: f32) -> StrategyDecision {
        if confidence > 0.75 {
            StrategyDecision::Continue
        } else if confidence > 0.45 {
            StrategyDecision::Modify
        } else if confidence > 0.20 {
            StrategyDecision::Escalate
        } else {
            StrategyDecision::Abandon
        }
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/cognition_loop.rs

use std::sync::Arc;

use crate::memory_cortex::MemoryCortex;
use crate::outcome_verifier::OutcomeVerifier;
use crate::strategy_revision::StrategyRevision;

pub struct CognitiveLoop {
    pub memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>,
}

impl CognitiveLoop {
    pub fn new(memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>) -> Self {
        Self { memory }
    }

    pub async fn evaluate_cycle(&self, expected: &str, observed: &str) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        let assessment = OutcomeVerifier::evaluate(expected, observed);

        let decision = StrategyRevision::decide(assessment.success_probability);

        self.memory
            .remember(crate::memory_types::CognitiveMemory {
                id: uuid::Uuid::new_v4(),

                kind: crate::memory_types::MemoryKind::Reflection,

                key: "cognitive_loop_result".into(),

                content: format!(
                    "Decision {:?}, confidence {}",
                    decision, assessment.success_probability
                ),

                source: "CognitiveLoop".into(),

                confidence: assessment.success_probability,

                importance: 0.8,

                created_at: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),

                tags: vec!["belief".into(), "feedback".into()],
            })
            .await?;

        Ok(())
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/belief_delta.rs 
=========================================
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct BeliefDelta {
    pub previous_confidence: f32,

    pub evidence_strength: f32,

    pub failure_penalty: f32,

    pub reflection_gain: f32,

    pub new_confidence: f32,

    pub explanation: String,
}

impl BeliefDelta {
    pub fn calculate(
        previous: f32,
        evidence: f32,
        failure: f32,
        reflection: f32,
        explanation: String,
    ) -> Self {
        let updated = (previous + evidence - failure + reflection).clamp(0.0, 1.0);

        Self {
            previous_confidence: previous,
            evidence_strength: evidence,
            failure_penalty: failure,
            reflection_gain: reflection,
            new_confidence: updated,
            explanation,
        }
    }
}

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct BeliefValidationEvent {
    pub prediction: String,

    pub observed: String,

    pub error_score: f32,

    pub confidence_delta: f32,

    pub lesson: String,
}

impl BeliefValidationEvent {
    pub fn new(prediction: String, observed: String) -> Self {
        let error_score = if prediction == observed { 0.0 } else { 1.0 };

        let confidence_delta = if error_score == 0.0 { 0.05 } else { -0.25 };

        let lesson = if error_score == 0.0 {
            "Prediction confirmed".to_string()
        } else {
            format!("Prediction failed. Expected '{}', observed '{}'", prediction, observed)
        };

        Self { prediction, observed, error_score, confidence_delta, lesson }
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/arbiter_agent.rs 
=========================================
use nexus_model_hub::neural_agent::MicroAgent;
use std::sync::Arc;

pub struct ArbiterAgent {
    pub agent: Arc<MicroAgent>,
}

impl ArbiterAgent {
    pub fn new(agent: Arc<MicroAgent>) -> Self {
        Self { agent }
    }

    pub fn validate_action(action_type: &str, command: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        let dangerous_patterns = [
            "rm -rf / ", // <-- Fixed: space added to allow local temp folder deletion
            "rm -rf ~",
            "mkfs",
            "> /dev/sda",
            "> /dev/nvme",
            "chmod 777 /",
            "/etc/shadow",
            "mv /etc/",
            "mv /usr/",
            ":(){ :|:& };:",
            "dd if=/dev/zero",
            "fork bomb",
            "sudo su",
            "sudo -i",
            "sudo bash",
            "sudo sh",
            "passwd root",
            "chown root",
            "$'\\x",
            "\\u0072\\u006d",
            "base64 -d |",
            "eval $(",
            "exec(",
            "curl | bash",
            "wget | bash",
            "curl | sh",
            "history -c",
            "unset HISTFILE",
        ];

        let normalized = command.to_lowercase();

        for pattern in dangerous_patterns.iter() {
            if normalized.contains(pattern) {
                return Err(format!(
                    "SANDBOX_VIOLATION: Threat pattern '{}' detected in {}.",
                    pattern, action_type
                ));
            }
        }

        if normalized.contains("../../../") || normalized.contains("/etc/passwd") {
            return Err("SANDBOX_VIOLATION: Directory traversal attempt blocked.".to_string());
        }

        if (normalized.contains("| bash")
            || normalized.contains("| sh")
            || normalized.contains("|bash")
            || normalized.contains("|sh"))
            && !normalized.starts_with("echo")
        {
            return Err("SANDBOX_VIOLATION: Pipe-to-shell pattern blocked.".to_string());
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn evaluate_entropy_delta(base_entropy: f32, new_entropy: f32) -> Result<(), String> {
        let delta = (new_entropy - base_entropy).abs();
        if delta > 0.5 {
            return Err(format!("ENTROPY_ANOMALY: Delta of {:.4} exceeds 0.5 threshold.", delta));
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn resolve_consensus(alpha_vector: &[f32], beta_vector: &[f32]) -> bool {
        if alpha_vector.len() != beta_vector.len() {
            return false;
        }
        let diff: f32 =
            alpha_vector.iter().zip(beta_vector.iter()).map(|(a, b)| (a - b).abs()).sum();
        diff < 0.5
    }

    pub fn bft_consensus(vectors: &[Vec<f32>], epsilon: f32) -> Option<Vec<f32>> {
        if vectors.is_empty() {
            return None;
        }
        let n = vectors.len();
        let threshold = (2 * n / 3) + 1;
        let dim = vectors[0].len();
        if vectors.iter().any(|v| v.len() != dim) {
            return None;
        }

        let (best_idx, neighbor_count) = (0..n)
            .map(|i| {
                let neighbors = (0..n)
                    .filter(|&j| {
                        vectors[i]
                            .iter()
                            .zip(vectors[j].iter())
                            .map(|(a, b)| (a - b).abs())
                            .sum::<f32>()
                            < epsilon
                    })
                    .count();
                (i, neighbors)
            })
            .max_by_key(|&(_, count)| count)?;

        if neighbor_count < threshold {
            return None;
        }

        let agreeing: Vec<&Vec<f32>> = (0..n)
            .filter(|&j| {
                vectors[best_idx]
                    .iter()
                    .zip(vectors[j].iter())
                    .map(|(a, b)| (a - b).abs())
                    .sum::<f32>()
                    < epsilon
            })
            .map(|j| &vectors[j])
            .collect();

        let centroid: Vec<f32> = (0..dim)
            .map(|k| agreeing.iter().map(|v| v[k]).sum::<f32>() / agreeing.len() as f32)
            .collect();

        Some(centroid)
    }
}

use chrono::Utc;

use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct MutationRecord {
    pub id: Uuid,

    pub path: String,

    pub target_function: String,

    pub method: String,

    pub verified: bool,

    pub confidence_delta: f32,

    pub timestamp: i64,
}

impl MutationRecord {
    pub fn new(path: String, function: String, method: String, verified: bool) -> Self {
        Self {
            id: Uuid::new_v4(),

            path,

            target_function: function,

            method,

            verified,

            confidence_delta: if verified { 0.05 } else { -0.20 },

            timestamp: Utc::now().timestamp(),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct MutationEvaluation {
    pub accepted: bool,
    pub structural_similarity: f32,
    pub confidence_delta: f32,
    pub reason: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Evaluation {
    Approved,
    Veto(String),
}

pub fn evaluate(proposed_code: &str) -> Evaluation {
    if proposed_code.contains("pub struct ") && proposed_code.contains(";") && !proposed_code.contains("{") {
        return Evaluation::Veto("Empty unit struct detected. Fields required.".to_string());
    }
    Evaluation::Approved
}

pub fn evaluate_evidence(proposed_code: &str) -> Evaluation {
    if proposed_code.contains("pub struct ") && proposed_code.contains(";") && !proposed_code.contains("{") {
        return Evaluation::Veto("Empty unit struct detected. Fields required.".to_string());
    }
    Evaluation::Approved
}

//! Mutation Evidence
//!
//! Captures objective evidence surrounding a code mutation.
//! Used by Mutation Critic for evaluation.

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct MutationEvidence {
    pub target_file: String,

    pub target_function: String,

    pub before_hash: String,

    pub after_hash: String,

    pub cargo_check_passed: bool,

    pub tests_passed: bool,

    pub ast_similarity: f32,

    pub changed_regions: usize,

    pub timestamp: i64,
}

impl MutationEvidence {
    pub fn verified(&self) -> bool {
        self.cargo_check_passed
            && self.tests_passed
            && self.ast_similarity >= 0.85
            && self.changed_regions <= 1
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/mutation/governor.rs 
=========================================
#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum ApprovalDecision {
    Approved(String),
    Rejected(String),
}

pub struct MutationGovernor;

impl MutationGovernor {
    pub fn evaluate(
        files_changed: usize,
        tests_passed: bool,
        backup_exists: bool,
    ) -> ApprovalDecision {
        if files_changed > 1 {
            return ApprovalDecision::Rejected("mutation scope exceeds policy".into());
        }

        if !tests_passed {
            return ApprovalDecision::Rejected("verification failed".into());
        }

        if !backup_exists {
            return ApprovalDecision::Rejected("rollback unavailable".into());
        }

        ApprovalDecision::Approved("mutation satisfies safety policy".into())
    }
}

        Self::new()
    }

        self.records.len()
