=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/epistemic_engine.rs 
=========================================
#![allow(warnings)]
use crate::model_config::{get_heavy_models, get_light_models};
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use reqwest::Client;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use serde_json::Value;
use tokio::time::Duration;

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct CriticResponse {
    pub critique: String,
    pub confidence_modifier: f32,
}

pub struct EpistemicEngine;

impl EpistemicEngine {
    async fn safe_api_call(
        client: &Client,
        api_key: &str,
        models: &[String],
        payload: &Value,
    ) -> Result<String> {
        let mut last_err = anyhow!("No models available in cascade.");
        for model_endpoint in models {
            let is_nvidia = model_endpoint.starts_with("nvidia/");
            let short_name = model_endpoint.split('/').last().unwrap_or(model_endpoint);
            let mut retries = 0;

            loop {
                // ⚡ DYNAMIC ROUTING: Convert payload based on provider
                let (url, req_payload, use_auth) = if is_nvidia {
                    let nv_key = std::env::var("NVIDIA_API_KEY").unwrap_or_default().trim().trim_matches('"').to_string();
                    let text = payload.pointer("/contents/0/parts/0/text").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).unwrap_or("");
                    let p = serde_json::json!({
                        "model": model_endpoint,
                        "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": text}],
                        "max_tokens": 4096,
                        "temperature": 0.1
                    });
                    ("https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1/chat/completions".to_string(), p, Some(nv_key))
                } else {
                    let key = if api_key.is_empty() { std::env::var("GEMINI_API_KEY").unwrap_or_default().trim().to_string() } else { api_key.to_string() };
                    let u = format!(
                        "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/{}:generateContent?key={}",
                        model_endpoint.replace("models/", ""),
                        key
                    );
                    (u, payload.clone(), None)
                };

                let mut req = client.post(&url).json(&req_payload);
                if let Some(auth_key) = use_auth {
                    req = req.header("Authorization", format!("Bearer {}", auth_key));
                }

                let res = req.send().await?;
                let body: Value = res.json().await.unwrap_or_default();

                // Error Handling
                if is_nvidia {
                    if body.get("error").is_some() {
                        last_err = anyhow!("NVIDIA API Error: {:?}", body);
                        break; // Move to next model in cascade
                    }
                } else {
                    if let Some(err) = body.pointer("/error/message").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) {
                        last_err = anyhow!("Gemini API Error: {}", err);
                        if err.contains("Internal error") || err.contains("quota") || err.contains("rate limit") || err.contains("503") {
                            retries += 1;
                            if retries > 6 { break; }
                            let backoff = std::cmp::min(2_u64.pow(retries as u32) * 2, 45);
                            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(backoff)).await;
                            continue;
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                }

                // Response Parsing
                let mut final_text = String::new();
                if is_nvidia {
                    if let Some(content) = body.pointer("/choices/0/message/content").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) {
                        final_text = content.to_string();
                    }
                } else {
                    if let Some(parts) = body.pointer("/candidates/0/content/parts").and_then(|v| v.as_array()) {
                        for part in parts {
                            if part.get("thought").and_then(|t| t.as_bool()).unwrap_or(false) { continue; }
                            if let Some(text) = part.get("text").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) {
                                final_text.push_str(text);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                if final_text.trim().is_empty() {
                    retries += 1;
                    if retries > 4 { break; }
                    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(2_u64.pow(retries as u32) * 2)).await;
                    continue;
                }
                return Ok(final_text.replace("```json", "").replace("```", "").trim().to_string());
            }
        }
        Err(anyhow!("Model Cascade Exhausted. Final error: {}", last_err))
    }

    pub async fn generate_hypotheses(client: &Client, api_key: &str, problem: &str, world_context: &str) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
        let prompt = format!("You are AthenaFusionX. Analyze this directive.\nDIRECTIVE: {}\nWORLD CONTEXT: {}\nCRITICAL RULE: If the directive explicitly tells you to execute a specific command, at least ONE of your strategies MUST be to simply execute that exact command.\nGenerate 3 distinct, actionable strategies. OUTPUT ONLY A JSON ARRAY OF STRINGS. No markdown.", problem, world_context);
        let payload = serde_json::json!({"contents": [{"role": "user", "parts": [{"text": prompt}]}], "generationConfig": { "temperature": 0.1, "maxOutputTokens": 2048 }});
        let text = Self::safe_api_call(client, api_key, &crate::model_config::get_heavy_models(), &payload).await?;
        serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e| anyhow!("Parse error: {}", e))
    }

    pub async fn propose_action(client: &Client, api_key: &str, mission: &str, strategy: &str, evidence_so_far: &str) -> Result<String> {
        let prompt = format!("You are the Action Strategist.\nORIGINAL DIRECTIVE (You must execute the exact steps requested here. Stay strictly within its scope -- do not investigate other files unless the directive or evidence explicitly points there): {}\nSTRATEGY: {}\nEVIDENCE GATHERED SO FAR:\n{}\nPropose the exact next step to fulfill the strategy. CRITICAL RULE: NEVER run `find /` or search the root directory! Only search the current working directory (`.`). 5. If your objective is to CREATE or WRITE a file, your immediate next action MUST be to use `write_file`.\nOUTPUT ONLY YOUR PROPOSED NEXT STEP IN PLAIN TEXT.", mission, strategy, evidence_so_far);
        let payload = serde_json::json!({"contents": [{"role": "user", "parts": [{"text": prompt}]}], "generationConfig": { "temperature": 0.2, "maxOutputTokens": 1024 }});
        Self::safe_api_call(client, api_key, &crate::model_config::get_light_models(), &payload).await
    }

    pub async fn criticize_proposal(client: &Client, api_key: &str, strategy: &str, proposed_action: &str, world_context: &str, evidence_history: &str) -> Result<CriticResponse> {
        let prompt = format!("You are the Adversarial Critic.\nSTRATEGY: {}\nPROPOSED ACTION: {}\nWORLD CONTEXT: {}\nEVIDENCE HISTORY (real observations already gathered this mission): {}\nEvaluate if this action is safe and logically sound. \nCRITICAL RULE 1: Always allow 'read_file' and 'find' operations even if the path is not visible in the WORLD CONTEXT. A failed read is safe exploration. Do not veto read operations for missing paths.\nCRITICAL RULE 2: If the proposed action is to HALT or declare the task complete, you MUST APPROVE IT ONLY IF the EVIDENCE HISTORY above already contains a concrete success marker ([WRITE_OK], [DIFF_PATCH_OK], [PATCH_FUNCTION_OK], or [CMD_OK] showing the specific outcome) that corroborates the exact claim being made. If the claimed completion is not backed by a matching entry in EVIDENCE HISTORY, it is an unverified assertion -- evaluate it under normal safety/soundness scrutiny instead of auto-approving, and treat an unsupported completion claim as a reason for a negative confidence_modifier.\nOUTPUT ONLY VALID JSON:\n{{\"critique\": \"<reason>\", \"confidence_modifier\": <float from -0.5 to 0.5>}}", strategy, proposed_action, world_context, evidence_history);
        let payload = serde_json::json!({"contents": [{"role": "user", "parts": [{"text": prompt}]}], "generationConfig": { "temperature": 0.1, "maxOutputTokens": 1024 }});
        let text = Self::safe_api_call(client, api_key, &crate::model_config::get_heavy_models(), &payload).await?;
        serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e| anyhow!("Parse error: {}", e))
    }

    pub async fn translate_to_action(client: &Client, api_key: &str, strategy: &str, proposed_action: &str) -> Result<String> {
        let prompt = format!(
            "You are the Action Translator.\nSTRATEGY: {}\nPROPOSED ACTION: {}\nCRITICAL RULE: Translate the PROPOSED ACTION into exactly ONE JSON Array using the explicit JSON object schema below:\n\
             VALID JSON ACTIONS (MUST MATCH RUST ENUM EXACTLY):\n\
             1. [{{ \"type\": \"execute_command\", \"command\": \"<bash_command>\", \"reasoning\": \"...\" }}]\n\
             2. [{{ \"type\": \"read_file\", \"path\": \"<filepath>\", \"reasoning\": \"...\" }}]\n\
             3. [{{ \"type\": \"write_file\", \"path\": \"<filepath>\", \"content\": \"<data>\", \"reasoning\": \"...\" }}]\n\
             4. [{{ \"type\": \"diff_patch\", \"path\": \"<filepath>\", \"search\": \"<search>\", \"replace\": \"<replace>\", \"reasoning\": \"...\" }}]\n\
             5. [{{ \"type\": \"patch_function\", \"path\": \"<filepath>\", \"function_name\": \"<name>\", \"new_body\": \"<full source>\", \"reasoning\": \"...\" }}]\n\
             6. [{{ \"type\": \"halt\", \"reasoning\": \"...\" }}]\n\
             NOTE: Do NOT use types like 'find_file', 'ls_tree', or 'grep_code'. If you need to search or list, use 'execute_command' (e.g. command: 'ls -F' or command: 'find .').\n\
             Select ONLY the relevant action object and enclose it in a JSON array. Do not output anything else.",
            strategy, proposed_action
        );
        let payload = serde_json::json!({"contents": [{"role": "user", "parts": [{"text": prompt}]}], "generationConfig": { "temperature": 0.1, "maxOutputTokens": 2048 }});
        Self::safe_api_call(client, api_key, &crate::model_config::get_light_models(), &payload).await
    }

    pub async fn evaluate_evidence(client: &Client, api_key: &str, strategy: &str, evidence: &str) -> Result<CriticResponse> {
        let truncated_evidence: String = evidence.chars().take(8000).collect();
        let prompt = format!("You are the Scientific Evaluator.\nSTRATEGY: {}\nEVIDENCE GATHERED:\n{}\nDoes this evidence represent meaningful partial progress toward the strategy? If it gathers useful context (like listing a directory or reading a file), give a positive modifier. DO NOT demand the entire strategy be completed in one step.\nOUTPUT ONLY VALID JSON:\n{{\"critique\": \"<reason>\", \"confidence_modifier\": <float from -0.5 to 0.5>}}", strategy, truncated_evidence);
        let payload = serde_json::json!({"contents": [{"role": "user", "parts": [{"text": prompt}]}], "generationConfig": { "temperature": 0.1, "maxOutputTokens": 1024 }});
        let text = Self::safe_api_call(client, api_key, &crate::model_config::get_heavy_models(), &payload).await?;
        serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e| anyhow!("Parse error: {}", e))
    }

    pub async fn verify_outcome(client: &Client, api_key: &str, strategy: &str, action: &str, evidence: &str) -> Result<crate::cognition_graph::VerificationReport> {
        let truncated_evidence: String = evidence.chars().take(8000).collect();
        let truncated_action: String = action.chars().take(2000).collect();
        let prompt = format!(
            "You are the Epistemic Verifier.\nSTRATEGY: {}\nACTION TAKEN: {}\nEVIDENCE:\n{}\n\
            Does this evidence mathematically prove the stated strategy's objective was achieved? \
            A successful action (like [WRITE_OK] or [CMD_OK]) is NOT proof of success on its own UNLESS the ACTION TAKEN itself contains the actual content that was written or patched (write_file/diff_patch/patch_function actions above include the real file content directly in ACTION TAKEN) — in that case, check whether that content satisfies the objective, and treat a confirmed match as proof. Do not reject a successful write or patch merely because the bare EVIDENCE line (e.g. [WRITE_OK]) doesn't repeat the content; the content to check is in ACTION TAKEN. \
            OUTPUT ONLY VALID JSON matching this schema:\n\
            {{\"goal_satisfied\": true, \"evidence_confidence\": 0.9, \"independent_check_passed\": true, \"rationale\": \"<reason>\"}}",
            strategy, truncated_action, truncated_evidence
        );
        let payload = serde_json::json!({"contents": [{"role": "user", "parts": [{"text": prompt}]}], "generationConfig": { "temperature": 0.1, "maxOutputTokens": 1024 }});
        let text = Self::safe_api_call(client, api_key, &crate::model_config::get_heavy_models(), &payload).await?;
        serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e| anyhow!("Parse error: {}", e))
    }

    pub async fn reflect_on_failure(client: &Client, api_key: &str, strategy: &str, evidence: &str) -> Result<String> {
        let truncated_evidence: String = evidence.chars().take(8000).collect();
        let prompt = format!(
            "You are the Meta-Cognitive Reflector.\nSTRATEGY: {}\nEVIDENCE (Failure/Anomaly):\n{}\n\
            Analyze why the strategy failed to achieve the objective based on the evidence. \
            Provide a concise 2-sentence reflection on the error and suggest a pivot. OUTPUT ONLY PLAIN TEXT.",
            strategy, truncated_evidence
        );
        let payload = serde_json::json!({"contents": [{"role": "user", "parts": [{"text": prompt}]}], "generationConfig": { "temperature": 0.3, "maxOutputTokens": 1024 }});
        Self::safe_api_call(client, api_key, &crate::model_config::get_light_models(), &payload).await
    }
}

use std::collections::HashMap;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct NodeId(pub String);

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Node {
    pub id: NodeId,
    pub label: String,
    pub file_path: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Edge {
    pub source: NodeId,
    pub target: NodeId,
    pub edge_type: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, Default)]
pub struct GraphMetadata {
    pub version: String,
    pub total_nodes: usize,
    pub total_edges: usize,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct DependencyGraph {
    pub nodes: HashMap<NodeId, Node>,
    pub edges: Vec<Edge>,
    pub metadata: GraphMetadata,
}

impl DependencyGraph {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            nodes: HashMap::new(),
            edges: Vec::new(),
            metadata: GraphMetadata::default(),
        }
    }
}

impl Default for DependencyGraph {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/repository_intelligence/symbol_index.rs 
=========================================
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, HashMap};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
pub struct SymbolId(pub String);

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Span {
    pub start: usize,
    pub end: usize,
    pub line: usize,
}

impl Ord for Span {
    fn cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> std::cmp::Ordering {
        self.line
            .cmp(&other.line)
            .then_with(|| self.start.cmp(&other.start))
            .then_with(|| self.end.cmp(&other.end))
    }
}

impl PartialOrd for Span {
    fn partial_cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> Option<std::cmp::Ordering> {
        Some(self.cmp(other))
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct SymbolInfo {
    pub id: SymbolId,
    pub name: String,
    pub kind: String,
    pub span: Span,
    pub file_path: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct SymbolReference {
    pub symbol_id: SymbolId,
    pub span: Span,
    pub file_path: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Default, Clone)]
pub struct SymbolIndex {
    pub symbols: HashMap<String, SymbolInfo>,
    pub references: Vec<SymbolReference>,
    pub span_map: BTreeMap<Span, SymbolId>,
}

impl SymbolIndex {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    pub fn insert_symbol(&mut self, info: SymbolInfo) {
        self.span_map.insert(info.span.clone(), info.id.clone());
        self.symbols.insert(info.id.0.clone(), info);
    }

    pub fn add_reference(&mut self, reference: SymbolReference) {
        self.references.push(reference);
    }

    pub fn get_symbol(&self, id: &SymbolId) -> Option<&SymbolInfo> {
        self.symbols.get(&id.0)
    }

    pub fn find_by_span(&self, span: &Span) -> Option<&SymbolId> {
        self.span_map.get(span)
    }
}

use crate::genome::{
    CognitiveGenes, CriticGenes, CriticStyle, Genome, GenomeMetadata, PlanningGenes,
    PlanningStyle, ToolBias,
};
use crate::repository::GenomeRepository;
use chrono::Utc;
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct EvolutionDirector {
    pub repository: GenomeRepository,
}

impl EvolutionDirector {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    /// Bootstraps Generation 0 if no active genome exists.
    pub fn boot_genesis_genome(&mut self) -> &Genome {
        if self.repository.active.is_none() {
            let genesis_id = Uuid::new_v4();
            
            let genesis_genome = Genome {
                id: genesis_id,
                metadata: GenomeMetadata {
                    generation: 0,
                    parent_ids: vec![],
                    mutation_reason: "SYSTEM_INIT: Baseline Genesis Genome".to_string(),
                    created_at: Utc::now(),
                },
                genes: CognitiveGenes {
                    planning: PlanningGenes {
                        style: PlanningStyle::GraphOfThoughts,
                        decomposition_depth: 3,
                        branch_factor: 2,
                    },
                    critic: CriticGenes {
                        style: CriticStyle::Adversarial,
                        veto_threshold: 0.85,
                        evidence_weight: 1.0,
                    },
                    risk_threshold: 0.20,
                    tool_bias: ToolBias::Conservative,
                },
            };

            self.repository.save_genome(genesis_genome);
            self.repository.active = Some(genesis_id);
        }

        self.repository.get_active_genome().unwrap()
    }

    pub fn analyze_evidence(&self, _evidence: &str) -> Option<Genome> {
        // Future implementation: Evaluate fitness and trigger mutation
        None
    }
}

FILE: crates/evolution_engine/src/fitness_evaluator.rs 
=========================================
use crate::genome::SystemGenome;
use anyhow::Result;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct FitnessScore {
    pub score: f32,
    pub execution_time_ms: u64,
    pub token_efficiency: f32,
}

pub struct FitnessEvaluator;

impl FitnessEvaluator {
    pub async fn evaluate(
        genome: &SystemGenome,
        benchmark_latency_ms: u64,
        tokens_used: u32,
    ) -> Result<FitnessScore> {
        let time_penalty = (benchmark_latency_ms as f32) * 0.01;
        let token_penalty = (tokens_used as f32) * 0.005;
        
        let base_score = 100.0 + (genome.weights.heuristic_reward * 50.0);
        let final_score = (base_score - time_penalty - token_penalty).max(0.0);

        Ok(FitnessScore {
            score: final_score,
            execution_time_ms: benchmark_latency_ms,
            token_efficiency: if tokens_used > 0 { 1000.0 / (tokens_used as f32) } else { 0.0 },
        })
    }
}

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct CognitiveWeights {
    pub meta_reasoning_threshold: f32, // When to trigger GoT
    pub critic_penalty_weight: f32,    // How heavily to penalize vetoes
    pub heuristic_reward: f32,         // Reward for physical exploration
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct SystemGenome {
    pub generation: u32,
    pub hash: String,
    pub weights: CognitiveWeights,
    pub active_wasm_modules: Vec<String>,
    pub primary_heavy_model: String,
    pub primary_light_model: String,
    pub fitness_score: f32,
}

impl SystemGenome {
    pub fn baseline() -> Self {
        Self {
            generation: 0,
            hash: "genesis_0000".to_string(),
            weights: CognitiveWeights {
                meta_reasoning_threshold: 0.60,
                critic_penalty_weight: -0.30,
                heuristic_reward: 0.30,
            },
            active_wasm_modules: vec![],
            primary_heavy_model: "gemma-4-31b-it".to_string(),
            primary_light_model: "gemini-3.1-flash-lite".to_string(),
            fitness_score: 100.0,
        }
    }
}

FILE: crates/evolution_engine/src/mutator.rs 
=========================================
use crate::genome::SystemGenome;
use rand::Rng;

pub struct Mutator;

impl Mutator {
    pub fn mutate(parent: &SystemGenome) -> SystemGenome {
        let mut rng = rand::thread_rng();
        let mut child = parent.clone();
        
        child.generation += 1;
        
        // Drift cognitive weights by +/- 10%
        let mut drift = || rng.gen_range(0.90..1.10);
        
        child.weights.meta_reasoning_threshold *= drift();
        child.weights.critic_penalty_weight *= drift();
        child.weights.heuristic_reward *= drift();

        // Enforce boundary constraints
        child.weights.meta_reasoning_threshold = child.weights.meta_reasoning_threshold.clamp(0.1, 0.9);
        child.weights.heuristic_reward = child.weights.heuristic_reward.clamp(0.1, 0.8);

        // Generate deterministic child hash
        child.hash = format!("gen_{}_{}", child.generation, hex::encode(rng.gen::<[u8; 4]>()));
        
        child
    }
}

use crate::genome::{FitnessVector, Genome};
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct GenomeEvaluation {
    pub benchmark_id: Uuid,
    pub fitness: FitnessVector,
    pub timestamp: DateTime<Utc>,
}

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct GenomeRepository {
    pub active: Option<Uuid>,
    pub lineage: HashMap<Uuid, Genome>,
    pub evaluations: HashMap<Uuid, Vec<GenomeEvaluation>>,
}

impl GenomeRepository {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    pub fn save_genome(&mut self, genome: Genome) {
        self.lineage.insert(genome.id, genome);
    }

    pub fn get_active_genome(&self) -> Option<&Genome> {
        self.active.and_then(|id| self.lineage.get(&id))
    }
}

pub fn add(left: u64, right: u64) -> u64 {
    left + right
}

    #[test]
    fn it_works() {
        let result = add(2, 2);
        assert_eq!(result, 4);
    }
}

FILE: bins/cli/src/main.rs

use std::io::{self, Write};
use futures_util::StreamExt;
use reqwest::Client;
use tokio_tungstenite::connect_async;

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    dotenvy::dotenv().ok();
    println!("\x1b[36m==================================================\x1b[0m");
    println!("\x1b[1;36m>> NEXUS SINGULARITY v4.0 — THIN CLIENT ONLINE\x1b[0m");
    println!("\x1b[36m==================================================\x1b[0m");

    let token = std::fs::read_to_string(".nexus_ui_token")
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| "".to_string())
        .trim()
        .to_string();

    let ws_url = "ws://127.0.0.1:3000/ws/telemetry";
    
    let ws_stream = match connect_async(ws_url).await {
        Ok((stream, _)) => {
            Some(stream)
        }
        Err(_) => {
            println!("\x1b[31m>> [ERROR] Could not connect to Daemon. Is it running?\x1b[0m");
            None
        }
    };

    if let Some(mut stream) = ws_stream {
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            let mut last_type = String::new();
            while let Some(Ok(msg)) = stream.next().await {
                if let tokio_tungstenite::tungstenite::Message::Text(text) = msg {
                    if let Ok(parsed) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&text) {
                        let msg_type = parsed["type"].as_str().unwrap_or("LOG");
                        let payload = parsed["payload"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                        match msg_type {
                            "NEURAL_THOUGHT" | "VITALS" | "HEARTBEAT" => {}
                            "NEURAL_STREAM" => {
                                if last_type != "NEURAL_STREAM" {
                                    print!("\x1b[0m\n\x1b[1;36m[CORTEX EXECUTING]\x1b[0m\n\x1b[36m");
                                }
                                print!("{}", payload);
                                let _ = io::stdout().flush();
                                last_type = "NEURAL_STREAM".to_string();
                            }
                            "TASK_STATUS" => {
                                println!("\x1b[0m\n\x1b[1;33m[SYS]\x1b[0m {}", payload);
                                last_type = "TASK_STATUS".to_string();
                            }
                            "LOG_EVENT" => {
                                println!("\x1b[0m\n\x1b[1;90m[EVENT]\x1b[0m {}", payload);
                                last_type = "LOG_EVENT".to_string();
                            }
                            _ => {}
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    }

    println!("\x1b[32m>> Neural Uplink Established. Substrate Ready.\x1b[0m");
    let client = Client::new();
    let stdin = io::stdin();

    loop {
        print!("\n\x1b[1;32mnexus>\x1b[0m (Type prompt, then 'EOF' on a new line to submit)\n> ");
        io::stdout().flush()?;

        let mut input = String::new();
        loop {
            let mut line = String::new();
            let bytes_read = stdin.read_line(&mut line)?;
            if bytes_read == 0 || line.trim() == "EOF" { break; }
            input.push_str(&line);
        }

        let directive = input.trim();
        if directive.is_empty() { continue; }
        if directive == "/exit" || directive == "/quit" { break; }

        let res = client.post("http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/task")
            .header("Authorization", format!("Bearer {}", token))
            .json(&serde_json::json!({ "directive": directive }))
            .send()
            .await;

        match res {
            Ok(response) => {
                if !response.status().is_success() {
                    println!("\x1b[31m>> [ERROR] Daemon rejected task: {}\x1b[0m", response.status());
                } else {
                    println!("\x1b[32m>> [OK] Task dispatched to Background Executive Cortex.\x1b[0m");
                }
            }
            Err(e) => println!("\x1b[31m>> [ERROR] Failed to contact Daemon: {}\x1b[0m", e),
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

FILE: bins/ide_backend/src/auth.rs

use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use jsonwebtoken::{decode, encode, Algorithm, DecodingKey, EncodingKey, Header, Validation};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

use zeroize::Zeroizing;
const MIN_KEY_BYTES: usize = 32;

/// Call once at startup. Returns a heap-allocated key that zeroes on drop.
/// Aborts with a descriptive error if the env var is missing or too short.
pub fn load_key_at_startup() -> anyhow::Result<Zeroizing<String>> {
    let key = std::env::var("NEXUS_AUTH_KEY")
        .map_err(|_| anyhow::anyhow!("NEXUS_AUTH_KEY env var is not set — refusing to start"))?;
    if key.len() < MIN_KEY_BYTES {
        return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
            "NEXUS_AUTH_KEY is too short ({} bytes). Minimum is {} bytes.",
            key.len(), MIN_KEY_BYTES
        ));
    }
    Ok(Zeroizing::new(key))
}
const DEFAULT_TTL_SECS: u64 = 86_400;

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Claims {
    pub sub: String,
    pub exp: usize,
    pub iat: usize,
}

fn load_key() -> Result<String> {
    // Key is validated and loaded once at startup via load_key_at_startup().
    // This shim reads it again for backward compatibility; prefer passing the
    // Zeroizing<String> from AppState directly in new code.
    let key = std::env::var("NEXUS_AUTH_KEY")
        .map_err(|_| anyhow!("NEXUS_AUTH_KEY env var is not set"))?;
    if key.len() < MIN_KEY_BYTES {
        return Err(anyhow!(
            "NEXUS_AUTH_KEY is too short ({} bytes). Minimum is {} bytes.",
            key.len(), MIN_KEY_BYTES
        ));
    }
    Ok(key)
}

fn token_ttl() -> u64 {
    match std::env::var("NEXUS_TOKEN_TTL_SECS") {
        Ok(v) => match v.parse::<u64>() {
            Ok(secs) => secs,
            Err(_) => {
                tracing::warn!("[AUTH] NEXUS_TOKEN_TTL_SECS='{}' is not a valid u64. Using default {}s. Fix your .env file.", v, DEFAULT_TTL_SECS);
                DEFAULT_TTL_SECS
            }
        },
        Err(_) => DEFAULT_TTL_SECS,
    }
}

pub fn issue_sovereign_token() -> Result<String> {
    let key = load_key()?;
    let now = SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)?.as_secs();
    let claims = Claims {
        sub: "architect".to_owned(),
        iat: now as usize,
        exp: (now + token_ttl()) as usize,
    };
    encode(&Header::default(), &claims, &EncodingKey::from_secret(key.as_bytes()))
        .map_err(|e| anyhow!("JWT_GEN_FAIL: {}", e))
}

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum TokenVerdict {
    Valid,
    Expired,
    InvalidSignature,
    MissingKey,
}

pub fn verify_token_detailed(token: &str) -> Result<TokenVerdict> {
    let key = load_key()?;
    let mut validation = Validation::new(Algorithm::HS256);
    validation.leeway = 0;
    match decode::<Claims>(token, &DecodingKey::from_secret(key.as_bytes()), &validation) {
        Ok(_) => Ok(TokenVerdict::Valid),
        Err(e) => Ok(match e.kind() {
            jsonwebtoken::errors::ErrorKind::ExpiredSignature => TokenVerdict::Expired,
            _ => TokenVerdict::InvalidSignature,
        }),
    }
}

pub fn verify_token(token: &str) -> Result<bool> {
    match verify_token_detailed(token)? {
        TokenVerdict::Valid => Ok(true),
        TokenVerdict::Expired => {
            tracing::warn!("[AUTH] Token rejected: expired");
            Ok(false)
        }
        TokenVerdict::InvalidSignature => {
            tracing::warn!("[AUTH] Token rejected: invalid signature");
            Ok(false)
        }
        TokenVerdict::MissingKey => Ok(false),
    }
}

FILE: bins/ide_backend/src/broker.rs 
=========================================
//! Broker — deduplicates rapid fire directives within a 200 ms window
//! so the same button-mash doesn't flood the agent pipeline.
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::Mutex;
use std::time::Instant;
use once_cell::sync::Lazy;

static LAST_FIRE: Lazy<Mutex<HashMap<String, Instant>>> =
    Lazy::new(|| Mutex::new(HashMap::new()));

const DEBOUNCE_MS: u128 = 200;

pub fn init() {
    tracing::info!(">> [BROKER] Directive deduplicator online");
}

/// Returns true when the directive should be forwarded (not a duplicate).
pub fn should_forward(key: &str) -> bool {
    let mut map = LAST_FIRE.lock().unwrap();
    let now = Instant::now();
    if let Some(last) = map.get(key) {
        if last.elapsed().as_millis() < DEBOUNCE_MS {
            return false;
        }
    }
    map.insert(key.to_string(), now);
    true
}

use crate::server::AppState;
use axum::{extract::State, Json};
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::sync::Arc;

pub async fn list_nodes(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>) -> Json<Value> {
    let nodes = state.cluster.get_all_nodes();
    Json(json!({ "nodes": nodes }))
}

pub async fn cluster_status(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>) -> Json<Value> {
    let best = state.cluster.best_node();
    Json(json!({
        "best_node": best,
        "has_capacity": best.is_some(),
    }))
}

/// WASM cell registry — returns cells that have been synthesised and stored.
/// Cells are written to .nexus_wasm_registry.json by wasm_forge when synthesised.
pub async fn list_wasm_cells(_state: axum::extract::State<std::sync::Arc<crate::server::AppState>>) -> axum::Json<serde_json::Value> {
    let path = ".nexus_wasm_registry.json";
    let cells: Vec<serde_json::Value> = if std::path::Path::new(path).exists() {
        tokio::fs::read_to_string(path).await
            .ok()
            .and_then(|s| serde_json::from_str(&s).ok())
            .unwrap_or_default()
    } else { vec![] };
    axum::Json(serde_json::json!({ "cells": cells }))
}

//! Heartbeat — sends a periodic JSON ping over the broadcast channel so
//! the WebSocket watchdog in socket.ts never fires a stale-connection close.
use tokio::sync::broadcast;
use tokio::time::{interval, Duration};
use serde_json::json;

pub fn init() {
    tracing::info!(">> [HEARTBEAT] Pulse initialised");
}

pub fn spawn(tx: broadcast::Sender<String>) {
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let mut ticker = interval(Duration::from_secs(5));
        loop {
            ticker.tick().await;
            let msg = json!({ "type": "HEARTBEAT", "ts": chrono_now() }).to_string();
            let _ = tx.send(msg);
        }
    });
}

fn chrono_now() -> u64 {
    std::time::SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
        .unwrap_or_default()
        .as_secs()
}

//! Logger — initialises tracing subscriber with env-filter and JSON output.
use tracing_subscriber::{fmt, EnvFilter};

pub fn init() {
    let filter = EnvFilter::try_from_default_env()
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| EnvFilter::new("nexus_ide_backend=info,nexus_runtime_core=info,warn"));

    fmt()
        .with_env_filter(filter)
        .with_target(true)
        .with_thread_ids(false)
        .compact()
        .init();

    tracing::info!(">> [LOGGER] Tracing substrate online");
}

use uuid::Uuid;

#[allow(dead_code)]
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub enum TaskState {
    Ready,
    Blocked,
    Suspended,
    Completed,
    Failed,
}

#[allow(dead_code)]
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ResourceBudget {
    pub cpu_budget_ms: u32,
    pub token_budget: usize,
    pub max_memory_bytes: usize,
    pub io_weight: u8,
}

#[allow(dead_code)]
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct RuntimeCounters {
    pub cpu_time_used_ms: u32,
    pub tokens_consumed: usize,
    pub memory_used_bytes: usize,
    pub io_operations: u64,
}

#[allow(dead_code)]
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct CognitiveTask {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub lineage_id: Uuid,
    pub parent_lineage_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub execution_class: ExecutionClass,
    pub state: TaskState,
    pub static_priority: u8,
    pub dynamic_priority: u32,
    pub budget: ResourceBudget,
    pub counters: RuntimeCounters,
    pub created_at: u64,
}

#[allow(dead_code)]
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub enum ExecutionClass {
    RealTimeTelemetry,
    SovereignInference,
    BackgroundConsolidation,
}

pub struct DeterministicScheduler {
    pub queue: Vec<CognitiveTask>,
}

impl DeterministicScheduler {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { queue: Vec::new() }
    }

    pub fn submit(&mut self, task: CognitiveTask) {
        self.queue.push(task);
    }

    #[allow(dead_code)]
    pub fn dispatch(&mut self) -> Option<CognitiveTask> {
        if self.queue.is_empty() {
            None
        } else {
            Some(self.queue.remove(0))
        }
    }
}

use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};

    _orchestrator: Arc<nexus_runtime_core::orchestrator::Orchestrator>,
    _client: reqwest::Client,
    _api_key: String,

FILE: bins/ide_backend/src/system_monitor.rs 
=========================================
use sysinfo::{CpuRefreshKind, MemoryRefreshKind, RefreshKind, System};

pub struct Vitals {
    pub cpu_load: f32,
    pub ram_gb: f32,
    pub total_ram_gb: f32,
    pub temperature: f32,
}

pub struct SystemMonitor {
    sys: System,
}

impl SystemMonitor {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        let mut sys = System::new_with_specifics(
            RefreshKind::new()
                .with_cpu(CpuRefreshKind::everything())
                .with_memory(MemoryRefreshKind::everything()),
        );
        sys.refresh_all();
        Self { sys }
    }

    fn read_thermal_zone(&self) -> f32 {
        std::fs::read_to_string("/sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone1/temp")
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| "45000".to_string())
            .trim()
            .parse::<f32>()
            .unwrap_or(45000.0) / 1000.0
    }

    pub fn get_vitals(&mut self) -> Vitals {
        self.sys.refresh_cpu_all();
        self.sys.refresh_memory();

        let cpu_load = self.sys.global_cpu_usage() / 100.0;
        let ram_gb   = (self.sys.used_memory() as f32) / 1_073_741_824.0;
        let total_ram_gb = (self.sys.total_memory() as f32) / 1_073_741_824.0;
        let temperature = self.read_thermal_zone();

        Vitals {
            cpu_load,
            ram_gb,
            total_ram_gb,
            temperature,
        }
    }
}

FILE: bins/ide_backend/src/telemetry_bridge.rs 
=========================================
//! TelemetryBridge — reads real system vitals every 3 s and broadcasts
//! them as a JSON heartbeat that the frontend's store.svelte.ts consumes.
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};
use tokio::time::{interval, Duration};
use opentelemetry::{global, propagation::Injector};
use reqwest::header::{HeaderMap, HeaderName, HeaderValue};

/// Carrier adapter for Reqwest / Axum HeaderMaps to support OpenTelemetry propagation.
pub struct HeaderMapInjector<'a> {
    pub headers: &'a mut HeaderMap,
}

impl<'a> Injector for HeaderMapInjector<'a> {
    fn set(&mut self, key: &str, value: String) {
        if let (Ok(name), Ok(val)) = (
            HeaderName::from_bytes(key.as_bytes()),
            HeaderValue::from_str(&value),
        ) {
            self.headers.insert(name, val);
        }
    }
}

/// Injects the current W3C traceparent context into outbound HTTP request headers.
pub fn inject_traceparent(headers: &mut HeaderMap) {
    global::get_text_map_propagator(|propagator| {
        propagator.inject_context(
            &opentelemetry::Context::current(),
            &mut HeaderMapInjector { headers },
        );
    });
}

pub fn init() {
    tracing::info!(">> [TELEMETRY] Bridge initialised");
}

pub fn spawn(
    tx: broadcast::Sender<String>,
    monitor: Arc<Mutex<crate::system_monitor::SystemMonitor>>,
    resonance: Arc<std::sync::atomic::AtomicU32>,
) {
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let mut ticker = interval(Duration::from_secs(3));
        loop {
            ticker.tick().await;
            let vitals = {
                let mut m = monitor.lock().await;
                m.get_vitals()
            };
            let res = resonance.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed) as f32 / 1000.0;
            let active_vmos = nexus_substrate::kernel::get_active_vmos();

            let msg = serde_json::json!({
                "type":        "VITALS",
                "cpu_load":    vitals.cpu_load,
                "ram_gb":      vitals.ram_gb,
                "total_ram_gb": vitals.total_ram_gb,
                "temperature": vitals.temperature,
                "resonance":   res,
                "active_vmos": active_vmos,
            });
            let _ = tx.send(msg.to_string());
        }
    });
}

FILE: bins/ide_backend/src/ws_bridge.rs 
=========================================
use crate::server::AppState;
use axum::{
    extract::ws::{Message, WebSocket, WebSocketUpgrade},
    extract::State,
    response::Response,
};
use std::sync::Arc;

pub async fn ws_telemetry_handler(
    ws: WebSocketUpgrade,
    State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>,
) -> Response {
    ws.on_upgrade(move |socket| handle_socket(socket, state))
}

async fn handle_socket(mut socket: WebSocket, state: Arc<AppState>) {
    let mut rx = state.tx.subscribe();
    while let Ok(msg) = rx.recv().await {
        if socket.send(Message::Text(msg)).await.is_err() {
            break;
        }
    }
}

FILE: bins/ide_backend/src/security.rs 
=========================================
use crate::auth;
use axum::{
    body::Body,
    http::{header, Request, StatusCode},
    middleware::Next,
    response::Response,
};

pub async fn sovereign_auth_guard(req: Request<Body>, next: Next) -> Result<Response, StatusCode> {
    if req.uri().path() == "/api/auth/login" {
        return Ok(next.run(req).await);
    }

    let auth_header =
        req.headers().get(header::AUTHORIZATION).and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok()).unwrap_or("");
    let token = auth_header.trim_start_matches("Bearer ").trim();

    if !token.is_empty() {
        match auth::verify_token_detailed(token) {
            Ok(auth::TokenVerdict::Valid) => return Ok(next.run(req).await),
            Ok(verdict) => tracing::warn!(
                "[SECURITY] 401 Unauthorized: Path={} | Verdict={:?}",
                req.uri().path(),
                verdict
            ),
            Err(e) => tracing::error!(
                "[SECURITY] Auth key misconfiguration at {}: {}",
                req.uri().path(),
                e
            ),
        }
    } else {
        tracing::warn!(
            "[SECURITY] 401 Unauthorized: Path={} | No token provided",
            req.uri().path()
        );
    }
    Err(StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED)
}

pub fn write_startup_token() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    use std::io::Write;
    use std::os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt;

    let token = auth::issue_sovereign_token()?;
    // ⚡ FIX: Strict 0600 permissions
    std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
        .write(true)
        .create(true)
        .truncate(true)
        .mode(0o600)
        .open(".nexus_ui_token")?
        .write_all(token.as_bytes())?;

    println!("\x1b[32m>> [SECURITY] Startup token written to .nexus_ui_token (0600)\x1b[0m");
    Ok(())
}

use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};
use tokio::time::{interval, Duration};
use nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace;

pub fn init() {
    tracing::info!(">> [DREAM_CYCLE] Subconscious synthesis engine initialised");
}

pub fn spawn(
    palace: Arc<Mutex<MemoryPalace>>,
    tx: broadcast::Sender<String>,
    client: reqwest::Client,
    api_key: String,
    orchestrator: Arc<nexus_runtime_core::orchestrator::Orchestrator>,
) {
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let mut ticker = interval(Duration::from_secs(30 * 60));
        ticker.tick().await; 
        loop {
            ticker.tick().await;
            tracing::info!("[DREAM_CYCLE] Beginning synthesis pass…");
            let _ = tx.send(
                serde_json::json!({"type":"LOG_EVENT","payload":"[DREAM] Synthesis cycle awakening…"})
                    .to_string()
            );

            let strands = {
                let mem = palace.lock().await;
                mem.fetch_random_strands(8).await.unwrap_or_default()
            };

            if strands.is_empty() {
                continue;
            }

            let context = strands.iter()
                .map(|s| format!("DIRECTIVE: {}\nRESULT: {}", s.directive, s.result))
                .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                .join("\n---\n");

            let prompt = format!(
                "You are NEXUS Archivist. Analyse these memory strands and write one concise \
                 architectural insight (3-5 sentences, plain text, no JSON):\n\n{}",
                context
            );

            let ctx = nexus_model_hub::neural_agent::ContextPacket {
                system_prompt: Some("Output plain text only. One paragraph.".into()),
                model_override: Some("gemma-4-26b-a4b-it".into()),
                ..Default::default()
            };

            match orchestrator.run(prompt, ctx, &client, &api_key, Some(tx.clone())).await {
                Ok(insight) => {
                    let mem = palace.lock().await;
                    if let Err(e) = mem.store_insight(&insight).await {
                        tracing::warn!("[DREAM_CYCLE] Failed to store insight: {}", e);
                    } else {
                        tracing::info!("[DREAM_CYCLE] Insight stored");
                    }
                }
                Err(e) => tracing::warn!("[DREAM_CYCLE] Synthesis failed: {}", e),
            }
        }
    });
}

use nexus_runtime_core::memory_cortex::MemoryCortex;
use axum::{
    extract::{State, Query},
    http::{header, StatusCode, Uri},
    response::IntoResponse,
    routing::{get, post},
    Router, Json, middleware,
};
use rust_embed::RustEmbed;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::net::TcpListener;
use nexus_runtime_core::shared_state::VmoState;
use serde_json::json;
use serde::Deserialize;
use secrecy::{ExposeSecret, SecretString};

use crate::{ws_bridge, vitals_handler, cluster_api, security};

#[derive(RustEmbed)]
#[folder = "ui/dist/"] 
struct Assets;

pub struct AppState {
    pub client:             reqwest::Client,
    pub monitor:            Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<crate::system_monitor::SystemMonitor>>,
    pub agent:              Arc<nexus_model_hub::neural_agent::MicroAgent>,
    pub orchestrator:       Arc<nexus_runtime_core::orchestrator::Orchestrator>,
    pub memory:             Arc<MemoryCortex>,
    pub api_key:            SecretString,
    pub tx:                 tokio::sync::broadcast::Sender<String>,
    pub arbiter:            Arc<nexus_runtime_core::arbiter_agent::ArbiterAgent>,
    pub workspace_root:     PathBuf,
    pub vmo_state:          Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<VmoState>>,
    pub cluster:            Arc<nexus_runtime_core::cluster::ClusterMesh>,
    pub dist:               Arc<nexus_runtime_core::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler>,
}

#[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
pub struct Intent {
    pub directive: Option<String>,
}

pub async fn unified_gateway(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>, Json(intent): Json<Intent>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let directive_str = intent.directive.unwrap_or_default();
    let s = state.clone();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let d = nexus_runtime_core::cli_utils::inject_workspace_dna(&directive_str).await;
        nexus_runtime_core::task_manager::send_task(
            s.orchestrator.clone(),
            nexus_runtime_core::task_manager::TaskContext {
                client: s.client.clone(),
                api_key: s.api_key.expose_secret().to_string(),
                memory: s.memory.clone(),
                vmo_state: s.vmo_state.clone(),
                tx: s.tx.clone(),
                dist: s.dist.clone(),
            },
            &d,
        ).await;
    });
    (StatusCode::OK, Json(json!({ "status": "QUEUED" }))).into_response()
}

async fn metrics_handler() -> impl IntoResponse {
    let text = nexus_runtime_core::metrics::get_metrics_text();
    ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "text/plain")], text)
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
pub struct FsQuery { path: Option<String> }

fn safe_path(workspace_root: &Path, user_path: &str) -> Result<PathBuf, (StatusCode, Json<serde_json::Value>)> {
    if user_path.is_empty() { return Err((StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, Json(json!({ "error": "PATH_EMPTY" })))); }
    let path = std::path::Path::new(user_path);
    if path.is_absolute() || path.components().any(|c| matches!(c, std::path::Component::ParentDir)) {
        return Err((StatusCode::FORBIDDEN, Json(json!({ "error": "PATH_TRAVERSAL_DENIED" }))));
    }
    Ok(workspace_root.join(path))
}

pub async fn fs_read_handler(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>, Query(q): Query<FsQuery>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let user_path = q.path.unwrap_or_default();
    match safe_path(&state.workspace_root, &user_path) {
        Err(e) => e.into_response(),
        Ok(safe) => match tokio::fs::read_to_string(&safe).await {
            Ok(content) => Json(json!({ "content": content })).into_response(),
            Err(e) => (StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, Json(json!({ "error": e.to_string() }))).into_response(),
        },
    }
}

pub async fn fs_write_handler(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>, Json(payload): Json<serde_json::Value>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let user_path = payload["path"].as_str().unwrap_or_default();
    let content   = payload["content"].as_str().unwrap_or_default();
    match safe_path(&state.workspace_root, user_path) {
        Err(e) => e.into_response(),
        Ok(safe) => {
            if let Some(parent) = safe.parent() {
                let _ = tokio::fs::create_dir_all(parent).await;
            }
            match tokio::fs::write(&safe, content).await {
                Ok(_) => Json(json!({ "status": "success" })).into_response(),
                Err(e) => (StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, Json(json!({ "error": e.to_string() }))).into_response(),
            }
        }
    }
}

pub async fn fs_list_handler(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>, Query(q): Query<FsQuery>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let user_path = q.path.unwrap_or_else(|| ".".into());
    match safe_path(&state.workspace_root, &user_path) {
        Err(e) => e.into_response(),
        Ok(safe) => {
            let mut entries = Vec::new();
            if let Ok(mut read_dir) = tokio::fs::read_dir(&safe).await {
                while let Ok(Some(entry)) = read_dir.next_entry().await {
                    let meta = entry.metadata().await.ok();
                    entries.push(json!({
                        "name": entry.file_name().to_string_lossy(),
                        "path": entry.path().strip_prefix(&state.workspace_root).unwrap_or(&entry.path()).to_string_lossy(),
                        "is_dir": meta.map(|m| m.is_dir()).unwrap_or(false)
                    }));
                }
            }
            Json(entries).into_response()
        }
    }
}

pub async fn start_server(state: Arc<AppState>) {
    // ⚡ SUTURE: Secure API Routes behind Auth Guard
    let api_routes = Router::new()
        .route("/task", post(unified_gateway))
        .route("/metrics", get(metrics_handler))
        .route("/fs/read", get(fs_read_handler))
        .route("/fs/write", post(fs_write_handler))
        .route("/fs/list", get(fs_list_handler))
        .route("/vitals", get(vitals_handler::handle_vitals))
        .route("/cluster/nodes", get(cluster_api::list_nodes))
        .route_layer(middleware::from_fn(security::sovereign_auth_guard));

    let app = Router::new()
        .nest("/api", api_routes)
        .route("/ws/telemetry", get(ws_bridge::ws_telemetry_handler))
        .fallback(static_handler)
        .with_state(state);
        
    let listener = TcpListener::bind("0.0.0.0:3000").await.unwrap();
    axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap();
}

async fn static_handler(uri: Uri) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let path = uri.path().trim_start_matches('/').to_string();
    let final_path = if path.is_empty() { "index.html".to_string() } else { path };
    
    let asset = Assets::get(&final_path)
        .or_else(|| Assets::get(&format!("./{}", final_path)));

    match asset {
        Some(content) => {
            let mime = mime_guess::from_path(&final_path).first_or_octet_stream();
            ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, mime.as_ref())], content.data).into_response()
        }
        None => {
            if final_path.contains('.') && !final_path.ends_with(".html") {
                return StatusCode::NOT_FOUND.into_response();
            }
            match Assets::get("index.html").or_else(|| Assets::get("./index.html")) {
                Some(content) => ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "text/html")], content.data).into_response(),
                None => StatusCode::NOT_FOUND.into_response(),
            }
        }
    }
}

FILE: bins/ide_backend/src/thermal_governor.rs 
=========================================
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicU32, Ordering};
use tokio::time::{interval, Duration};

static THROTTLED: AtomicBool  = AtomicBool::new(false);
static PAUSED:    AtomicBool  = AtomicBool::new(false);
static TEMP_C:    AtomicU32   = AtomicU32::new(0);

pub fn init() {
    tracing::info!(">> [THERMAL] Governor initialised");
}

pub fn is_throttled() -> bool { THROTTLED.load(Ordering::Relaxed) }
pub fn is_paused()    -> bool { PAUSED.load(Ordering::Relaxed) }
pub fn current_temp() -> f32  { TEMP_C.load(Ordering::Relaxed) as f32 }

pub fn spawn() {
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let mut ticker = interval(Duration::from_secs(10));
        let mut components = sysinfo::Components::new();
        loop {
            ticker.tick().await;
            components.refresh();

            let max_temp = components
                .iter()
                .map(|c| c.temperature())
                .filter(|t| t.is_finite())
                .fold(0f32, f32::max);

            TEMP_C.store(max_temp as u32, Ordering::Relaxed);
            THROTTLED.store(max_temp > 70.0, Ordering::Relaxed);
            PAUSED.store(max_temp > 80.0, Ordering::Relaxed);

            if max_temp > 75.0 {
                tracing::warn!("[THERMAL] Temperature {}°C — throttling agent pipeline", max_temp);
            }
        }
    });
}

#![allow(dead_code)]
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};
use std::path::PathBuf;

use nexus_runtime_core::memory_cortex::MemoryCortex;
use nexus_runtime_core::orchestrator::Orchestrator;

use nexus_runtime_core::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler;
use nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace;
use secrecy::SecretString;

mod auth;
mod broker;
mod cluster_api;

mod gateway_trait;
mod heartbeat;
mod logger;
mod scheduler;

mod server_terminal;
mod system_monitor;
mod telemetry_bridge;
mod thermal_governor;
mod vitals_handler;
mod ws_bridge;

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    dotenvy::dotenv().ok();
    logger::init();
    
    tracing::info!("==================================================");
    tracing::info!(">> NEXUS SINGULARITY v4.0 — DAEMON ONLINE");
    tracing::info!("==================================================");

    security::write_startup_token()?;
    let api_key_str = std::env::var("GEMINI_API_KEY").unwrap_or_default().trim().to_string();
    let api_key = SecretString::from(api_key_str.clone());
    let req_client = reqwest::Client::new();

    let (tx, _) = broadcast::channel::<String>(256);
    
    let palace = Arc::new(Mutex::new(MemoryPalace::new("nexus_daemon.db").await?));
    
    // 👇 The crucial fix: We clone the palace Arc so it can be shared!
    let memory = Arc::new(MemoryCortex::new(palace.clone()));
    let vmo_state = Arc::new(Mutex::new(VmoState::default()));
    let cluster = Arc::new(ClusterMesh::new());
    let dist = Arc::new(DistributedScheduler::new(cluster.clone()));
    let orchestrator = Arc::new(Orchestrator::new());
    
    let monitor = Arc::new(Mutex::new(system_monitor::SystemMonitor::new()));
    let resonance = Arc::new(std::sync::atomic::AtomicU32::new(1000));
    
    let agent = Arc::new(nexus_model_hub::neural_agent::MicroAgent::init(tx.clone())?);
    let arbiter = Arc::new(nexus_runtime_core::arbiter_agent::ArbiterAgent::new(agent.clone()));

    // 🌟 Boot all Subconscious Background Threads 🌟
    dream_cycle::spawn(palace.clone(), tx.clone(), req_client.clone(), api_key_str.clone(), orchestrator.clone());
    self_healing_engine::spawn(orchestrator.clone(), req_client.clone(), api_key_str.clone(), memory.clone(), vmo_state.clone(), tx.clone(), dist.clone());
    telemetry_bridge::spawn(tx.clone(), monitor.clone(), resonance.clone());
    thermal_governor::spawn();
    heartbeat::spawn(tx.clone());
    
    let state = Arc::new(server::AppState {
        client: req_client,
        monitor,
        agent,
        orchestrator,
        memory,
        api_key,
        tx,
        arbiter,
        workspace_root: PathBuf::from("."),
        vmo_state,
        cluster,
        dist,
    });

    tracing::info!(">> Core Subsystems online. Binding Axum API Gateway to 0.0.0.0:3000...");
    server::start_server(state).await;

    Ok(())
}

FILE: bins/node/src/main.rs

use axum::{
    body::Body,
    extract::{Json, State},
    http::{Request, StatusCode},
    response::IntoResponse,
    routing::{get, post},
    Router,
};
use base64::Engine;
use nexus_runtime_core::agent_protocol::AgentAction;
use nexus_runtime_core::wasm_forge::WasmForge;
use nexus_runtime_core::wasm_runtime::WasmRuntime;
use nexus_substrate::kernel::safe_kernel::SafeKernel;
use nexus_substrate::kernel::safe_vmo::SafeVmo;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::Mutex;

// --- State Management ---
struct NodeState {
    vmo_registry: Mutex<HashMap<u32, SafeVmo>>,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct VmoCreateReq {
    size: usize,
}
#[derive(Serialize)]
struct VmoCreateRes {
    handle: u32,
}
#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct VmoWriteReq {
    handle: u32,
    offset: usize,
    data: Vec<u8>,
}
#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct VmoReadReq {
    handle: u32,
    offset: usize,
    size: usize,
}
#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct ForgeReq {
    code: String,
}
#[derive(Serialize)]
struct ForgeRes {
    ok: bool,
    b64: Option<String>,
    error: Option<String>,
}
#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct ExecuteReq {
    wasm_b64: String,
    func_name: String,
    input: f32,
}
#[derive(Serialize)]
struct ExecuteRes {
    ok: bool,
    result: Option<f32>,
    error: Option<String>,
}
#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct VmoEntropyReq {
    handle: u32,
}
#[derive(Serialize)]
struct VmoEntropyRes {
    entropy: f32,
}

// --- Middleware: Sovereign Guard ---
async fn auth_guard(req: Request<Body>, next: axum::middleware::Next) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let auth_header = req.headers().get("Authorization").and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok());
    if auth_header == Some("SOVEREIGN") {
        next.run(req).await
    } else {
        (StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED, "Access Denied: SOVEREIGN token required").into_response()
    }
}

// --- Handlers ---
async fn create_vmo(
    State(state): State<Arc<NodeState>>,
    Json(req): Json<VmoCreateReq>,
) -> impl IntoResponse {
    match SafeKernel::create_vmo(req.size) {
        Ok(handle) => {
            if let Ok(vmo) = SafeVmo::new(req.size) {
                state.vmo_registry.lock().await.insert(handle, vmo);
                (StatusCode::OK, Json(VmoCreateRes { handle })).into_response()
            } else {
                (StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, "VMO Wrapper failure").into_response()
            }
        }
        Err(e) => (StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, format!("Kernel Error: {:?}", e)).into_response(),
    }
}

async fn write_vmo(
    State(state): State<Arc<NodeState>>,
    Json(req): Json<VmoWriteReq>,
) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let registry = state.vmo_registry.lock().await;
    if let Some(vmo) = registry.get(&req.handle) {
        match vmo.write(req.offset, &req.data) {
            Ok(_) => StatusCode::OK.into_response(),
            Err(e) => (StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, format!("Write Error: {:?}", e)).into_response(),
        }
    } else {
        (StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, "VMO handle not found").into_response()
    }
}

async fn read_vmo(
    State(state): State<Arc<NodeState>>,
    Json(req): Json<VmoReadReq>,
) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let registry = state.vmo_registry.lock().await;
    if let Some(vmo) = registry.get(&req.handle) {
        let mut buf = vec![0u8; req.size];
        match vmo.read_into(req.offset, &mut buf) {
            Ok(_) => Json(buf).into_response(),
            Err(e) => (StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, format!("Read Error: {:?}", e)).into_response(),
        }
    } else {
        (StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, "VMO handle not found").into_response()
    }
}

async fn calculate_entropy(
    State(state): State<Arc<NodeState>>,
    Json(req): Json<VmoEntropyReq>,
) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let registry = state.vmo_registry.lock().await;
    if registry.contains_key(&req.handle) {
        let entropy = nexus_substrate::kernel::calculate_entropy(req.handle);
        (StatusCode::OK, Json(VmoEntropyRes { entropy })).into_response()
    } else {
        (StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, "VMO handle not found").into_response()
    }
}

async fn synthesize_wasm(Json(req): Json<ForgeReq>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    match WasmForge::synthesize_cell(&req.code).await {
        Ok(b64) => Json(ForgeRes { ok: true, b64: Some(b64), error: None }).into_response(),
        Err(e) => {
            Json(ForgeRes { ok: false, b64: None, error: Some(e.to_string()) }).into_response()
        }
    }
}

async fn execute_wasm(Json(req): Json<ExecuteReq>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let bytes = match base64::engine::general_purpose::STANDARD.decode(&req.wasm_b64) {
        Ok(b) => b,
        Err(e) => {
            return Json(ExecuteRes { ok: false, result: None, error: Some(e.to_string()) })
                .into_response()
        }
    };
    match WasmRuntime::run_bytes(&bytes, &req.func_name, req.input) {
        Ok(res) => Json(ExecuteRes { ok: true, result: Some(res), error: None }).into_response(),
        Err(e) => {
            Json(ExecuteRes { ok: false, result: None, error: Some(e.to_string()) }).into_response()
        }
    }
}

// ⚡ PHASE 3: The magic endpoint that receives LLM actions from the Duet and executes them locally on the Node's filesystem.
async fn execute_physical(Json(action): Json<AgentAction>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    // ⚡ FIX: ToolExecutor was deleted during the Executor Unification pass.
    // As `nexus_node` acts as a remote satellite without a true Memory Palace
    // or VmoState right now, we spin up a localized RoboticExecutor instance.
    let (tx, _) = tokio::sync::broadcast::channel(1);
    let mut executor = nexus_runtime_core::executor::RoboticExecutor::new(
        String::new(), // Edge nodes don't need the API key
        tx,
    );

    // Mock up the substrate dependencies for the edge node
    let memory = Arc::new(nexus_runtime_core::memory_cortex::MemoryCortex::new(Arc::new(
        Mutex::new(nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace::new(":memory:").await.unwrap()),
    )));
    let vmo_state = Arc::new(Mutex::new(nexus_runtime_core::shared_state::VmoState::default()));
    let dist = Arc::new(nexus_runtime_core::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler::new(
        Arc::new(nexus_runtime_core::cluster::ClusterMesh::new()),
    ));

    match executor.execute_single_action(action, memory, vmo_state, dist).await {
        Ok(obs) => (StatusCode::OK, obs).into_response(),
        Err(e) => {
            (StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, format!("ACTION_FAILED: {}", e)).into_response()
        }
    }
}

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() {
    tracing_subscriber::fmt::init();
    let state = Arc::new(NodeState { vmo_registry: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()) });

    let app = Router::new()
        .route("/api/vmo/create", post(create_vmo))
        .route("/api/vmo/write", post(write_vmo))
        .route("/api/vmo/read", post(read_vmo))
        .route("/api/vmo/entropy", post(calculate_entropy))
        .route("/api/action/physical", post(execute_physical))
        .route("/api/forge/synthesize", post(synthesize_wasm))
        .route("/api/forge/execute", post(execute_wasm))
        .route("/", get(|| async { "Sovereign Muscle Node Online" }))
        .layer(axum::middleware::from_fn(auth_guard))
        .with_state(state);

    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind("0.0.0.0:8080").await.unwrap();
    tracing::info!(">> [NODE] Sovereign Muscle active on port 8080");
    axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap();
}
